[GCF_finer_quotient_01] Actual congruence subgroup, KDq, DtQ, UOQ, rational map and positive-part relative determinant
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Definitions/AutomorphicMembership/IsRationalUnitaryDeterminantMapRationalUnitary.lean#L16-L125
Range SHA256: b552420aa696849b5beb72b5093e1c72c0712686f4168fb1b9be4f827c71ac99
def AtQ (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 H.E)) : Prop := v ∈ SQ ∨ ∃ w ∈ SQ, IsConjPl H w v

def congQ : Set (Ideles H.E) :=
  {a | a ∈ unitIdl H.E ∧ (∀ k v, OverP H (H.g k) v → Valued.v ((a : AdeleRing (𝓞 H.E) H.E).2 v - 1) < 1) ∧
    ∀ v, AtQ H SQ v → Valued.v ((a : AdeleRing (𝓞 H.E) H.E).2 v - 1) < 1}

def KDq : Subgroup (Ideles H.E) :=
  Subgroup.closure (congQ H SQ ∪ {a | ∃ v k, a = locF H.F v k})

abbrev DtQ : Type := Ideles H.E ⧸ KDq H SQ

open Classical in

def tildeQ (χ : HChar H.E) : DtQ H SQ →* ℂˣ :=
  if h : KDq H SQ ≤ (χ.1.comp (finPartI H.E)).ker then QuotientGroup.lift (KDq H SQ) (χ.1.comp (finPartI H.E)) h else 1

def muTilQ (mu : Fin R.m → HChar H.E) (j : ℕ) : DtQ H SQ →* ℂˣ :=
  if h : 1 ≤ j ∧ j ≤ R.m then tildeQ H SQ (mu ⟨j - 1, by omega⟩) else 1

def UOQ : Subgroup (DtQ H SQ) := (unitIdl H.E).map (QuotientGroup.mk' (KDq H SQ))

def IsUnitaryPointsMapFinerDeterminants (f : (letI := H.cmStar; unitary H.E) →* DtQ H SQ) : Prop :=
  letI := H.cmStar
  ∀ (k : H.Eˣ) (z : unitary H.E), (z : H.E) * H.conj (k : H.E) = k → QuotientGroup.mk (diagUnits H.E k) = f z

open Classical in

def e1Q : (letI := H.cmStar; unitary H.E) →* DtQ H SQ := if h : ∃ f, IsUnitaryPointsMapFinerDeterminants H SQ f then h.choose else 1

def relDetQ (N : ℕ) (M M' : Submodule (𝓞 H.E) (Fin N → H.E)) : DtQ H SQ ⧸ UOQ H SQ :=
  QuotientGroup.mk (QuotientGroup.mk (∏ᶠ w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 H.E),
    localIdele H.E w (atFin w (unif w)) ^ (ordSub w (detSub M') - ordSub w (detSub M)).toNat))

theorem congQ_subset_congG : congQ H SQ ⊆ congG H := fun _ h => ⟨h.1, h.2.1⟩

theorem mem_KDq_of_congQ {a : Ideles H.E} (h : a ∈ congQ H SQ) : a ∈ KDq H SQ :=
  Subgroup.subset_closure (Or.inl h)

theorem locF_mem_KDq (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 H.F)) (k : (H.F)ˣ) : locF H.F v k ∈ KDq H SQ :=
  Subgroup.subset_closure (Or.inr ⟨v, k, rfl⟩)

theorem KDq_le_iff {B : Subgroup (Ideles H.E)} :
    KDq H SQ ≤ B ↔ (∀ a ∈ congQ H SQ, a ∈ B) ∧ ∀ v k, locF H.F v k ∈ B := by
  refine ⟨fun h => ⟨fun a ha => h (mem_KDq_of_congQ H SQ ha), fun v k => h (locF_mem_KDq H SQ v k)⟩, fun h => ?_⟩
  refine (Subgroup.closure_le _).2 ?_
  rintro a (ha | ⟨v, k, rfl⟩)
  exacts [h.1 a ha, h.2 v k]

theorem KDq_le_KD : KDq H SQ ≤ KD H :=
  Subgroup.closure_mono (Set.union_subset_union_left _ (congQ_subset_congG H SQ))

theorem KDq_anti {SQ SQ' : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 H.E))} (h : SQ ⊆ SQ') : KDq H SQ' ≤ KDq H SQ :=
  Subgroup.closure_mono (Set.union_subset_union_left _ fun _ ha => ⟨ha.1, ha.2.1, fun v hv =>
    ha.2.2 v (hv.imp (fun h' => h h') fun ⟨w, hw, hc⟩ => ⟨w, h hw, hc⟩)⟩)

theorem congQ_empty : congQ H ∅ = congG H :=
  Set.Subset.antisymm (congQ_subset_congG H ∅) fun _ h => ⟨h.1, h.2, fun v hv => by simp [AtQ] at hv⟩

theorem KDq_empty : KDq H ∅ = KD H := by
  unfold KDq KD
  rw [congQ_empty]

theorem KD_le_sup : KD H ≤ KDq H SQ ⊔ unitIdl H.E := by
  refine (Subgroup.closure_le _).2 ?_
  rintro a (ha | ⟨v, k, rfl⟩)
  · exact Subgroup.mem_sup_right ha.1
  · exact Subgroup.mem_sup_left (locF_mem_KDq H SQ v k)

theorem KDq_sup_unitIdl : KDq H SQ ⊔ unitIdl H.E = KD H ⊔ unitIdl H.E :=
  le_antisymm (sup_le_sup_right (KDq_le_KD H SQ) _) (sup_le (KD_le_sup H SQ) le_sup_right)

theorem finite_level : {v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 H.E) | (∃ k, OverP H (H.g k) v) ∨ AtQ H SQ v}.Finite := by
  have h1 : ∀ k, {v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 H.E) | OverP H (H.g k) v}.Finite := fun k => by
    have h0 : Ideal.span {((H.g k : ℕ) : 𝓞 H.E)} ≠ 0 := by
      rw [Ne, Ideal.zero_eq_bot, Ideal.span_singleton_eq_bot]
      exact Nat.cast_ne_zero.2 (H.g_prime k).ne_zero
    refine (Ideal.finite_factors h0).subset fun v hv => ?_
    exact Ideal.dvd_span_singleton.2 hv
  have h2 : ∀ w, {v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 H.E) | IsConjPl H w v}.Finite := fun w =>
    Set.Subsingleton.finite fun v hv v' hv' => (existsUnique_conjPl H.conj.toRingEquiv w).unique
      ((conjPl_iff_mem _ _ _).2 hv) ((conjPl_iff_mem _ _ _).2 hv')
  refine (((Set.finite_iUnion h1).union SQ.finite_toSet).union (SQ.finite_toSet.biUnion fun w _ => h2 w)).subset ?_
  rintro v (⟨k, hk⟩ | hv | ⟨w, hw, hc⟩)
  · exact Or.inl (Or.inl (Set.mem_iUnion.2 ⟨k, hk⟩))
  · exact Or.inl (Or.inr hv)
  · exact Or.inr (Set.mem_biUnion hw hc)

theorem mem_congQ_of_eq_one {a : Ideles H.E} (hu : a ∈ unitIdl H.E)
    (h : ∀ v, (∃ k, OverP H (H.g k) v) ∨ AtQ H SQ v → (a : AdeleRing (𝓞 H.E) H.E).2 v = 1) : a ∈ congQ H SQ :=
  ⟨hu, fun k v hv => by rw [h v (Or.inl ⟨k, hv⟩), sub_self, map_zero]; exact zero_lt_one,
    fun v hv => by rw [h v (Or.inr hv), sub_self, map_zero]; exact zero_lt_one⟩

theorem infIdele_mem_KDq (w : InfinitePlace H.E) (y : (w.Completion)ˣ) : infIdele H.E w y ∈ KDq H SQ :=
  mem_KDq_of_congQ H SQ (mem_congQ_of_eq_one H SQ (fun v => show Valued.v (1 : v.adicCompletion H.E) = 1 from map_one _)
    fun _ _ => rfl)

theorem isOpen_KDq : IsOpen (KDq H SQ : Set (Ideles H.E)) := by
  have ho := (GlobalTheory.Mem.UnitOpen.unit_open_and_congr_open H.E).2 (finite_level H SQ).toFinset 1
  have hlt : WithZero.exp (-((1 : ℕ) : ℤ)) < (1 : WithZero (Multiplicative ℤ)) := by
    rw [← WithZero.exp_zero, WithZero.exp_lt_exp]; norm_num
  refine Subgroup.isOpen_of_mem_nhds _ (g := 1) (Filter.mem_of_superset (ho.mem_nhds ⟨one_mem _, fun v _ => ?_⟩) ?_)
  · show Valued.v ((1 : v.adicCompletion H.E) - 1) ≤ _
    rw [sub_self, map_zero]
    exact zero_le
  · intro a ha
    refine mem_KDq_of_congQ H SQ ⟨ha.1, fun k v hv => (ha.2 v ?_).trans_lt hlt, fun v hv => (ha.2 v ?_).trans_lt hlt⟩
    · exact (Set.Finite.mem_toFinset _).2 (Or.inl ⟨k, hv⟩)
    · exact (Set.Finite.mem_toFinset _).2 (Or.inr hv)

theorem mk_mem_UOQ {a : Ideles H.E} : (QuotientGroup.mk a : DtQ H SQ) ∈ UOQ H SQ ↔ a ∈ KDq H SQ ⊔ unitIdl H.E := by
  rw [← QuotientGroup.comap_map_mk']
  rfl

theorem comap_UOT : (UOT H).comap (toDtT H SQ) = UOQ H SQ := by
  ext x
  obtain ⟨a, rfl⟩ := QuotientGroup.mk_surjective x
  rw [mk_mem_UOQ, KDq_sup_unitIdl, ← QuotientGroup.comap_map_mk']
  rfl

def toDtTMod : DtQ H SQ ⧸ UOQ H SQ →* DtT H ⧸ UOT H :=
  QuotientGroup.map (UOQ H SQ) (UOT H) (toDtT H SQ) (comap_UOT H SQ).ge

@[simp] theorem toDtTMod_mk (x : DtQ H SQ) :
    toDtTMod H SQ (QuotientGroup.mk x) = QuotientGroup.mk (toDtT H SQ x) := rfl

theorem toDtTMod_bijective : Function.Bijective (toDtTMod H SQ) := by
  refine ⟨(injective_iff_map_eq_one _).2 fun x hx => ?_, fun y => ?_⟩
  · obtain ⟨a, rfl⟩ := QuotientGroup.mk_surjective x
    rw [toDtTMod_mk, QuotientGroup.eq_one_iff, ← Subgroup.mem_comap, comap_UOT] at hx
    exact (QuotientGroup.eq_one_iff _).2 hx
  · obtain ⟨b, rfl⟩ := QuotientGroup.mk_surjective y
    obtain ⟨a, rfl⟩ := toDtT_surjective H SQ b
    exact ⟨QuotientGroup.mk a, rfl⟩

def modEquiv : DtQ H SQ ⧸ UOQ H SQ ≃* DtT H ⧸ UOT H := MulEquiv.ofBijective _ (toDtTMod_bijective H SQ)

theorem toDtTMod_relDetQ (N : ℕ) (M M' : Submodule (𝓞 H.E) (Fin N → H.E)) :
    toDtTMod H SQ (relDetQ H SQ N M M') = relDetT H N M M' := rfl

theorem relDetQ_self (N : ℕ) (M : Submodule (𝓞 H.E) (Fin N → H.E)) : relDetQ H SQ N M M = 1 :=


[GCF_finer_quotient_03] Rational Hilbert--90 and the original e1Q characterization
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Definitions/AutomorphicMembership/IsRationalUnitaryDeterminantMapRationalUnitary.lean#L285-L340
Range SHA256: 0ead8c4732cb1d93ac4802a54bcf6d1ae30205112807a8d08e58bef9e00d6deb
theorem exists_eq_div_conj : letI := H.cmStar
    ∀ z : unitary H.E, ∃ k : (H.E)ˣ, (z : H.E) * H.conj (k : H.E) = k := by
  letI := H.cmStar
  intro z
  have hcm : IsCMField H.E := H.isCMField_hostE
  have hc : ∀ x, IsCMField.complexConj H.E x = H.conj x := fun x => bridge_star_holds R H x
  have hz : (z : H.E) * H.conj (z : H.E) = 1 := by
    rw [← bridge_star_holds R H]
    exact z.2.2
  by_cases h : 1 + (z : H.E) = 0
  · obtain ⟨x, hx⟩ : ∃ x : H.E, H.conj x ≠ x := by
      by_contra hall
      refine IsCMField.complexConj_ne_one H.E (AlgEquiv.ext fun x => ?_)
      rw [hc]
      exact not_not.1 fun h' => hall ⟨x, h'⟩
    have hcc : H.conj (H.conj x) = x := by
      rw [← hc, ← hc]
      exact IsCMField.complexConj_apply_apply H.E x
    refine ⟨Units.mk0 (H.conj x - x) (sub_ne_zero.2 hx), ?_⟩
    rw [Units.val_mk0, map_sub, hcc]
    linear_combination (x - H.conj x) * h
  · refine ⟨Units.mk0 _ h, ?_⟩
    rw [Units.val_mk0, map_add, map_one]
    linear_combination hz

theorem mk_diag_eq {z : H.E} {k k' : (H.E)ˣ} (h : z * H.conj (k : H.E) = k) (h' : z * H.conj (k' : H.E) = k') :
    (QuotientGroup.mk (diagUnits H.E k) : DtQ H SQ) = QuotientGroup.mk (diagUnits H.E k') := by
  have hcm : IsCMField H.E := H.isCMField_hostE
  have hc : ∀ x, IsCMField.complexConj H.E x = H.conj x := fun x => bridge_star_holds R H x
  have hk : H.conj (k : H.E) ≠ 0 := (map_ne_zero _).2 k.ne_zero
  have hk0 : (k : H.E) ≠ 0 := k.ne_zero
  have hfix : IsCMField.complexConj H.E ((k⁻¹ * k' : (H.E)ˣ) : H.E) = ((k⁻¹ * k' : (H.E)ˣ) : H.E) := by
    rw [hc, Units.val_mul, Units.val_inv_eq_inv_val, map_mul, map_inv₀]
    field_simp
    linear_combination H.conj (k : H.E) * h' - H.conj (k' : H.E) * h
  have hF : ((k⁻¹ * k' : (H.E)ˣ) : H.E) ∈ H.F := (IsCMField.complexConj_eq_self_iff H.E _).1 hfix
  have h0 : (⟨_, hF⟩ : H.F) ≠ 0 := fun e => (k⁻¹ * k').ne_zero (congrArg Subtype.val e)
  have e : upF (L := H.E) H.F (Units.mk0 _ h0) = k⁻¹ * k' := Units.ext rfl
  rw [QuotientGroup.eq, ← map_inv, ← map_mul, ← e]
  exact diag_upF_mem_KDq H SQ _

theorem exists_isUnitaryPointsMapFinerDeterminants : ∃ f, IsUnitaryPointsMapFinerDeterminants H SQ f := by
  letI := H.cmStar
  choose κ hκ using exists_eq_div_conj H
  refine ⟨MonoidHom.mk' (fun z => QuotientGroup.mk (diagUnits H.E (κ z))) fun z z' => ?_,
    fun k z hk => mk_diag_eq H SQ hk (hκ z)⟩
  rw [← QuotientGroup.mk_mul, ← map_mul]
  refine mk_diag_eq H SQ (hκ (z * z')) ?_
  rw [Units.val_mul, map_mul, Submonoid.coe_mul]
  linear_combination ((z' : H.E) * H.conj (κ z' : H.E)) * hκ z + (κ z : H.E) * hκ z'

theorem isUnitaryPointsMap_unitaryRationalPointsFiner : IsUnitaryPointsMapFinerDeterminants H SQ (e1Q H SQ) := by
  unfold e1Q
  rw [dif_pos (exists_isUnitaryPointsMapFinerDeterminants H SQ)]
  exact (exists_isUnitaryPointsMapFinerDeterminants H SQ).choose_spec



[GCF_finer_quotient_04] Literal LevOk and local-congruence / conjugate-prime quotient criteria
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Definitions/AutomorphicMembership/ReferenceCharactersFactorTame.lean#L430-L551
Range SHA256: 33634feff800993e431e855c05f155cb134c3db9daf6227dc43f4c8f1a700376
theorem isConjPl_iff (w w' : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 H.E)) : IsConjPl H w w' ↔ w' = conjPrime₂ w := by
  rw [← conjPl_star_iff, conjPl_iff_mem]
  refine forall_congr' fun x => forall_congr' fun y => ?_
  rw [← bridge_star_holds R H]
  rfl

theorem under_eq_iff (w w' : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 H.E)) :
    w'.under (𝓞 H.F) = w.under (𝓞 H.F) ↔ w' = w ∨ w' = conjPrime₂ w := by
  obtain ⟨c, hc, hcm, -⟩ := HostCM.host_cm R H
  rw [under_eq_iff_conjPl c hcm.rank hcm.ne, ← conjPl_star_iff]
  have e : c.toRingEquiv = (starRingAut : H.E ≃+* H.E) := RingEquiv.ext fun x => (hc x).trans (bridge_star_holds R H x).symm
  rw [e]

structure LevOk : Prop where
  cover : ∀ k v, OverP H (H.g k) v → v ∈ SG ∨ conjPrime₂ v ∈ SG
  split : ∀ P ∈ uSG SG SQ, conjPrime₂ P ∉ uSG SG SQ

theorem mem_KDq_of_local (a : Ideles H.E) (h : ∀ v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 H.F), ∃ k : (H.F)ˣ, ∀ w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 H.E),
    w.under (𝓞 H.F) = v → Valued.v (compAt w a / algebraMap H.E _ (algebraMap H.F H.E k)) = 1 ∧
      ((∃ i, OverP H (H.g i) w) ∨ AtQ H SQ w → Valued.v (compAt w a / algebraMap H.E _ (algebraMap H.F H.E k) - 1) < 1)) :
    a ∈ KDq H SQ := by
  classical
  choose κ hκ using h
  have hfin := (finite_level H SQ).union (IdlFact.finite_valAt_ne_one a)
  set S := hfin.toFinset.image fun w => w.under (𝓞 H.F) with hS
  set b := a / ∏ v ∈ S, locF (L := H.E) H.F v (κ v) with hb
  have hc : ∀ w, compAt w b = if w.under (𝓞 H.F) ∈ S then
      compAt w a / algebraMap H.E _ (algebraMap H.F H.E (κ (w.under (𝓞 H.F)))) else compAt w a := fun w => by
    rw [hb, map_div, map_prod, Finset.prod_congr rfl fun v _ => compAt_locF H.F w v (κ v), Finset.prod_ite_eq]
    split_ifs
    · rfl
    · exact div_one _
  have hmem : ∀ w, ((∃ i, OverP H (H.g i) w) ∨ AtQ H SQ w) ∨ IdlFact.valAt w a ≠ 1 → w.under (𝓞 H.F) ∈ S := fun w hw =>
    Finset.mem_image_of_mem _ (hfin.mem_toFinset.2 hw)
  have hl : ∀ w, (∃ i, OverP H (H.g i) w) ∨ AtQ H SQ w → Valued.v (compAt w b - 1) < 1 := fun w hw => by
    rw [hc, if_pos (hmem w (Or.inl hw))]
    exact (hκ _ w rfl).2 hw
  have hbK : b ∈ KDq H SQ := by
    refine mem_KDq_of_congQ H SQ ⟨fun w => ?_, fun i w hw => hl w (Or.inl ⟨i, hw⟩), fun w hw => hl w (Or.inr hw)⟩
    show Valued.v (compAt w b) = 1
    rw [hc]
    split_ifs with h1
    · exact (hκ _ w rfl).1
    · by_contra hne
      exact h1 (hmem w (Or.inr hne))
  rw [← div_mul_cancel a (∏ v ∈ S, locF (L := H.E) H.F v (κ v))]
  exact mul_mem hbK (prod_mem fun v _ => locF_mem_KDq H SQ v _)

variable {H SG SQ}

theorem LevOk.level (h : LevOk H SG SQ) {w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 H.E)} (hw : (∃ i, OverP H (H.g i) w) ∨ AtQ H SQ w) :
    w ∈ uSG SG SQ ∨ conjPrime₂ w ∈ uSG SG SQ := by
  classical
  simp only [Finset.mem_union]
  rcases hw with ⟨i, hi⟩ | hw | ⟨v, hv, hc⟩
  · exact (h.cover i w hi).imp Or.inl Or.inl
  · exact Or.inl (Or.inr hw)
  · rw [(isConjPl_iff H v w).1 hc, CharValCompare.conjP_conjP]
    exact Or.inr (Or.inr hv)

theorem LevOk.mono (h : LevOk H SG SQ) {Q : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 H.E))} (hQ : Q ⊆ SQ) : LevOk H SG Q := by
  classical
  have hs : uSG SG Q ⊆ uSG SG SQ := by
    intro P
    simp only [Finset.mem_union]
    exact Or.imp_right fun h => hQ h
  exact ⟨h.cover, fun P hP h' => h.split P (hs hP) (hs h')⟩

theorem mem_KDq_of_conj (h : LevOk H SG SQ) (b : Ideles H.E)
    (h1 : ∀ w, Valued.v (compAt w b) = Valued.v (compAt (conjPrime₂ w) b))
    (h2 : ∀ P ∈ uSG SG SQ, Valued.v (compAt P b /
      adAt H.F P (Formalism.UG.sigR (host_cmData R H).sig _ (finAf H.F H.E b : AfE H.F H.E)) - 1) < 1) : b ∈ KDq H SQ := by
  refine mem_KDq_of_local H SQ b fun v => ?_
  have hb0 : ∀ w, compAt w b ≠ 0 := fun w => ((Group.isUnit b).map (compAt w)).ne_zero
  by_cases hv : ∃ P ∈ uSG SG SQ, P.under (𝓞 H.F) = v
  · obtain ⟨P, hP, rfl⟩ := hv
    have hne : conjPrime₂ (conjPrime₂ P) ≠ conjPrime₂ P := by
      rw [CharValCompare.conjP_conjP]
      exact fun e => h.split P hP (e ▸ hP)
    obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := exists_real_approx (host_cmData R H) hne ((Group.isUnit b).map (compAt (conjPrime₂ P))).unit
    rw [IsUnit.unit_spec] at hk
    have k1 := (DetClass.div_sub_one_lt _ hk).2
    have e1 := valued_sigR (host_cmData R H) P (finAf H.F H.E b : AfE H.F H.E)
    have e2 := valued_sigR (host_cmData R H) P ((finAf H.F H.E b : AfE H.F H.E) -
      Formalism.UG.inclE (F := H.F) (FiniteAdeleRing (𝓞 H.F) H.F) (algebraMap H.F H.E k))
    rw [map_sub (Formalism.UG.sigR _ _), map_sub (adAt _ _), map_sub (adAt _ _), sigR_inclE,
      ((host_cmData R H).fixed _).2 ⟨_, rfl⟩, adAt_inclE, adAt_inclE, adAt_finAf] at e2
    rw [adAt_finAf] at e1
    rw [← e1, ← e2] at hk
    have k2 := (DetClass.div_sub_one_lt _ hk).2
    have hs0 : adAt H.F P (Formalism.UG.sigR (host_cmData R H).sig _ (finAf H.F H.E b : AfE H.F H.E)) ≠ 0 := by
      rw [← (Valued.v : Valuation (P.adicCompletion H.E) _).ne_zero_iff, e1, Valuation.ne_zero_iff]
      exact hb0 _
    have k3 : Valued.v (compAt P b / algebraMap H.E _ (algebraMap H.F H.E k) - 1) < 1 := by
      rw [← div_mul_div_cancel₀ hs0]
      exact val_mul_sub_one_lt _ (h2 P hP) k2
    refine ⟨k, fun w hw => ?_⟩
    rcases (under_eq_iff H P w).1 hw with rfl | rfl
    · exact ⟨val_eq_one_of_sub _ k3, fun _ => k3⟩
    · exact ⟨val_eq_one_of_sub _ k1, fun _ => k1⟩
  · by_cases hv' : ∃ P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 H.E), P.under (𝓞 H.F) = v
    · obtain ⟨P, rfl⟩ := hv'
      obtain ⟨-, -, -, hun, -⟩ := HostCM.host_cm R H
      refine ⟨unif (P.under (𝓞 H.F)) ^ (-WithZero.log (Valued.v (compAt P b))), fun w hw => ⟨?_, fun hl => ?_⟩⟩
      · have e1 : Valued.v (compAt w b) = Valued.v (compAt P b) := by
          rcases (under_eq_iff H P w).1 hw with rfl | rfl
          · rfl
          · exact (h1 P).symm
        have e2 := hun w (unif (P.under (𝓞 H.F)))
        rw [hw, ordF_unif_self] at e2
        have e3 : w.valuation H.E (algebraMap H.F H.E (unif (P.under (𝓞 H.F)) : (H.F)ˣ)) = WithZero.exp (-1 : ℤ) := by
          rw [← e2]
          unfold ordF
          rw [neg_neg, WithZero.exp_log (by simp)]
          rfl
        rw [map_div₀, valued_algebraMap, Units.val_zpow_eq_zpow_val, map_zpow₀, map_zpow₀, e3, e1, ← WithZero.exp_zsmul]
        simp only [smul_eq_mul, mul_neg, mul_one, neg_neg]
        rw [WithZero.exp_log ((Valuation.ne_zero_iff _).2 (hb0 P)), div_self ((Valuation.ne_zero_iff _).2 (hb0 P))]
      · refine absurd ?_ hv
        rcases h.level hl with h' | h'
        · exact ⟨w, h', hw⟩
        · exact ⟨_, h', ((under_eq_iff H w _).2 (Or.inr rfl)).trans hw⟩


[GCF_finer_quotient_05] Fixed finite-adele coefficients and approximation by the original rational local generators
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/IwasawaDecomposition/H90USurjective.lean#L168-L242

theorem map_one_tmul {ι : AfE F E →+* FiniteAdeleRing (𝓞 E) E} (h1 : ∀ x : E, ι (x ⊗ₜ[F] 1) = algebraMap E _ x)
    (h2 : ∀ z ∈ RInt F E, ι z ∈ intAd E) (b : FiniteAdeleRing (𝓞 F) F) : ι (1 ⊗ₜ[F] b) = adMap F E b := by
  rw [← sub_eq_zero]
  refine (GlobalTheory.AdLat.integral_finiteAdele_complete_separated E).1 _ fun N hN => ?_
  obtain ⟨k, a, ha, rfl⟩ := (GlobalTheory.AdLat.strong_approximation_mod_nat F N hN).1 b
  have hi : (1 : E) ⊗ₜ[F] a ∈ RInt F E := by simpa using levelaf_tmul_mem (F := F) (1 : 𝓞 E) ha
  refine ⟨ι (1 ⊗ₜ[F] a) - adMap F E a, sub_mem (h2 _ hi) (adMap_int ha), ?_⟩
  have e : (1 : E) ⊗ₜ[F] (algebraMap F (FiniteAdeleRing (𝓞 F) F) k) = algebraMap F E k ⊗ₜ[F] 1 := by
    rw [Algebra.algebraMap_eq_smul_one, ← TensorProduct.smul_tmul, Algebra.algebraMap_eq_smul_one]
  rw [TensorProduct.tmul_add, map_add, map_add, e, h1, adMap_algebraMap, TensorProduct.tmul_smul, map_nsmul, map_nsmul,
    smul_sub]
  abel

theorem exists_eq_one_tmul {σ : E →ₐ[F] E} (hσ : ∀ x, ∃ t : F, algebraMap F E t = x + σ x) {z : AfE F E}
    (hz : sigR σ (FiniteAdeleRing (𝓞 F) F) z = z) : ∃ b, z = 1 ⊗ₜ[F] b := by
  have h : ∀ y : AfE F E, ∃ b, y + sigR σ (FiniteAdeleRing (𝓞 F) F) y = 1 ⊗ₜ[F] b := fun y => by
    induction y using TensorProduct.induction_on with
    | zero => exact ⟨0, by simp⟩
    | add y y' hy hy' =>
      obtain ⟨b, hb⟩ := hy
      obtain ⟨b', hb'⟩ := hy'
      exact ⟨b + b', by rw [map_add, TensorProduct.tmul_add, ← hb, ← hb']; abel⟩
    | tmul x a =>
      obtain ⟨t, ht⟩ := hσ x
      refine ⟨t • a, ?_⟩
      have e : sigR σ (FiniteAdeleRing (𝓞 F) F) (x ⊗ₜ[F] a) = σ x ⊗ₜ[F] a := by simp [sigR]
      rw [e, ← TensorProduct.add_tmul, ← ht, ← TensorProduct.smul_tmul, Algebra.algebraMap_eq_smul_one]
  obtain ⟨b, hb⟩ := h z
  refine ⟨(2 : F)⁻¹ • b, ?_⟩
  rw [TensorProduct.tmul_smul, ← hb, hz, ← two_smul F z, smul_smul, inv_mul_cancel₀ two_ne_zero, one_smul]

theorem exists_mul_congr_one (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)) {b : v.adicCompletion F} (hb : b ≠ 0) :
    ∃ k : Fˣ, Valued.v (algebraMap F (v.adicCompletion F) k * b - 1) ≤ WithZero.exp (-((1 : ℕ) : ℤ)) := by
  have hc : Continuous fun y : v.adicCompletion F => y * b - 1 := (continuous_mul_const b).sub continuous_const
  have ho : IsOpen {y : v.adicCompletion F | Valued.v (y * b - 1) ≤ WithZero.exp (-((1 : ℕ) : ℤ))} :=
    (GlobalTheory.Idele.isOpen_valued_le (K := F) v (-((1 : ℕ) : ℤ))).preimage hc
  obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := (HeightOneSpectrum.denseRange_algebraMap (K := F) v).exists_mem_open ho
    ⟨b⁻¹, by simp [inv_mul_cancel₀ hb]⟩
  have h0 : k ≠ 0 := by
    rintro rfl
    simp only [map_zero, Set.mem_setOf_eq, zero_mul, zero_sub, Valuation.map_neg, map_one] at hk
    rw [← WithZero.exp_zero, WithZero.exp_le_exp] at hk
    omega
  exact ⟨Units.mk0 k h0, hk⟩

theorem exists_locF_mul {a : Ideles E} {b : FiniteAdeleRing (𝓞 F) F} (hab : (a : AdeleRing (𝓞 E) E).2 = adMap F E b)
    {T : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E))} (hT : T.Finite) :
    ∃ (S : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) (k : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F) → Fˣ),
      (∏ v ∈ S, locF F v (k v)) * a ∈ unitIdl E ∧
        ∀ w ∈ T, Valued.v (compAt w ((∏ v ∈ S, locF F v (k v)) * a) - 1) < 1 := by
  classical
  have hk : ∀ v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F), ∃ k : Fˣ, b v ≠ 0 →
      Valued.v (algebraMap F (v.adicCompletion F) k * b v - 1) ≤ WithZero.exp (-((1 : ℕ) : ℤ)) := fun v => by
    by_cases h : b v = 0
    · exact ⟨1, fun h' => absurd h h'⟩
    · obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := exists_mul_congr_one v h
      exact ⟨k, fun _ => hk⟩
  choose k hk using hk
  have hfin := hT.union (IdlFact.finite_valAt_ne_one a)
  set S := hfin.toFinset.image fun w => w.under (𝓞 F) with hS
  set P := ∏ v ∈ S, locF (L := E) F v (k v) with hP
  have hc : ∀ w, compAt w P =
      if w.under (𝓞 F) ∈ S then compAt w (diagUnits E (upF F (k (w.under (𝓞 F))))) else 1 := fun w => by
    rw [hP, map_prod, Finset.prod_congr rfl fun v _ => compAt_locF F w v (k v), Finset.prod_ite_eq]
  have ha : ∀ w, compAt w a = locMap F w (b (w.under (𝓞 F))) := fun w => by
    rw [← adMap_apply, ← hab]
    rfl
  have hlt : WithZero.exp (-((1 : ℕ) : ℤ)) < (1 : WithZero (Multiplicative ℤ)) := by
    rw [← WithZero.exp_zero, WithZero.exp_lt_exp]; norm_num
  have h1 : ∀ w, w.under (𝓞 F) ∈ S → Valued.v (compAt w (P * a) - 1) < 1 := fun w hw => by
    have hb : b (w.under (𝓞 F)) ≠ 0 := fun h0 => by
      have hu : IsUnit (compAt w a) := (Group.isUnit a).map (compAt w)
      rw [ha, h0, map_zero] at hu
      exact not_isUnit_zero hu
    have e : compAt w (diagUnits E (upF F (k (w.under (𝓞 F))))) =


[GCF_finer_quotient_06] Canonical quotient map from norm-one finite adeles and universal lift equation

def finIdele (ι : AfE F E ≃+* FiniteAdeleRing (𝓞 E) E) : (AfE F E)ˣ →* Ideles E :=
  Units.map ((MonoidHom.inr (InfiniteAdeleRing E) (FiniteAdeleRing (𝓞 E) E)).comp ι.toRingHom.toMonoidHom)

section NormOne

open Formalism.L2.DIV Formalism.UG

variable {R : Ranks} (H : Formalism.BasicNotions.Host R) (SQ : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 H.E)))

theorem mk_eq_of_snd_eq {a a' : Ideles H.E} (h : (a : AdeleRing (𝓞 H.E) H.E).2 = (a' : AdeleRing (𝓞 H.E) H.E).2) :
    (QuotientGroup.mk a : DtQ H SQ) = QuotientGroup.mk a' := by
  have e : ∀ w, compAt w (a⁻¹ * a') = 1 := fun w => by
    have e' : compAt w a' = compAt w a := congrArg (fun z : FiniteAdeleRing (𝓞 H.E) H.E => z w) h.symm
    rw [map_mul, e', ← map_mul, inv_mul_cancel, map_one]
  refine QuotientGroup.eq.2 (mem_KDq_of_congQ H SQ (mem_congQ_of_eq_one H SQ (fun w => ?_) fun w _ => e w))
  show Valued.v (compAt w (a⁻¹ * a')) = 1
  rw [e, map_one]

theorem mem_KDq_of_adMap {a : Ideles H.E} {b : FiniteAdeleRing (𝓞 H.F) H.F}
    (hab : (a : AdeleRing (𝓞 H.E) H.E).2 = GlobalTheory.AdeleBaseChange.adMap H.F H.E b) : a ∈ KDq H SQ := by
  obtain ⟨S, k, hu, h1⟩ := exists_locF_mul hab (finite_level H SQ)
  exact (Subgroup.mul_mem_cancel_left _ (prod_mem fun v _ => locF_mem_KDq H SQ v (k v))).1
    (mem_KDq_of_congQ H SQ ⟨hu, fun j v hv => h1 v (Or.inl ⟨j, hv⟩), fun v hv => h1 v (Or.inr hv)⟩)

variable (σ : H.E →ₐ[H.F] H.E) (hσ : ∀ x, σ x = H.conj x) (ι : AfE H.F H.E ≃+* FiniteAdeleRing (𝓞 H.E) H.E)
  (h1 : ∀ x : H.E, ι (x ⊗ₜ[H.F] 1) = algebraMap H.E _ x)
  (h2 : ∀ z, z ∈ RInt H.F H.E ↔ ∀ Q : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 H.E), Valued.v (ι z Q) ≤ 1)

include hσ h1 h2 in

theorem finIdele_mem_KDq {x : (AfE H.F H.E)ˣ} (hx : h90 σ x = 1) : finIdele ι x ∈ KDq H SQ := by
  have hcm : IsCMField H.E := H.isCMField_hostE
  have hc : ∀ x, IsCMField.complexConj H.E x = H.conj x := fun x => bridge_star_holds R H x
  have ht : ∀ y : H.E, ∃ t : H.F, algebraMap H.F H.E t = y + σ y := fun y =>
    ⟨⟨y + σ y, (IsCMField.complexConj_eq_self_iff H.E _).1 (by
      rw [hσ, ← hc, map_add, IsCMField.complexConj_apply_apply, add_comm])⟩, rfl⟩
  have hfix : conjId σ x = x := (div_eq_one.1 hx).symm
  obtain ⟨b, hb⟩ := exists_eq_one_tmul ht (z := (x : AfE H.F H.E)) (congrArg Units.val hfix)
  refine mem_KDq_of_adMap H SQ (b := b) ?_
  show ι (x : AfE H.F H.E) = _
  rw [hb]
  exact map_one_tmul (ι := ι.toRingHom) h1 (fun z hz => (h2 z).1 hz) b

def h90U : (AfE H.F H.E)ˣ →* U1 σ :=
  (h90 σ).codRestrict (U1 σ) fun x => (GlobalTheory.Mem.DetClass.exists_detClass R H σ hσ ι h1 h2).1 ▸ ⟨x, rfl⟩

theorem h90U_surjective : Function.Surjective (h90U H σ hσ ι h1 h2) := fun y => by
  obtain ⟨x, hx⟩ : (y : (AfE H.F H.E)ˣ) ∈ (h90 σ).range := (GlobalTheory.Mem.DetClass.exists_detClass R H σ hσ ι h1 h2).1.ge y.2
  exact ⟨x, Subtype.ext hx⟩

def normOneIdeleToFinerDeterminantClass : U1 σ →* DtQ H SQ :=
  (h90U H σ hσ ι h1 h2).liftOfSurjective (h90U_surjective H σ hσ ι h1 h2)
    ⟨(QuotientGroup.mk' (KDq H SQ)).comp (finIdele ι), fun _ hx =>
      (QuotientGroup.eq_one_iff _).2 (finIdele_mem_KDq H SQ σ hσ ι h1 h2 (congrArg Subtype.val hx))⟩

theorem normOneIdeleToFinerDeterminantClass_eq_mk (x : (AfE H.F H.E)ˣ) (h : h90 σ x ∈ U1 σ) (a : Ideles H.E)
    (ha : (a : AdeleRing (𝓞 H.E) H.E).2 = ι (x : AfE H.F H.E)) :
    normOneIdeleToFinerDeterminantClass H SQ σ hσ ι h1 h2 ⟨_, h⟩ = QuotientGroup.mk a :=
  (MonoidHom.liftOfRightInverse_comp_apply (h90U H σ hσ ι h1 h2) _ _ _ x).trans (mk_eq_of_snd_eq H SQ ha.symm)

theorem u1ToDtQ_surjective : Function.Surjective (normOneIdeleToFinerDeterminantClass H SQ σ hσ ι h1 h2) := fun y => by
  obtain ⟨a, rfl⟩ := QuotientGroup.mk_surjective y
  let x : (AfE H.F H.E)ˣ := Units.map (ι.symm.toRingHom.toMonoidHom.comp
    (MonoidHom.snd (InfiniteAdeleRing H.E) (FiniteAdeleRing (𝓞 H.E) H.E))) a
  exact ⟨h90U H σ hσ ι h1 h2 x, normOneIdeleToFinerDeterminantClass_eq_mk H SQ σ hσ ι h1 h2 x _ a (ι.apply_symm_apply _).symm⟩

theorem eq_u1ToDtQ {f : U1 σ →* DtQ H SQ} (hf : ∀ (x : (AfE H.F H.E)ˣ) (h : h90 σ x ∈ U1 σ) (a : Ideles H.E),
    (a : AdeleRing (𝓞 H.E) H.E).2 = ι (x : AfE H.F H.E) → f ⟨_, h⟩ = QuotientGroup.mk a) :
    f = normOneIdeleToFinerDeterminantClass H SQ σ hσ ι h1 h2 := by
  refine MonoidHom.ext fun y => ?_
  obtain ⟨x, rfl⟩ := h90U_surjective H σ hσ ι h1 h2 y
  exact (hf x _ (finIdele ι x) rfl).trans (normOneIdeleToFinerDeterminantClass_eq_mk H SQ σ hσ ι h1 h2 x _ (finIdele ι x) rfl).symm

def detU : Formalism.L2.Herm.unitaryOneFiniteAdeles (host_cmData R H) →* U1 (host_cmData R H).sig :=
  (Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det.comp (Formalism.L2.Herm.unitaryOneFiniteAdeles (host_cmData R H)).subtype).codRestrict _ fun x =>
    Units.ext (DetLift.det_mul_conj_det _ _ x.2)

def u1Dt (SQ : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 H.E))) : Formalism.L2.Herm.unitaryOneFiniteAdeles (host_cmData R H) →* DtQ H SQ :=
  (normOneIdeleToFinerDeterminantClass H SQ (host_cmData R H).sig (fun x => bridge_star_holds R H x) (afIso H.F H.E)
    (afIso_tmul_one H.F H.E) (mem_rInt_iff H.F H.E)).comp (detU H)

theorem normOneFiniteAdeleDetClass_of_isDetLift (SQ : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 H.E))) (x : Formalism.L2.Herm.unitaryOneFiniteAdeles (host_cmData R H)) (a : GlobalTheory.Mem.Chr.Ideles H.E)
    (ha : IsDetLift (host_cmData R H) x.1 a) : u1Dt H SQ x = QuotientGroup.mk a := by
  have e : detU H x = ⟨h90 (host_cmData R H).sig (finAf H.F H.E a), by
      rw [ha]; exact (detU H x).2⟩ := Subtype.ext ha.symm
  show normOneIdeleToFinerDeterminantClass H SQ _ _ _ _ _ (detU H x) = _
  rw [e]
  exact normOneIdeleToFinerDeterminantClass_eq_mk H SQ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ (afIso_finAf H.F H.E a).symm

theorem exists_lift (x : Formalism.L2.Herm.unitaryOneFiniteAdeles (host_cmData R H)) : ∃ a : GlobalTheory.Mem.Chr.Ideles H.E, IsDetLift (host_cmData R H) x.1 a :=
  DetLift.exists_isDetLift R H 1 x.1 x.2

theorem exists_of_idele (a : GlobalTheory.Mem.Chr.Ideles H.E) : ∃ x : Formalism.L2.Herm.unitaryOneFiniteAdeles (host_cmData R H), IsDetLift (host_cmData R H) x.1 a := by
  have hu : h90 (host_cmData R H).sig (finAf H.F H.E a) ∈ U1 (host_cmData R H).sig :=
    (DetClass.exists_detClass R H (host_cmData R H).sig (fun x => bridge_star_holds R H x) (afIso H.F H.E)
      (afIso_tmul_one H.F H.E) (mem_rInt_iff H.F H.E)).1.le ⟨_, rfl⟩
  refine ⟨⟨Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.scalar (Fin 1) (h90 (host_cmData R H).sig (finAf H.F H.E a)), ?_⟩, ?_⟩
  · show Formalism.UG.cT _ _ * _ * _ = _
    have hz := congrArg Units.val (show (h90 (host_cmData R H).sig (finAf H.F H.E a)) * conjId (host_cmData R H).sig (h90 (host_cmData R H).sig (finAf H.F H.E a)) = 1 from hu)
    ext i j
    obtain rfl : i = 0 := Subsingleton.elim _ _
    obtain rfl : j = 0 := Subsingleton.elim _ _
    simp [Formalism.UG.cT, Matrix.mul_apply]
    exact (mul_comm _ _).trans hz
  · exact Units.ext (by simp)

theorem normOneFiniteAdeleDetClass_surjective (SQ : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 H.E))) : Function.Surjective (u1Dt H SQ) := fun t => by
  obtain ⟨a, rfl⟩ := QuotientGroup.mk_surjective t
  obtain ⟨x, hx⟩ := exists_of_idele H a
  exact ⟨x, normOneFiniteAdeleDetClass_of_isDetLift H SQ x a hx⟩


[GCF_scalar_determinant_02] Original finite-adelic topology and open kernel endpoint
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/IwasawaDecomposition/IsOpenKerNormOneFiniteAdeleDetClass.lean#L18-L83

theorem lift_mem_KDq (hl : LevOk H SG SQ) (x : Formalism.L2.Herm.unitaryOneFiniteAdeles (host_cmData R H)) (b : GlobalTheory.Mem.Chr.Ideles H.E)
    (hb : IsDetLift (host_cmData R H) x.1 b) (c : 𝓞 H.E) (hc : ∀ P ∈ uSG SG SQ, c ∈ P.asIdeal) (s : AfE H.F H.E)
    (hs : s ∈ RInt H.F H.E)
    (hx : ((x.1 : GL (Fin 1) (AfE H.F H.E)) : Matrix (Fin 1) (Fin 1) (AfE H.F H.E)) 0 0 =
      1 + iA H.F H.E (algebraMap (𝓞 H.E) H.E c) * s) : b ∈ KDq H SQ := by
  have hd : ((x.1 : GL (Fin 1) (AfE H.F H.E)) : Matrix (Fin 1) (Fin 1) (AfE H.F H.E)).det =
      1 + iA H.F H.E (algebraMap (𝓞 H.E) H.E c) * s := by
    rw [Matrix.det_fin_one, hx]
  have hdd := DetLift.det_mul_conj_det _ _ x.2
  set d := ((x.1 : GL (Fin 1) (AfE H.F H.E)) : Matrix (Fin 1) (Fin 1) (AfE H.F H.E)).det with hdef
  have hci : ∀ w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 H.E), adAt H.F w (iA H.F H.E (algebraMap (𝓞 H.E) H.E c)) =
      algebraMap H.E _ (algebraMap (𝓞 H.E) H.E c) := fun w => adAt_inclE H.F w _
  have hsv : ∀ w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 H.E), Valued.v (adAt H.F w s) ≤ 1 := (mem_rInt_iff H.F H.E s).1 hs
  have hle : ∀ w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 H.E), Valued.v (adAt H.F w d) ≤ 1 := fun w => by
    rw [hd, map_add, map_one, map_mul, hci]
    refine Valuation.map_add_le _ (by rw [map_one]) ?_
    rw [map_mul, valued_algebraMap]
    exact mul_le_one' (HeightOneSpectrum.valuation_le_one _ _) (hsv w)
  have hv1 : ∀ w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 H.E), Valued.v (adAt H.F w d) = 1 := fun w => by
    have e := congrArg (fun z => Valued.v (adAt H.F w z)) hdd
    simp only [map_mul, map_one] at e
    refine le_antisymm (hle w) ?_
    have e' : Valued.v (adAt H.F w d) * Valued.v (adAt H.F (conjPrime₂ w) d) = 1 := by
      rw [← valued_sigR (host_cmData R H) w d]
      exact e
    calc (1 : _) = Valued.v (adAt H.F w d) * Valued.v (adAt H.F (conjPrime₂ w) d) := e'.symm
      _ ≤ _ := mul_le_of_le_one_right' (hle _)
  have hcomp := fun w => DetSpec.compAt_eq hb w
  refine mem_KDq_of_conj hl b (fun w => ?_) fun P hP => ?_
  · rw [hcomp w, map_mul, hv1 w, one_mul, valued_sigR, adAt_finAf]
  · have h0 : adAt H.F P (Formalism.UG.sigR (host_cmData R H).sig _ (finAf H.F H.E b : AfE H.F H.E)) ≠ 0 := by
      rw [← (Valued.v : Valuation (P.adicCompletion H.E) _).ne_zero_iff, valued_sigR, adAt_finAf]
      exact DetSpec.val_compAt_ne _ _
    rw [hcomp P, mul_div_cancel_right₀ _ h0]
    show Valued.v (adAt H.F P d - 1) < 1
    rw [hd, map_add, map_one, add_sub_cancel_left, map_mul, hci, map_mul,
      valued_algebraMap]
    have hlt : P.valuation H.E (algebraMap (𝓞 H.E) H.E c) < 1 :=
      (HeightOneSpectrum.valuation_lt_one_iff_mem P c).2 (hc P hP)
    exact (mul_le_of_le_one_right' (hsv P)).trans_lt hlt

theorem isOpen_ker_u1Dt : ∀ (R : Ranks) (H : Formalism.BasicNotions.Host R) (SG SQ : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 H.E))), LevOk H SG SQ → letI := Formalism.BasicNotions.tensTopL (F := H.F) (E := H.E) (Formalism.L2.Herm.Af H.F); IsOpen {x : Formalism.L2.Herm.unitaryOneFiniteAdeles (host_cmData R H) | u1Dt H SQ x = 1} := by
  intro R H SG SQ hl
  classical
  obtain ⟨a, ha0, ha⟩ := rlt_exists_a (uSG SG SQ)
  set c : AfE H.F H.E := iA H.F H.E (algebraMap (𝓞 H.E) H.E a)⁻¹ with hc'
  have hca : iA H.F H.E (algebraMap (𝓞 H.E) H.E a) * c = 1 := by
    rw [hc', ← map_mul, mul_inv_cancel₀ (by simpa using ha0), map_one]
  let W : Set (GL (Fin 1) (AfE H.F H.E)) :=
    {u | c * ((u : Matrix (Fin 1) (Fin 1) (AfE H.F H.E)) 0 0 - 1) ∈ Formalism.L2.Herm.intAf H.F H.E}
  have hWo : IsOpen W := by
    refine rlt_isOpen_intAf.preimage ?_
    exact continuous_const.mul ((Units.continuous_val.matrix_elem 0 0).sub continuous_const)
  have hW1 : (1 : GL (Fin 1) (AfE H.F H.E)) ∈ W := by
    show c * (((1 : GL (Fin 1) (AfE H.F H.E)) : Matrix (Fin 1) (Fin 1) (AfE H.F H.E)) 0 0 - 1) ∈ Formalism.L2.Herm.intAf H.F H.E
    simp
  have hsub : ∀ x : Formalism.L2.Herm.unitaryOneFiniteAdeles (host_cmData R H), x.1 ∈ W → x ∈ (u1Dt H SQ).ker := by
    intro x hx
    obtain ⟨b, hb⟩ := exists_lift H x
    rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, normOneFiniteAdeleDetClass_of_isDetLift H SQ x b hb]
    refine (QuotientGroup.eq_one_iff b).2 (lift_mem_KDq hl x b hb a ha _ hx ?_)
    rw [← mul_assoc, hca, one_mul]
    ring
  have hnh : (((u1Dt H SQ).ker : Subgroup _) : Set (Formalism.L2.Herm.unitaryOneFiniteAdeles (host_cmData R H))) ∈ 𝓝 (1 : Formalism.L2.Herm.unitaryOneFiniteAdeles (host_cmData R H)) :=
    Filter.mem_of_superset ((hWo.preimage continuous_subtype_val).mem_nhds hW1) fun x hx => hsub x hx
  exact Subgroup.isOpen_of_mem_nhds _ hnh


[GCF_scalar_determinant_03] Integral norm-one kernel and exact UOQ image
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/IwasawaDecomposition/ExistsFullHermitianColumnsTrueColumns.lean#L27-L56
Range SHA256: 1725b6c79236543cf91954a44445ab1fdcd2fde37121112b9d2af59b3542ec3e
theorem mem_integralNormOne_of_normOneDetClass_eq_one (x : Formalism.L2.Herm.unitaryOneFiniteAdeles (host_cmData R H)) (h : u1Dt H SQ x = 1) :
    x.1 ∈ Formalism.L2.Herm.IntegralUnitGroup (host_cmData R H) := by
  obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := exists_lift H x
  have e : (QuotientGroup.mk a : DtQ H SQ) = 1 := (normOneFiniteAdeleDetClass_of_isDetLift H SQ x a ha).symm.trans h
  have ha' : a ∈ KDq H SQ := (QuotientGroup.eq_one_iff a).1 e
  refine (GlobalTheory.T.mem_normOneIntegral_iff_conjugate_valuations_eq R H x a ha).2 fun w => ?_
  exact (GlobalTheory.Mem.Chr.mem_KD_sup_unitIdl_iff R H a).1 (Subgroup.mem_sup_left (KDq_le_KD H SQ ha')) w (conjPrime₂ w)
    ((isConjPl_iff H w _).2 rfl)

theorem unitIdeleImage_eq_map_integralNormOne : UOQ H SQ = ((Formalism.L2.Herm.IntegralUnitGroup (host_cmData R H)).subgroupOf (Formalism.L2.Herm.unitaryOneFiniteAdeles (host_cmData R H))).map (u1Dt H SQ) := by
  ext t
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨a, ha, rfl⟩
    obtain ⟨x, hx⟩ := exists_of_idele H a
    refine ⟨x, ?_, normOneFiniteAdeleDetClass_of_isDetLift H SQ x a hx⟩
    refine (GlobalTheory.T.mem_normOneIntegral_iff_conjugate_valuations_eq R H x a hx).2 fun w => ?_
    exact (ha w).trans (ha (conjPrime₂ w)).symm
  · rintro ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩
    obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := exists_lift H x
    rw [normOneFiniteAdeleDetClass_of_isDetLift H SQ x a ha, mk_mem_UOQ, KDq_sup_unitIdl]
    refine (GlobalTheory.Mem.Chr.mem_KD_sup_unitIdl_iff R H a).2 fun w w' hw => ?_
    rw [(isConjPl_iff H w w').1 hw]
    exact (GlobalTheory.T.mem_normOneIntegral_iff_conjugate_valuations_eq R H x a ha).1 hx w

end Gen

[GCF_decorated_points_01] Given local frames, point extension and positive-part point idele
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Definitions/AutomorphicMembership/ReferenceCharactersFactorTame.lean#L34-L182
Range SHA256: eda0396f6d571ba43d32dfdcdedd21c527ff216536319feae48c71f1c7ff7580
def latFr (P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) (N : Submodule (𝓞 E) (Sp E n)) : Sp E n ≃ₗ[E] Sp E n :=
  if localization₂ P.asIdeal N = stdAt E n P then 1 else frameAt P N

theorem isFr_latFr (P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) {N : Submodule (𝓞 E) (Sp E n)} (hN : Submodule.IsLattice E N) :
    IsFr P N (latFr P N) := by
  unfold latFr
  split_ifs with h
  · intro x
    rw [h]
    rfl
  · exact CharValCompare.isFr_frameAt (CharValCompare.exists_isFr P hN)

open Classical in

def ptFr (p : DecoF E n ST SG Q) (P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) : Sp E n ≃ₗ[E] Sp E n :=
  if h : P ∈ SG then p.deco.1.liftFr ⟨P, h⟩ else if h' : P ∈ Q then p.fr ⟨P, h'⟩ else latFr P p.deco.1.N

theorem isLiftFr_ptFr (p : DecoF E n ST SG Q) (w : SG) : IsLiftFr p.deco.1 w (ptFr p w.1) := by
  unfold ptFr
  rw [dif_pos w.2]
  exact TopStep.liftFr_spec p.deco.2 w

theorem ptFr_of_mem (p : DecoF E n ST SG Q) {P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)} (h : P ∉ SG) (h' : P ∈ Q) :
    ptFr p P = p.fr ⟨P, h'⟩ := by
  unfold ptFr
  rw [dif_neg h, dif_pos h']

theorem isFr_ptFr (p : DecoF E n ST SG Q) (P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) : IsFr P p.deco.1.N (ptFr p P) := by
  by_cases h : P ∈ SG
  · exact (isLiftFr_ptFr p ⟨P, h⟩).1
  by_cases h' : P ∈ Q
  · rw [ptFr_of_mem p h h']
    exact p.2 ⟨P, h'⟩
  · unfold ptFr
    rw [dif_neg h, dif_neg h']
    exact isFr_latFr P p.deco.2.lat

theorem ptFr_congr {ST' : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E))} {p : DecoF E n ST SG Q} {p' : DecoF E n ST' SG Q}
    (hN : p.deco.1.N = p'.deco.1.N) (hbe : p.deco.1.be = p'.deco.1.be) (hfr : p.fr = p'.fr) : ptFr p = ptFr p' := by
  funext P
  unfold ptFr Deco.liftFr Deco.at
  rw [hN, hbe, hfr]

theorem frsF_eq_ptFr {X : Fields} {Pr : Primes X} {R : Ranks}
    (sel : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 X.F) → HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 X.E)) {S : Set ℕ} (mu : Tup X.F X.E X.K S) {u : Pr.Word} {j : ℕ}
    (p : ChPt (R := R) sel mu u j) (P : rowSG Pr sel mu) : frsF sel mu p P = ptFr p P.1 := by
  unfold frsF
  split_ifs with h
  · unfold ptFr
    rw [dif_pos h]
  · exact (ptFr_of_mem p h _).symm

def extTo (SQ : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E))) (p : DecoF E n ST SG Q) : DecoF E n ST SG SQ :=
  ⟨(p.deco, fun P => ptFr p P.1), fun P => isFr_ptFr p P.1⟩

theorem extTo_deco (SQ : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E))) (p : DecoF E n ST SG Q) : (extTo SQ p).deco = p.deco := rfl

theorem extTo_fr (SQ : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E))) (p : DecoF E n ST SG Q) (P : SQ) :
    (extTo SQ p).fr P = ptFr p P.1 := rfl

theorem extTo_self (hd : Disjoint SG Q) (p : DecoF E n ST SG Q) : extTo Q p = p :=
  Subtype.ext (Prod.ext rfl (funext fun P => ptFr_of_mem p (Finset.disjoint_right.1 hd P.2) P.2))

open Classical in

def detIdl (SB : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E))) (N : Submodule (𝓞 E) (Sp E n))
    (fr : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E) → Sp E n ≃ₗ[E] Sp E n) : Ideles E :=
  (∏ P ∈ SB, localIdele E P (atFin P (LinearEquiv.det (fr P)))) *
    ∏ᶠ w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E),
      if w ∈ SB ∨ conjPrime₂ w ∈ SB then 1 else localIdele E w (atFin w (unif w)) ^ (ordSub w (detSub N)).toNat

omit [StarRing E] in

theorem finite_ordSub {N : Submodule (𝓞 E) (Sp E n)} (hN : Submodule.IsLattice E N) :
    {w | ordSub w (detSub N) ≠ 0}.Finite :=
  (ordSub_detSub_lattice E n N hN.fg hN.span_eq_top).1

open Classical in

theorem finite_detIdl (SB : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E))) {N : Submodule (𝓞 E) (Sp E n)} (hN : Submodule.IsLattice E N) :
    Function.HasFiniteMulSupport fun w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E) =>
      if w ∈ SB ∨ conjPrime₂ w ∈ SB then 1 else localIdele E w (atFin w (unif w)) ^ (ordSub w (detSub N)).toNat := by
  refine (finite_ordSub hN).subset fun w hw h0 => hw ?_
  simp [h0]

open Classical in

theorem compAt_detIdl (SB : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E))) {N : Submodule (𝓞 E) (Sp E n)} (hN : Submodule.IsLattice E N)
    (fr : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E) → Sp E n ≃ₗ[E] Sp E n) (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) :
    compAt w (detIdl SB N fr) =
      if w ∈ SB then algebraMap E _ ((LinearEquiv.det (fr w) : Eˣ) : E)
      else if conjPrime₂ w ∈ SB then 1 else algebraMap E _ ((unif w : Eˣ) : E) ^ (ordSub w (detSub N)).toNat := by
  unfold detIdl
  rw [map_mul, map_prod, MonoidHom.map_finprod _ (finite_detIdl SB hN), finprod_eq_single _ w]
  · by_cases h : w ∈ SB
    · rw [Finset.prod_eq_single_of_mem w h fun Q _ hQ => compAt_localIdele_ne hQ.symm _, compAt_localIdele_self,
        if_pos (Or.inl h), if_pos h, map_one, mul_one]
      rfl
    · rw [Finset.prod_eq_one fun Q hQ => compAt_localIdele_ne (ne_of_mem_of_not_mem hQ h).symm _, one_mul, if_neg h]
      by_cases h' : conjPrime₂ w ∈ SB
      · rw [if_pos (Or.inr h'), if_pos h', map_one]
      · rw [if_neg (not_or.2 ⟨h, h'⟩), if_neg h', map_pow, compAt_localIdele_self]
        rfl
  · intro v hv
    split_ifs
    · exact map_one _
    · rw [map_pow, compAt_localIdele_ne hv.symm, one_pow]

theorem finPartI_detIdl (SB : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E))) {N : Submodule (𝓞 E) (Sp E n)} (hN : Submodule.IsLattice E N)
    (fr : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E) → Sp E n ≃ₗ[E] Sp E n) : finPartI E (detIdl SB N fr) = detIdl SB N fr := by
  classical
  unfold detIdl
  rw [map_mul, map_prod, MonoidHom.map_finprod _ (finite_detIdl SB hN)]
  congr 1
  refine finprod_congr fun w => ?_
  split_ifs
  · exact map_one _
  · rw [map_pow, KerDet.finPartI_localIdele]

theorem ordSub_detSub_eq {P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)} {N : Submodule (𝓞 E) (Sp E n)} {f : Sp E n ≃ₗ[E] Sp E n}
    (hN : Submodule.IsLattice E N) (hf : IsFr P N f) : ordSub P (detSub N) = ordF P (LinearEquiv.det f) := by
  classical
  have e : detSub N = CharValCompare.detI N := by
    unfold detSub CharValCompare.detI
    congr 1
    ext z
    constructor
    · rintro ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩
      refine ⟨(Matrix.of x).transpose, fun v hv => ?_, by rw [Matrix.det_transpose]⟩
      choose r hr using (mem_stdLat v).1 hv
      have : (Matrix.of x).transpose.mulVec v = ∑ i, r i • x i := by
        funext j
        simp [Matrix.mulVec, dotProduct, Finset.sum_apply, Algebra.smul_def, hr, mul_comm]
      rw [this]
      exact Submodule.sum_mem _ fun i _ => N.smul_mem _ (hx i)
    · rintro ⟨A, hA, rfl⟩
      refine ⟨fun i => A.mulVec (Pi.single i 1), fun i => hA _ (by simpa using single_mem_stdLat (E := E) i 1), ?_⟩
      rw [← Matrix.det_transpose]
      congr 1
      ext i j
      simp
  have hg := CharValCompare.gen_detI hf
  rw [← e] at hg
  have hl := (ordSub_detSub_lattice E n N hN.fg hN.span_eq_top).2.1 P
  have h0 : P.valuation E (CharValCompare.dt f) ≠ 0 := (Valuation.ne_zero_iff _).2 hg.1
  apply le_antisymm
  · obtain ⟨s, hs, hx⟩ := (mem_locP _ _ _).1 ((hg.2 _).2 le_rfl)
    have hs0 : s ≠ 0 := by
      rintro rfl


[GCF_decorated_points_02] All four IsPtOf fields, IsDetLift and actual frame realization
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Definitions/AutomorphicMembership/ReferenceCharactersFactorTame.lean#L256-L306
Range SHA256: 660b6ad549c955f8cf80d4c54792ee29ca59df88adf6c9e6e9add4e4c0f2168c
def pullV {n : ℕ} (g : GL (Fin n) (AfE F E)) (x : Sp E n) : Fin n → AfE F E :=
  ((g⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) (AfE F E)) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (AfE F E)).mulVec
    fun i => Formalism.UG.inclE (F := F) (FiniteAdeleRing (𝓞 F) F) (x i)

variable [StarRing E] (hc : Formalism.BasicNotions.CMData F E) {n : ℕ} {ST SG Q : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E))}

structure IsPtOf (g : GL (Fin n) (AfE F E)) (p : DecoF E n ST SG Q) : Prop where
  uni : g ∈ Formalism.L2.Herm.UstdAf hc n
  lat : Formalism.L2.Herm.Carries g (stdLat E n) p.deco.1.N
  f : ∀ (P : ST), ∀ x ∈ p.deco.1.N, ∀ i : Fin n, i.1 + 1 = n → HasRes P.1 (adAt F P.1 (pullV g x i)) (p.deco.1.f P x)
  be : ∀ (w : SG), ∀ x ∈ p.deco.1.N, ∀ i, HasRes w.1 (adAt F w.1 (pullV g x i)) (p.deco.1.be w x i)
  fr : ∀ (P : Q) (i j : Fin n), adAt F P.1 ((g : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (AfE F E)) i j) =
    algebraMap E _ (p.fr P (Pi.single j 1) i)

def IsDetLift (g : GL (Fin n) (AfE F E)) (a : Ideles E) : Prop :=
  h90 hc.sig (finAf F E a) = Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det g

variable {hc}

theorem IsPtOf.of_extTo {SQ : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E))} (hQ : Q ⊆ SQ) (hd : Disjoint SG Q)
    {g : GL (Fin n) (AfE F E)} {p : DecoF E n ST SG Q} (h : IsPtOf hc g (extTo SQ p)) : IsPtOf hc g p :=
  ⟨h.uni, h.lat, h.f, h.be, fun P i j => by
    rw [h.fr ⟨P.1, hQ P.2⟩, extTo_fr, ptFr_of_mem p (Finset.disjoint_right.1 hd P.2) P.2]⟩

theorem IsPtOf.extTo {SQ : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E))} (hQ : SQ ⊆ Q) (hd : Disjoint SG SQ)
    {g : GL (Fin n) (AfE F E)} {p : DecoF E n ST SG Q} (h : IsPtOf hc g p) : IsPtOf hc g (extTo SQ p) :=
  ⟨h.uni, h.lat, h.f, h.be, fun P i j => by
    rw [h.fr ⟨P.1, hQ P.2⟩, extTo_fr, ptFr_of_mem p (Finset.disjoint_right.1 hd P.2) (hQ P.2)]⟩

variable (hc) in

theorem isPtOf_base [NeZero n] : IsPtOf hc 1 (DecoF.base E n ST SG Q) := by
  have h1 : ∀ (x : Sp E n) i, pullV (1 : GL (Fin n) (AfE F E)) x i = Formalism.UG.inclE (F := F) (FiniteAdeleRing (𝓞 F) F) (x i) :=
    fun x i => by simp [pullV]
  refine ⟨one_mem _, fun x => by simp; rfl, fun P x hx i hi => ?_, fun w x hx i => ?_, fun P i j => ?_⟩
  · replace hx : x ∈ stdLat E n := hx
    obtain ⟨r, hr⟩ := (mem_stdLat x).1 hx i
    have e : lastIdx n = i := Fin.ext (by simp [lastIdx]; omega)
    show HasRes _ _ ((Deco.base E n ST SG).f P x)
    simp only [Deco.base]
    rw [if_pos (le_locP _ _ hx), h1, adAt_inclE, e, ← hr, reduction_coe]
    exact hasRes_coe _ _
  · replace hx : x ∈ stdLat E n := hx
    obtain ⟨r, hr⟩ := (mem_stdLat x).1 hx i
    show HasRes _ _ ((Deco.base E n ST SG).be w x i)
    simp only [Deco.base]
    rw [if_pos (le_locP _ _ hx), h1, adAt_inclE, ← hr, reduction_coe]
    exact hasRes_coe _ _
  · show _ = algebraMap E _ ((Pi.single j 1 : Sp E n) i)
    rw [Units.val_one, Matrix.one_apply, Pi.single_apply]
    split_ifs <;> simp


[GCF_decorated_points_03] Literal ptIdl and detPt
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Definitions/AutomorphicMembership/ReferenceCharactersFactorTame.lean#L563-L565

[GCF_decorated_points_04] Actual K matrix, congruence determinants, valuation differences and point quotient equality
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/IwasawaDecomposition/ValDetSubOneLt.lean#L54-L241
Range SHA256: 8d179f732efb1108822ebe14c01c39554fe879ccee837420974d43bb2aecaf64
def kMat (P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) (g : GL (Fin n) (AfE F E)) (f : Sp E n ≃ₗ[E] Sp E n) :
    Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (P.adicCompletion E) := fun i j => adAt F P (pullV g (f (Pi.single j 1)) i)

theorem mul_kMat (P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) (g : GL (Fin n) (AfE F E)) (f : Sp E n ≃ₗ[E] Sp E n) :
    (adAt F P).mapMatrix (g : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (AfE F E)) * kMat P g f =
      (algebraMap E (P.adicCompletion E)).mapMatrix (LinearMap.toMatrix' (f : Sp E n →ₗ[E] Sp E n)) := by
  ext1 i j
  have e : (g : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (AfE F E)).mulVec (pullV g (f (Pi.single j 1))) =
      fun i => Formalism.UG.inclE (F := F) (FiniteAdeleRing (𝓞 F) F) (f (Pi.single j 1) i) := by
    unfold pullV
    rw [Matrix.mulVec_mulVec, ← Units.val_mul, mul_inv_cancel, Units.val_one, Matrix.one_mulVec]
  have e2 := congrArg (fun y => adAt F P (y i)) e
  simp only [adAt_inclE, Matrix.mulVec, dotProduct, map_sum, map_mul] at e2
  simp only [Matrix.mul_apply, RingHom.mapMatrix_apply, Matrix.map_apply, LinearMap.toMatrix'_apply, kMat]
  rw [e2]
  congr 2

theorem det_kMat (P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) (g : GL (Fin n) (AfE F E)) (f : Sp E n ≃ₗ[E] Sp E n) :
    adAt F P (g : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (AfE F E)).det * (kMat P g f).det = algebraMap E _ (dt f) := by
  rw [RingHom.map_det, ← Matrix.det_mul, mul_kMat, ← RingHom.map_det, LinearMap.det_toMatrix', CharValCompare.dt_eq]

theorem adAt_pullV_smul (P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) (g : GL (Fin n) (AfE F E)) (s : 𝓞 E) (x : Sp E n) (i : Fin n) :
    adAt F P (pullV g (s • x) i) = algebraMap E _ (algebraMap (𝓞 E) E s) * adAt F P (pullV g x i) := by
  simp only [pullV, Matrix.mulVec, dotProduct, map_sum, map_mul, Pi.smul_apply, Algebra.smul_def, adAt_inclE, Finset.mul_sum]
  exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun _ _ => by ring

variable [StarRing E]

theorem val_le_of_mem_hatL (P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) {y : Fin n → AfE F E}
    (hy : y ∈ Formalism.L2.Herm.hatL (F := F) (stdLat E n)) (i : Fin n) : Valued.v (adAt F P (y i)) ≤ 1 := by
  induction hy using Submodule.span_induction with
  | mem y hy =>
    obtain ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩ := hy
    obtain ⟨r, hr⟩ := (mem_stdLat x).1 hx i
    show Valued.v (adAt F P (Formalism.UG.inclE (F := F) (FiniteAdeleRing (𝓞 F) F) (x i))) ≤ 1
    rw [adAt_inclE, valued_algebraMap, ← hr]
    exact P.valuation_le_one r
  | zero => simp
  | add a b _ _ ha hb =>
    rw [Pi.add_apply, map_add]
    exact Valuation.map_add_le _ ha hb
  | smul c a _ ha =>
    show Valued.v (adAt F P (c.1 * a i)) ≤ 1
    rw [map_mul, map_mul]
    exact mul_le_one' ((mem_rInt_iff F E c.1).1 c.2 P) ha

theorem val_kMat_le {P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)} {g : GL (Fin n) (AfE F E)} {f : Sp E n ≃ₗ[E] Sp E n}
    {N : Submodule (𝓞 E) (Sp E n)} (hg : Formalism.L2.Herm.Carries g (stdLat E n) N) (hf : IsFr P N f) (i j : Fin n) :
    Valued.v (kMat P g f i j) ≤ 1 := by
  obtain ⟨s, hs, hx⟩ := (mem_locP _ _ _).1 ((hf _).2 (CharValCompare.single_mem_stdAt P j))
  have h1 : pullV g (s • f (Pi.single j 1)) ∈ Formalism.L2.Herm.hatL (F := F) (stdLat E n) :=
    (hg _).1 (Submodule.subset_span ⟨_, hx, rfl⟩)
  have h2 := val_le_of_mem_hatL P h1 i
  rwa [adAt_pullV_smul, map_mul, valued_algebraMap, CharValCompare.val_of_notMem hs, one_mul] at h2

variable {hc : Formalism.BasicNotions.CMData F E} {ST SG Q : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E))}

theorem val_kMat_sub_lt {g : GL (Fin n) (AfE F E)} {p : DecoF E n ST SG Q} (h : IsPtOf hc g p) (w : SG)
    {f : Sp E n ≃ₗ[E] Sp E n} (hf : TopStep.IsLiftFr p.deco.1 w f) (i j : Fin n) :
    Valued.v (kMat w.1 g f i j - (1 : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (w.1.adicCompletion E)) i j) < 1 := by
  obtain ⟨s, hs, hx⟩ := (mem_locP _ _ _).1 ((hf.1 _).2 (CharValCompare.single_mem_stdAt w.1 j))
  obtain ⟨r, hr, h1⟩ := h.be w _ hx i
  have e : (s • (Pi.single j 1 : Sp E n)) i = algebraMap (𝓞 E) E (if i = j then s else 0) := by
    rw [Pi.smul_apply, Pi.single_apply]
    split_ifs <;> simp [Algebra.smul_def]
  rw [← equiv_osmul, hf.2 _ (Submodule.smul_mem _ _ (CharValCompare.single_mem_stdAt w.1 j)), redV, e, reduction_coe,
    Ideal.Quotient.eq, ← HeightOneSpectrum.valuation_lt_one_iff_mem (K := E), ← valued_algebraMap] at hr
  replace hr : Valued.v (algebraMap E (w.1.adicCompletion E) (algebraMap (𝓞 E) E (r - if i = j then s else 0))) < 1 := hr
  rw [map_sub, map_sub] at hr
  rw [adAt_pullV_smul] at h1
  have h2 := Valuation.map_add_lt _ h1 hr
  rw [sub_add_sub_cancel] at h2
  have e2 : algebraMap E (w.1.adicCompletion E) (algebraMap (𝓞 E) E (if i = j then s else 0)) =
      algebraMap E (w.1.adicCompletion E) (algebraMap (𝓞 E) E s) * (1 : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (w.1.adicCompletion E)) i j := by
    rw [Matrix.one_apply]
    split_ifs <;> simp
  rwa [e2, ← mul_sub, map_mul, valued_algebraMap, CharValCompare.val_of_notMem hs, one_mul] at h2

end Loc

section Glob

variable {F E : Type} [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field E] [NumberField E] [Algebra F E] [StarRing E] {n : ℕ}
  {hc : Formalism.BasicNotions.CMData F E}

theorem compAt_eq {g : GL (Fin n) (AfE F E)} {a : Ideles E} (h : IsDetLift hc g a) (P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) :
    compAt P a = adAt F P (g : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (AfE F E)).det *
      adAt F P (Formalism.UG.sigR hc.sig _ (finAf F E a : AfE F E)) := by
  have e : finAf F E a = Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det g * conjId hc.sig (finAf F E a) := by
    rw [← h]
    exact (div_mul_cancel _ _).symm
  rw [← map_mul, ← adAt_finAf F]
  exact congrArg (fun u : (AfE F E)ˣ => adAt F P u) e

omit [StarRing E] in

theorem val_compAt_ne (P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) (a : Ideles E) : Valued.v (compAt P a) ≠ 0 :=
  (Valuation.ne_zero_iff _).2 ((Group.isUnit a).map (compAt P)).ne_zero

theorem val_compAt_eq {g : GL (Fin n) (AfE F E)} {a : Ideles E} (h : IsDetLift hc g a) {N : Submodule (𝓞 E) (Sp E n)}
    (hN : IsSdLat N) (hg : Formalism.L2.Herm.Carries g (stdLat E n) N) {fr : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E) → Sp E n ≃ₗ[E] Sp E n}
    (hf : ∀ P, IsFr P N (fr P)) (P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) :
    Valued.v (compAt P a) = P.valuation E (dt (fr P)) * Valued.v (compAt (conjPrime₂ P) a) := by
  have k : ∀ P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E), P.valuation E (dt (fr P)) * Valued.v (compAt (conjPrime₂ P) a) ≤ Valued.v (compAt P a) := by
    intro P
    rw [compAt_eq h P, map_mul, valued_sigR, adAt_finAf, ← valued_algebraMap, ← det_kMat P g, map_mul]
    exact mul_le_mul_left (mul_le_of_le_one_right' (val_det_le _ (val_kMat_le hg (hf P)))) _
  refine le_antisymm ?_ (k P)
  have k2 := k (conjPrime₂ P)
  have k3 := CharValCompare.val_dual hN (hf P) (hf (conjPrime₂ P))
  rw [map_mul, valuation_star] at k3
  rw [CharValCompare.conjP_conjP] at k2 k3
  calc Valued.v (compAt P a) = P.valuation E (dt (fr P)) * ((conjPrime₂ P).valuation E (dt (fr (conjPrime₂ P))) * Valued.v (compAt P a)) := by
        rw [← mul_assoc, k3, one_mul]
    _ ≤ _ := mul_le_mul_right k2 _

theorem val_compAt_detIdl {SB : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E))} (hSB : ∀ P ∈ SB, conjPrime₂ P ∉ SB)
    {N : Submodule (𝓞 E) (Sp E n)} (hN : IsSdLat N) {fr : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E) → Sp E n ≃ₗ[E] Sp E n}
    (hf : ∀ P, IsFr P N (fr P)) (P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) :
    Valued.v (compAt P (detIdl SB N fr)) = P.valuation E (dt (fr P)) * Valued.v (compAt (conjPrime₂ P) (detIdl SB N fr)) := by
  have k3 := CharValCompare.val_dual hN (hf P) (hf (conjPrime₂ P))
  rw [map_mul, valuation_star, CharValCompare.conjP_conjP] at k3
  rw [compAt_detIdl _ hN.1, compAt_detIdl _ hN.1, CharValCompare.conjP_conjP]
  by_cases h1 : P ∈ SB
  · rw [if_pos h1, if_neg (hSB P h1), if_pos h1, map_one, mul_one, valued_algebraMap]
  by_cases h2 : conjPrime₂ P ∈ SB
  · rw [if_neg h1, if_pos h2, if_pos h2, map_one, valued_algebraMap, k3]
  rw [if_neg h1, if_neg h2, if_neg h2, if_neg h1, map_pow, map_pow, valued_algebraMap, valued_algebraMap,
    ordSub_detSub_eq hN.1 (hf P), ordSub_detSub_eq hN.1 (hf (conjPrime₂ P))]
  have ex : ∀ (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) (u : Eˣ), w.valuation E (u : E) = WithZero.exp (-ordF w u) := fun w u => by
    unfold ordF
    rw [neg_neg, WithZero.exp_log (by simp)]
  rw [ex, ex, ← WithZero.exp_add, ← WithZero.exp_zero, WithZero.exp_inj] at k3
  rw [ex, ex, ex, ordF_unif_self, ordF_unif_self, ← WithZero.exp_nsmul, ← WithZero.exp_nsmul, ← WithZero.exp_add,
    WithZero.exp_inj]
  simp only [nsmul_eq_mul]
  omega

end Glob

attribute [local instance] Formalism.BasicNotions.Host.cmStar

theorem mk_eq_detPt (R : Ranks) (H : Formalism.BasicNotions.Host R) (n : ℕ) (ST SG Q SQ : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 H.E)))
    (p : DecoF H.E n ST SG Q) (g : GL (Fin n) (AfE H.F H.E)) (a : Ideles H.E) (hl : LevOk H SG SQ)
    (hp : IsPtOf (host_cmData R H) g (extTo SQ p)) (ha : IsDetLift (host_cmData R H) g a) :
    (QuotientGroup.mk a : DtQ H SQ) = detPt H SQ p := by
  classical
  have hN : IsSdLat p.deco.1.N := ⟨p.deco.2.lat, p.deco.2.uni⟩
  refine QuotientGroup.eq.2 (mem_KDq_of_conj hl _ (fun w => ?_) fun P hP => ?_)
  · rw [map_mul, map_mul, map_mul, map_mul, map_inv, map_inv, map_inv₀, map_inv₀,
      val_compAt_eq ha hN hp.lat (isFr_ptFr p) w, ptIdl, val_compAt_detIdl hl.split hN (isFr_ptFr p) w]
    have : w.valuation H.E (dt (ptFr p w)) ≠ 0 := (Valuation.ne_zero_iff _).2 (CharValCompare.dt_ne _)
    have := val_compAt_ne (conjPrime₂ w) a
    field_simp
  · have k1 : Valued.v ((kMat P g (ptFr p P)).det - 1) < 1 := by
      by_cases h : P ∈ SG
      · exact val_det_sub_one_lt _ (val_kMat_sub_lt hp ⟨P, h⟩ (isLiftFr_ptFr p ⟨P, h⟩))
      · have h' : P ∈ SQ := by simpa [h] using hP
        have e : (adAt H.F P).mapMatrix (g : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (AfE H.F H.E)) =
            (algebraMap H.E (P.adicCompletion H.E)).mapMatrix
              (LinearMap.toMatrix' (ptFr p P : Sp H.E n →ₗ[H.E] Sp H.E n)) := by
          ext1 i j
          refine (hp.fr ⟨P, h'⟩ i j).trans ?_
          simp only [RingHom.mapMatrix_apply, Matrix.map_apply, LinearMap.toMatrix'_apply, extTo_fr]
          congr 2
        have e2 := det_kMat P g (ptFr p P)
        rw [RingHom.map_det, e, ← RingHom.map_det, LinearMap.det_toMatrix', ← CharValCompare.dt_eq,
          mul_right_eq_self₀, map_eq_zero] at e2
        rw [e2.resolve_right (CharValCompare.dt_ne _), sub_self, map_zero]
        exact zero_lt_one
    have k2 : Valued.v (adAt H.F P (Formalism.UG.sigR (host_cmData R H).sig _ (finAf H.F H.E (ptIdl H SQ p) : AfE H.F H.E)) - 1) < 1 := by
      have e := valued_sigR (host_cmData R H) P ((finAf H.F H.E (ptIdl H SQ p) : AfE H.F H.E) - 1)
      rw [map_sub (Formalism.UG.sigR _ _), map_sub (adAt _ _), map_sub (adAt _ _), map_one, map_one, map_one, adAt_finAf] at e
      rw [e, ptIdl, compAt_detIdl _ hN.1, if_neg (hl.split P hP), CharValCompare.conjP_conjP, if_pos hP, sub_self, map_zero]
      exact zero_lt_one
    have e1 : compAt P (ptIdl H SQ p) = algebraMap H.E _ (dt (ptFr p P)) := by
      rw [ptIdl, compAt_detIdl _ hN.1, if_pos hP]
    have e2 := compAt_eq ha P
    have e3 : adAt H.F P (Formalism.UG.sigR (host_cmData R H).sig _ (finAf H.F H.E a : AfE H.F H.E)) *
        adAt H.F P (Formalism.UG.sigR (host_cmData R H).sig _ (finAf H.F H.E (a⁻¹ * ptIdl H SQ p) : AfE H.F H.E)) =
        adAt H.F P (Formalism.UG.sigR (host_cmData R H).sig _ (finAf H.F H.E (ptIdl H SQ p) : AfE H.F H.E)) := by
      rw [← map_mul, ← map_mul, ← Units.val_mul, ← map_mul, mul_inv_cancel_left]
    have h0 : compAt P a ≠ 0 := ((Group.isUnit a).map (compAt P)).ne_zero
    have k3 := val_mul_sub_one_lt _ k1 (val_inv_sub_one_lt _ k2)
    rw [e2] at h0
    rw [map_mul (compAt P), map_inv, e1, ← det_kMat P g, e2]
    rw [← e3] at k3
    convert k3 using 3


[GCF_decorated_points_05] Exact same-class pointDeterminant_eq_normOneClass_of_level endpoint
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/IwasawaDecomposition/PointDeterminantEqNormOneClassOfLevel.lean#L13-L50

theorem adAt_eq_one_of_prV {F E : Type} [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field E] [NumberField E] [Algebra F E]
    (P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) {z : AfE F E}
    (hz : Formalism.UG.prV (E := E) (P.under (𝓞 F)) z = 1) : adAt F P z = 1 := by
  set v := P.under (𝓞 F)
  have h1 : Formalism.UG.P.afsplitEps E v * z = Formalism.UG.P.afsplitEps E v := by
    rw [← Formalism.UG.P.afsplit_decomp, hz]
    rfl
  have h2 : Formalism.UG.P.afsplitEps E v = (1 : E) ⊗ₜ[F] Formalism.UG.P.afsplitI v (1 : v.adicCompletion F) := by
    rw [Formalism.UG.P.afsplitEps, Algebra.TensorProduct.one_def, Formalism.UG.P.afsplitIE_tmul]
  have h3 : adAt F P (Formalism.UG.P.afsplitEps E v) = 1 := by
    rw [h2, adAt_apply, GlobalTheory.Mem.ReadTwist.afIso_one_tmul (k := 1)]
    · simp
    · rw [Formalism.UG.P.afsplitI_self]; simp
  have h4 := congrArg (adAt F P) h1
  rwa [map_mul, h3, one_mul] at h4

theorem detPt_eq_u1Dt_of_level : ∀ (R : Ranks) (H : Formalism.BasicNotions.Host R) (n : ℕ) (ST SG Q SQ : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 H.E))) (p : DecoF H.E n ST SG Q) (y k : GL (Fin n) (AfE H.F H.E)) (x : Formalism.L2.Herm.unitaryOneFiniteAdeles (host_cmData R H)), LevOk H SG SQ → IsPtOf (host_cmData R H) (y * k) (extTo SQ p) → k ∈ Formalism.L2.Herm.UstdAf (host_cmData R H) n → Formalism.L2.Herm.Carries k (stdLat H.E n) (stdLat H.E n) → (∀ P ∈ uSG SG SQ, Formalism.UG.bc (Formalism.UG.prV (E := H.E) (P.under (𝓞 H.F))) k = 1) → Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det x.1 = Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det y → detPt H SQ p = u1Dt H SQ x := by
  intro R H n ST SG Q SQ p y k x hl hp hk hc hbc hx
  obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := exists_lift H x
  obtain ⟨b, hb⟩ := DetLift.exists_isDetLift R H n k hk
  have hab : IsDetLift (host_cmData R H) (y * k) (a * b) := by
    unfold IsDetLift at ha hb ⊢
    rw [map_mul, map_mul, ha, hb, hx, map_mul]
  have hK : b ∈ KDq H SQ := by
    refine mem_KDq_of_conj hl b (fun w => ?_) fun P hP => ?_
    · rw [DetSpec.val_compAt_eq hb ⟨stdLat_lattice, stdLat_unimodular⟩ hc (fr := fun _ => LinearEquiv.refl _ _)
        (fun _ _ => Iff.rfl) w]
      simp
    · have hdet : adAt H.F P (k : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (AfE H.F H.E)).det = 1 := by
        refine adAt_eq_one_of_prV P ?_
        have h := congrArg (fun g => Matrix.det (Units.val g)) (hbc P hP)
        simp only [Formalism.UG.bc, Units.coe_map, Units.val_one, Matrix.det_one] at h
        rw [RingHom.map_det]
        exact h
      have hs0 : adAt H.F P (Formalism.UG.sigR (host_cmData R H).sig _ (finAf H.F H.E b : AfE H.F H.E)) ≠ 0 :=
        (((Units.isUnit (finAf H.F H.E b)).map (Formalism.UG.sigR (host_cmData R H).sig _)).map (adAt H.F P)).ne_zero
      rw [DetSpec.compAt_eq hb P, hdet, one_mul, div_self hs0, sub_self, map_zero]
      exact zero_lt_one


[GCF_decorated_points_06] Actual selected primes, absence of extra auxiliary primes, LevOk and disjointness
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/IwasawaDecomposition/LevOkTwists.lean#L21-L85
Range SHA256: e6c8ec42836c3c7cbaaf05b6006c757d3c9df6bc3f6241154a2cc401d2ebd563
theorem under_sel (h : I.Ok) (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 B.host.F)) : (I.sel v).under (𝓞 B.host.F) = v :=
  HeightOneSpectrum.ext (h.sel_ok v)

theorem good_of_mem (h : I.Ok) {P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 B.host.E)} (hP : P ∈ I.SQ) :
    I.sel (P.under (𝓞 B.host.F)) = P ∧ GoodPl B.host.E I.S (P.under (𝓞 B.host.F)) := by
  obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := I.mem_SQ.1 hP
  obtain ⟨v, -, rfl, hg⟩ := I.exists_of_mem_Q ha
  rw [under_sel h]
  exact ⟨rfl, hg⟩

theorem plc_mem (h : I.Ok) {P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 B.host.E)} (hP : P ∈ (B.primes X).vGs) :
    Formalism.EigenvalueLevelCeiling.plc (P.under (𝓞 B.host.F)) ∈ I.S := by
  classical
  have hPr := (GlobalTheory.BasePrimes.primes_ok B X).1
  have h0 : ∀ i, B.fields.conjO ((B.primes X).piG i) ≠ 0 := fun i h => by
    have := hPr.piG_norm i
    rw [h, mul_zero] at this
    exact (hPr.g_prime i).ne_zero (Nat.cast_eq_zero.1 this.symm)
  have hm : ∃ i, B.fields.conjO ((B.primes X).piG i) ∈ P.asIdeal := by
    unfold Primes.vGs Primes.vG at hP
    simpa only [Finset.mem_union, mem_divs (h0 _), Fin.exists_fin_two] using hP
  obtain ⟨i, hi⟩ := hm
  have h1 : ((B.primes X).g i : 𝓞 B.fields.E) ∈ P.asIdeal := by
    rw [← hPr.piG_norm i]; exact Ideal.mul_mem_left _ _ hi
  rw [(ReadTwist.plc_under_eq_iff (F := B.host.F) P (hPr.g_prime i)).2 h1]
  exact h.g_mem i

theorem levOk_twists : ∀ (B : Base) (X : PreX B.toPre) (I : ColIn B X), I.Ok → LevOk B.host (B.primes X).vGs I.SQ ∧ Disjoint (B.primes X).vGs I.SQ ∧ ∀ a, KDq B.host (I.Q a) ≤ ((I.xi a).1.comp (finPartI B.host.E)).ker ∧ (I.xi a).IsUnitary := by
  classical
  intro B X I h
  have hb := levOk_base B X
  have hu : ∀ P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 B.host.E), (conjPrime₂ P).under (𝓞 B.host.F) = P.under (𝓞 B.host.F) := fun P =>
    (under_eq_iff B.host P _).2 (Or.inr rfl)
  refine ⟨⟨hb.cover, fun P hP hP' => ?_⟩, Finset.disjoint_left.2 fun P h1 h2 => (good_of_mem h h2).2.2.2 (plc_mem h h1),
    fun a => ?_⟩
  · simp only [Finset.mem_union] at hP hP'
    rcases hP with hP | hP <;> rcases hP' with hP' | hP'
    · exact hb.split P (by simpa using hP) (by simpa using hP')
    · refine (good_of_mem h hP').2.2.2 ?_
      rw [hu]
      exact plc_mem h hP
    · refine (good_of_mem h hP).2.2.2 ?_
      rw [← hu]
      exact plc_mem h hP'
    · have e := (good_of_mem h hP').1
      rw [hu, (good_of_mem h hP).1] at e
      exact conjP_ne B.host ((GoodPlace.goodPl_under_iff _ B.host _ P).1 (good_of_mem h hP).2).1 e.symm
  · obtain ⟨⟨⟨n, hn, ho⟩, hT, hta, -⟩, hq, -⟩ :=
      ReadTwist.isTwist_of_reads B.R B.host B.LL B.embLL I.S (I.tup a) (I.xi a) (h.xi_reads a)
    refine ⟨KDq_le_ker_of_tame _ hT hta fun w hw => by_contra fun hr => hw (Or.inr ?_), fun x => ?_⟩
    · have hfin : (Qprime (I.tup a : Chr B.fields.F B.fields.E B.fields.K)).Finite :=
        ((UnrAe.finite_ramified (I.xi a)).image (·.under (𝓞 B.host.F))).subset fun v hv => by
          obtain ⟨P, e, hP⟩ := (hq v).1 hv
          exact ⟨P, hP, e⟩
      have hmem : I.sel (w.under (𝓞 B.host.F)) ∈ I.Q a := by
        unfold ColIn.Q QE
        rw [dif_pos hfin]
        convert Finset.mem_image_of_mem I.sel (hfin.mem_toFinset.2 ((hq _).2 ⟨w, rfl, hr⟩))
      rcases (under_eq_iff B.host _ w).1 (under_sel h _).symm with e | e
      · exact Or.inl (e ▸ hmem)
      · exact Or.inr ⟨_, hmem, (isConjPl_iff B.host _ _).2 e⟩
    · have h1 : (I.xi a).1 x ^ n = 1 := congrArg (fun χ : HChar B.host.E => χ.1 x) ho
      have h2 : ‖(((I.xi a).1 x : ℂˣ) : ℂ)‖ ^ n = 1 := by
        rw [← norm_pow, ← Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, h1, Units.val_one, norm_one]
      exact (pow_eq_one_iff_of_nonneg (norm_nonneg _) hn.ne').1 h2


[GCF_full_hermitian_01] All eight SymmetricHermitianColumns fields, relative law and every FullTiedS field
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/LocalToGlobal/HermitianColumns/FullSym.lean#L14-L77
Range SHA256: ad90b31bb1cbe5ec1ce1b7281c6ff0ea86eca3f8b176b4db62cc4ac4cd2053ee
structure SymmetricHermitianColumns (D : D7) extends HermColsCh D where

  dt : unitaryOneFiniteAdeles D.sp.cm →* D.Dt
  dt_surj : Function.Surjective dt

  dt_ker : ∀ x : unitaryOneFiniteAdeles D.sp.cm, dt x = 1 → (x : GL (Fin 1) (D.host.E ⊗[D.host.F] Af D.host.F)) ∈ IntegralUnitGroup D.sp.cm

  dt_open : letI := tensTopL (F := D.host.F) (E := D.host.E) (Af D.host.F)
    IsOpen {x : unitaryOneFiniteAdeles D.sp.cm | dt x = 1}

  dt_UO : D.UO = ((IntegralUnitGroup D.sp.cm).subgroupOf (unitaryOneFiniteAdeles D.sp.cm)).map dt

  dt_rat : ∀ (z : unitary D.host.E) (x : unitaryOneFiniteAdeles D.sp.cm),
    Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det (x : GL (Fin 1) (D.host.E ⊗[D.host.F] Af D.host.F)) = unitaryOneRationalAdele D.host.F z →
    D.e1Dt z = dt x

  det0 : ∀ (u : D.what₂.Word) (c : (D.mem u).C.cls 0) (x : unitaryOneFiniteAdeles D.sp.cm),
    Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det (x : GL (Fin 1) (D.host.E ⊗[D.host.F] Af D.host.F)) =
      Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det (((lev0 u).y c : GL (Fin D.R.k) (D.host.E ⊗[D.host.F] Af D.host.F))) →
    D.detCls u 0 c = dt x

  detI : ∀ (u : D.what₂.Word) (i : ℕ) (h1 : 1 ≤ i) (hm : i ≤ D.R.m) (c : (D.mem u).C.cls i) (x : unitaryOneFiniteAdeles D.sp.cm),
    Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det (x : GL (Fin 1) (D.host.E ⊗[D.host.F] Af D.host.F)) =
      Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det
        (((cls u i h1 hm (chOf u i c)).y ⟨c, rfl⟩ : GL (Fin (D.sp.X i).rank) (D.host.E ⊗[D.host.F] Af D.host.F))) →
    D.detCls u i c = dt x

def SymmetricGroupCompatibility (D : D7) (H : HermColsCh D) : Type :=
  ∀ u (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)),
    GLevTie (H.lev0 u) (D.gat u w hg)

def SymmetricRelativeDeterminant (D : D7) (H : SymmetricHermitianColumns D) : Prop :=
  ∀ (N : ℕ) (L L' : SDLat D.host.E N), ∀ g ∈ UstdAf D.sp.cm N, Carries g L.1 L'.1 →
    ∀ x : unitaryOneFiniteAdeles D.sp.cm, IsDetOf D.sp.cm x g → D.relDet N L L' = QuotientGroup.mk (H.dt x)

structure SymmetricCompatibleData (D : D7) where
  base : SymmetricHermitianColumns D
  atT : ∀ u, AtT D u (base.vT u) (base.Fv u)
  vol : VolumeCompatible D

structure FullTiedS (D : D7) extends SymmetricCompatibleData D where
  levelZeroCompatibility : SymmetricLevelZeroData D base.toHermColsCh atT
  g : SymmetricGroupCompatibility D base.toHermColsCh
  ad : SymmetricAdelicCompatibility D base.toHermColsCh atT
  cof : SymmetricCofactorCompatibility D base.toHermColsCh atT
  piG : PiGConstS D base.toHermColsCh atT levelZeroCompatibility
  stabMap : SymmetricStabilizerMapCompatibility D base.toHermColsCh atT
  fvI : SymmetricResidueFieldLevels D base.toHermColsCh atT
  relDet : SymmetricRelativeDeterminant D base
  std : StdTied D
  vpos : PositiveVolumeCompatible D
  baseL : SymmetricBaseCompatibility D base.toHermColsCh atT

def FullTiedS.cols {D : D7} (X : FullTiedS D) : ColsHermS D where
  base := X.base.toHermColsCh
  atT := X.atT
  levelZeroCompatibility := X.levelZeroCompatibility
  ad := X.ad
  cof := X.cof
  piG := X.piG
  stabMap := X.stabMap
  fvI := X.fvI
  std := X.std
  baseL := X.baseL


[GCF_full_hermitian_02] Exact local prime and kernel equalities in symmetric extra compatibility
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/LocalToGlobal/HermitianColumns/FullSym.lean#L101-L130

def SymmetricGroupCompatibleAtPlaces (D : D7) (H : HermColsCh D) (G : SymmetricGroupCompatibility D H) (Gt : GatTies D) : Prop :=
  ∀ u w hg (v : Fin (D.gat u w hg).P.nV) (s : Fin ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v)) (x : 𝓞 D.host.F),
    x ∈ ((G u w hg).pl v).1.asIdeal ↔
      (0 : WithTop ℤ) < D.what₂.places.val w
        (tauLL D (Gt.gat_pos u w hg ⟨v, s⟩) (algebraMap D.host.F D.host.E (x : D.host.F)))

def SymmetricKernelCompatibleAtPlace {D : D7} (H : HermColsCh D) (G : SymmetricGroupCompatibility D H) {Gt : GatTies D} {c : LatCmp D} {Tw : TwForms D}
    (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) [Fact (D.what₂.places.resChar w).Prime]
    (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) {Fl : Formalism.MainNotions.G.Fills (D.gat u w hg)}
    {Lv : Formalism.MainNotions.G.Levels Fl} {Ch : Formalism.MainNotions.G.Chain Lv} (Kp : LocalToGlobal₂ D Gt c Tw u w hg Ch) : Prop :=
  ∀ (v : Fin (D.gat u w hg).P.nV) (x : D.host.E),
    (G u w hg).eQ v _ rfl (Kp.iw 0 v x) =
      ((G u w hg).spl _ ((G u w hg).pl v).2
        (inclE (F := D.host.F) (((G u w hg).pl v).1.adicCompletion D.host.F) x)).1

structure JoinTiedS {D : D7} (X : FullTiedS D) (lev : LevelData D) (eX : ErrX D) (Gt : GatTies D) : Prop where
  lev_eq : lev = X.base.lev0
  errX_eq : eX = toErrXS X
  gpl : SymmetricGroupCompatibleAtPlaces D X.base.toHermColsCh X.g Gt

structure SymmetricExtraCompatibility {D : D7} (X : FullTiedS D) {L : SizeData₂ D} {hT : GatTies D} {Gi : Formalism.MainNotions.G.Inst D L}
    {Gl : Formalism.MainNotions.G.LatInst D} {K : Formalism.MainNotions.G.Consts} {Y : DivX D} {cX : ExtraC}
    (ex : Extra D L hT Gi Gl K Y cX) : Prop where
  join : JoinTiedS X ex.lev ex.errX hT
  iw : ∀ u w (h : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)),
    haveI : Fact (D.what₂.places.resChar w).Prime := ⟨D.what₂.places.resChar_prime w⟩
    SymmetricKernelCompatibleAtPlace X.base.toHermColsCh X.g u w h (ex.ker u w h)
  iw_pos : ∀ u w (h : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)),
    haveI : Fact (D.what₂.places.resChar w).Prime := ⟨D.what₂.places.resChar_prime w⟩
    GIwPos u w h (ex.ker u w h)

[GCF_full_hermitian_05] Actual endpoint/middle/terminal determinant readings and prescribed-g full extension
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/IwasawaDecomposition/ExistsFullHermitianColumnsTrueColumns.lean#L60-L159
Range SHA256: a50de39794c7e921e1c66b1e0eb0488e2f0db0b978eb3042d1cb3553992837cd
theorem detPt_of_reads (hI : I.Ok) (u : B.Word) (i : ℕ) {Ch : Type} {chOf : (I.stp u I.mu0 i).Cl → Ch}
    {Lb : Ch → Submodule (𝓞 B.host.E) (Fin (B.R.N i) → B.host.E)} {U : Ch → Subgroup (GA B (B.R.N i))} {chi : ∀ β, U β →* ℂˣ}
    {y : (I.stp u I.mu0 i).Cl → GA B (B.R.N i)} {st : ∀ c, (I.stp u I.mu0 i).Stab c → GL (Fin (B.R.N i)) B.host.E}
    {k : Ch → GA B (B.R.N i)} (hR : I.Reads u I.mu0 i chOf Lb U chi y st k) (c : (I.stp u I.mu0 i).Cl)
    (x : Formalism.L2.Herm.unitaryOneFiniteAdeles (host_cmData B.R B.host))
    (hx : Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det x.1 = Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det (y c)) :
    detPt B.host I.SQ ((I.stp u I.mu0 i).rep c).1.1 = u1Dt B.host I.SQ x := by
  obtain ⟨p, hp, hN⟩ := hR.k_std (chOf c)
  refine GlobalTheory.T.pointDeterminant_eq_normOneClass_of_level B.R B.host _ _ _ _ I.SQ _ (y c) (k (chOf c)) x (GlobalTheory.Mem.LevTwist.levOk_twists B X I hI).1 (hR.pt c) hp.uni
    (hN ▸ hp.lat) (fun P hP => ?_) hx
  refine hR.k_off _ _ (GlobalTheory.T.memberLevelPrime_not_mem_under_levelPrimes B X I hI u P hP) ?_ (Or.inl rfl)
  rintro ⟨Q, hQ, -⟩
  rw [GlobalTheory.T.q_mu0_eq_empty B X I] at hQ
  exact absurd hQ (Finset.notMem_empty Q)

theorem det_reGL {a b : ℕ} (h : a = b) {A : Type} [CommRing A] (g : GL (Fin a) A) :
    Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det (reGL h A g) = Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det g := by
  subst h
  refine Units.ext ?_
  simp [reGL, Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det]

variable (l : I.Laws) (Z : TC.Later B (I.cols l)) (C : ColsTiedSym (I.rec7 l Z))

include I in

theorem det_normOne_classStepZero_trueColumns (hI : I.Ok) (hS : I.StdC l Z C) (u : B.Word) (c : ((I.rec7 l Z).mem u).C.cls 0)
    (x : Formalism.L2.Herm.unitaryOneFiniteAdeles (host_cmData B.R B.host))
    (hx : Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det x.1 = Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det ((C.herm.base.lev0 u).y c).1) :
    (I.rec7 l Z).detCls u 0 c = u1Dt B.host I.SQ x := by
  obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := (hS u).ends 0 (Nat.zero_le _) (Or.inl rfl)
  refine detPt_of_reads I hI u 0 hk c x ?_
  have e := (C.car0 u).y_eq c
  rw [hx]
  exact (e ▸ det_reGL _ _).symm

include I in

theorem detI_std (hI : I.Ok) (hS : I.StdC l Z C) (u : B.Word) (i : ℕ) (h1 : 1 ≤ i) (hm : i ≤ B.R.m)
    (c : ((I.rec7 l Z).mem u).C.cls i) (x : Formalism.L2.Herm.unitaryOneFiniteAdeles (host_cmData B.R B.host))
    (hx : Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det x.1 = Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det
      ((C.herm.base.cls u i h1 hm (C.herm.base.chOf u i c)).y ⟨c, rfl⟩).1) :
    (I.rec7 l Z).detCls u i c = u1Dt B.host I.SQ x := by
  by_cases h2 : i + 1 ≤ B.R.m
  · obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := (hS u).mid i h1 h2
    refine detPt_of_reads I hI u i hk c x ?_
    have e := (C.carC u i h1 h2 (C.herm.base.chOf u i c)).y_eq ⟨c, rfl⟩
    rw [hx]
    exact (e ▸ det_reGL _ _).symm
  · obtain rfl : i = B.R.m := by omega
    obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := (hS u).ends B.R.m le_rfl (Or.inr rfl)
    refine detPt_of_reads I hI u _ hk c x ?_
    have e := (C.carM u).y_eq c
    have key : ∀ (β : C.herm.base.Ch u B.R.m) (hb : C.herm.base.chOf u B.R.m c = β),
        Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det ((C.herm.base.cls u B.R.m h1 hm β).y ⟨c, hb⟩).1 =
          Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det ((C.herm.base.cls u B.R.m h1 hm (C.herm.base.chOf u B.R.m c)).y ⟨c, rfl⟩).1 := by
      rintro _ rfl
      rfl
    rw [hx, ← key _ (chOf_m C.herm.base u c)]
    exact (e ▸ det_reGL _ _).symm

def hermS (hI : I.Ok) (hS : I.StdC l Z C) : Formalism.L2.Herm.SymmetricHermitianColumns (I.rec7 l Z) :=
  { C.herm.base with
    dt := u1Dt B.host I.SQ
    dt_surj := normOneFiniteAdeleDetClass_surjective B.host I.SQ
    dt_ker := mem_integralNormOne_of_normOneDetClass_eq_one B.host I.SQ
    dt_open := GlobalTheory.T.isOpen_ker_normOneFiniteAdeleDetClass B.R B.host _ I.SQ (GlobalTheory.Mem.LevTwist.levOk_twists B X I hI).1
    dt_UO := unitIdeleImage_eq_map_integralNormOne B.host I.SQ
    dt_rat := fun z x h => GlobalTheory.T.rationalNormOne_class_eq_finiteAdelic_det_class B.R B.host I.SQ z x h
    det0 := fun u c x h => det_normOne_classStepZero_trueColumns I l Z C hI hS u c x h
    detI := fun u i h1 hm c x h => detI_std I l Z C hI hS u i h1 hm c x h }

theorem relDet_std (hI : I.Ok) (hS : I.StdC l Z C) : Formalism.L2.Herm.SymmetricRelativeDeterminant (I.rec7 l Z) (hermS I l Z C hI hS) := by
  intro N L L' g _ hg x hx
  obtain ⟨idl, hp⟩ := GlobalTheory.Mem.NormOneIdeal.pinned_of_finer (I.rec7 l Z) I.SQ (MulEquiv.refl _)
    (by ext t; exact ⟨fun h => ⟨t, h, rfl⟩, fun ⟨_, h, e⟩ => e ▸ h⟩) (fun _ => rfl)
    (fun N L L' t h => h)
  exact GlobalTheory.Mem.NormOneIdeal.relDet_eq_of_pinned (I.rec7 l Z) idl hp _ (hermS I l Z C hI hS).dt (hermS I l Z C hI hS).dt_ker
    (hermS I l Z C hI hS).dt_UO (hermS I l Z C hI hS).dt_rat L L' g x hg hx

theorem exists_colsFullS : ∀ {B : GlobalTheory.Mem.Base} {X : PreX B.toPre} (I : TC B X), I.Ok → TC.Pins B X → ∀ (l : I.Laws) (Z : TC.Later B (I.cols l)), ∀ (C : ColsTiedSym (I.rec7 l Z)) (g : Formalism.L2.Herm.SymmetricGroupCompatibility (I.rec7 l Z) C.herm.base), I.StdC l Z C → Formalism.L2.Herm.VolumeCompatible (I.rec7 l Z) → ∃ Fu : ColsFullS C, HEq Fu.1.g g ∧ I.StdDt l Z Fu.1.base.dt := by
  intro B X I hI _ l Z C g hS hV
  let Fu : Formalism.L2.Herm.FullTiedS (I.rec7 l Z) :=
    { base := hermS I l Z C hI hS
      atT := C.herm.atT
      vol := hV
      levelZeroCompatibility := C.herm.levelZeroCompatibility
      g := g
      ad := C.herm.ad
      cof := C.herm.cof
      piG := C.herm.piG
      stabMap := C.herm.stabMap
      fvI := C.herm.fvI
      relDet := relDet_std I l Z C hI hS
      std := C.herm.std
      vpos := B.V_pos
      baseL := C.herm.baseL }
  refine ⟨⟨Fu, rfl⟩, HEq.rfl, ?_⟩
  intro x a ha
  exact normOneFiniteAdeleDetClass_of_isDetLift B.host I.SQ x a ha
