[GCF_torsion_01] Finite CM torsion action, cubic/quartic identities and three-torsion pair
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/IwasawaDecomposition/ExistsOrderThree.lean#L18-L130
Range SHA256: aaa9c55d8d9de9df1f35504e159f4370d69fb0b6e5da8ac741877061cc9fe795
theorem exists_action (L : PeriodPair) (σ : ℂ ≃+* ℂ) (h2 : σ (L.g₂ ^ 3) = L.g₂ ^ 3) (h3 : σ (L.g₃ ^ 2) = L.g₃ ^ 2) :
    ∃ η : ℂ, σ L.g₂ = η ^ 2 * L.g₂ ∧ σ L.g₃ = η ^ 3 * L.g₃ ∧ ∀ t, ∃ t', ∀ c : ℂ, σ c = c →
      (∀ l ∈ L.lattice, c * l ∈ L.lattice) → (c * t ∈ L.lattice ↔ c * t' ∈ L.lattice) ∧
        (c * t ∉ L.lattice → σ (℘[L] (c * t)) = η * ℘[L] (c * t')) := by
  rw [map_pow] at h2 h3
  obtain ⟨u, hu, hu2⟩ : ∃ u : ℂ, u ^ 3 = 1 ∧ σ L.g₂ = u * L.g₂ := by
    by_cases h0 : L.g₂ = 0
    · exact ⟨1, one_pow _, by simp [h0]⟩
    · exact ⟨σ L.g₂ / L.g₂, by rw [div_pow, h2, div_self (pow_ne_zero _ h0)], by field_simp⟩
  obtain ⟨η, hη, e2, e3⟩ : ∃ η : ℂ, η ≠ 0 ∧ σ L.g₂ = η ^ 2 * L.g₂ ∧ σ L.g₃ = η ^ 3 * L.g₃ := by
    have hu0 : u ≠ 0 := by rintro rfl; simp at hu
    rcases sq_eq_sq_iff_eq_or_eq_neg.1 h3 with e | e
    · exact ⟨u ^ 2, pow_ne_zero _ hu0, by rw [hu2]; linear_combination -u * L.g₂ * hu,
        by rw [e]; linear_combination -(u ^ 3 + 1) * L.g₃ * hu⟩
    · exact ⟨-u ^ 2, neg_ne_zero.2 (pow_ne_zero _ hu0), by rw [hu2]; linear_combination -u * L.g₂ * hu,
        by rw [e]; linear_combination (u ^ 3 + 1) * L.g₃ * hu⟩
  obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := IsAlgClosed.exists_pow_nat_eq η⁻¹ (n := 2) (by norm_num)
  have hm0 : m ≠ 0 := by
    rintro rfl
    exact hη (by simpa using hm.symm)
  have hmu : (m ^ 2)⁻¹ = η := by rw [hm, inv_inv]
  have H := isHomothetic_scalePeriods L m hm0
  generalize scalePeriods L m hm0 = L' at H
  have f2 : L'.g₂ = σ L.g₂ := by rw [H.g₂_eq, e2, ← hmu]; ring
  have f3 : L'.g₃ = σ L.g₃ := by rw [H.g₃_eq, e3, ← hmu]; ring
  refine ⟨η, e2, e3, fun t => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨s, -, hc⟩ := exists_conjEquiv f2 f3
  obtain ⟨w, hw⟩ := latticePoint_surjective L' (s (latticePoint L t))
  obtain ⟨t', rfl⟩ : ∃ t', w = m * t' := ⟨m⁻¹ * w, by field_simp⟩
  refine ⟨t', fun c hσ hc' => ?_⟩
  have := IsConj.mul f2 f3 hc' ((hc _ _).2 hw.symm)
  rw [hσ, mul_left_comm] at this
  rcases this with ⟨a, b⟩ | ⟨a, b, e, -⟩
  · exact ⟨iff_of_true a ((H _).1 b), fun h => absurd a h⟩
  · exact ⟨iff_of_false a fun h => b ((H _).2 h), fun _ => by rw [← e, H.weierstrassP_mul, hmu]⟩

theorem cubic_eq_zero (L : PeriodPair) {t : ℂ} (ht : t ∉ L.lattice) (h : t + t ∈ L.lattice) :
    4 * ℘[L] t ^ 3 - L.g₂ * ℘[L] t - L.g₃ = 0 := by
  rw [← L.derivWeierstrassP_sq t ht, (derivWeierstrassP_eq_zero_iff L ht).2 h]; simp

theorem quartic_eq_zero (L : PeriodPair) {t : ℂ} (ht : t ∉ L.lattice) (h : 3 * t ∈ L.lattice) :
    3 * ℘[L] t ^ 4 - 3 / 2 * L.g₂ * ℘[L] t ^ 2 - 3 * L.g₃ * ℘[L] t - L.g₂ ^ 2 / 16 = 0 := by
  have h0 : 3 • latticePoint L t = 0 := by
    rw [← latticePointHom_apply, ← map_nsmul, latticePointHom_apply, latticePoint_eq_zero_iff]
    simpa using h
  rw [latticePoint_of_notMem L ht, three_nsmul_eq_zero_iff] at h0
  simp only [WeierstrassCurve.Ψ₃, WeierstrassCurve.b₂, WeierstrassCurve.b₄, WeierstrassCurve.b₆,
    WeierstrassCurve.b₈, latticeCurve, Polynomial.eval_add, Polynomial.eval_mul, Polynomial.eval_pow,
    Polynomial.eval_C, Polynomial.eval_X, Polynomial.eval_ofNat] at h0
  linear_combination h0

theorem cubic_discr {G T x y : ℂ} (hx : 4 * x ^ 3 - G * x - T = 0) (hy : 4 * y ^ 3 - G * y - T = 0) (hxy : x ≠ y) :
    (4 * ((x - y) * (2 * x + y) * (x + 2 * y))) ^ 2 = G ^ 3 - 27 * T ^ 2 := by
  have h1 : G = 4 * (x ^ 2 + x * y + y ^ 2) :=
    mul_left_cancel₀ (sub_ne_zero.2 hxy) (by linear_combination hy - hx)
  have h2 : T = 4 * x ^ 3 - G * x := by linear_combination -hx
  rw [h2, h1]; ring

theorem quartic_discr {G T x y : ℂ} (hx : 3 * x ^ 4 - 3 / 2 * G * x ^ 2 - 3 * T * x - G ^ 2 / 16 = 0)
    (hy : 3 * y ^ 4 - 3 / 2 * G * y ^ 2 - 3 * T * y - G ^ 2 / 16 = 0) (hxy : x ≠ y) :
    (G - 3 * (x + y) ^ 2) ^ 3 = G ^ 3 - 27 * T ^ 2 := by
  have h1 : T = (x + y) * (x ^ 2 + y ^ 2) - G * (x + y) / 2 :=
    mul_left_cancel₀ (sub_ne_zero.2 hxy) (by linear_combination (hy - hx) / 3)
  have h2 : -3 * (x * y) * ((x + y) ^ 2 - x * y) + 3 / 2 * G * (x * y) - G ^ 2 / 16 = 0 :=
    mul_left_cancel₀ (sub_ne_zero.2 hxy) (by linear_combination x * hy - y * hx)
  rw [h1]
  linear_combination 36 * (x + y) ^ 2 * h2

theorem quartic_sum {A B C x y z w : ℂ} (hx : 3 * x ^ 4 - A * x ^ 2 - B * x - C = 0)
    (hy : 3 * y ^ 4 - A * y ^ 2 - B * y - C = 0) (hz : 3 * z ^ 4 - A * z ^ 2 - B * z - C = 0)
    (hw : 3 * w ^ 4 - A * w ^ 2 - B * w - C = 0) (hxy : x ≠ y) (hxz : x ≠ z) (hxw : x ≠ w) (hyz : y ≠ z)
    (hyw : y ≠ w) (hzw : z ≠ w) : z + w = -(x + y) := by
  have k : ∀ u, 3 * u ^ 4 - A * u ^ 2 - B * u - C = 0 → x ≠ u →
      3 * (x ^ 3 + x ^ 2 * u + x * u ^ 2 + u ^ 3) - A * (x + u) - B = 0 := fun u hu h =>
    mul_left_cancel₀ (sub_ne_zero.2 h) (by linear_combination hx - hu)
  have k' : ∀ u, 3 * u ^ 4 - A * u ^ 2 - B * u - C = 0 → x ≠ u → y ≠ u →
      3 * (x ^ 2 + x * (y + u) + (y ^ 2 + y * u + u ^ 2)) - A = 0 := fun u hu h h' =>
    mul_left_cancel₀ (sub_ne_zero.2 h') (by linear_combination k y hy hxy - k u hu h)
  exact mul_left_cancel₀ (sub_ne_zero.2 hzw) (by linear_combination (k' z hz hxz hyz - k' w hw hxw hyw) / 3)

theorem exists_order_three (L : PeriodPair) {β : ℂ} {d : ℕ} (hd : 0 < d) (hβ : β ^ 2 = -d)
    (hL : ∀ l ∈ L.lattice, β * l ∈ L.lattice) :
    ∃ t, 3 * t ∈ L.lattice ∧ (β - 1) * t ∉ L.lattice ∧ (β + 1) * t ∉ L.lattice := by
  have uniq : ∀ a b : ℤ, (a : ℂ) * L.ω₁ + b * L.ω₂ = 0 → a = 0 ∧ b = 0 := fun a b h => by
    have := LinearIndependent.pair_iff.1 L.indep (a : ℝ) (b : ℝ) (by simpa using h)
    exact_mod_cast this
  obtain ⟨a, b, h1⟩ := mem_lattice.1 (hL _ L.ω₁_mem_lattice)
  obtain ⟨c, e, h2⟩ := mem_lattice.1 (hL _ L.ω₂_mem_lattice)
  obtain ⟨q1, q2⟩ := uniq (a ^ 2 + b * c + d) (b * (a + e)) (by
    push_cast
    linear_combination (a + β) * h1 + b * h2 + L.ω₁ * hβ)
  have hb : b ≠ 0 := by rintro rfl; nlinarith [sq_nonneg a]
  obtain rfl : e = -a := by have := (mul_eq_zero.1 q2).resolve_left hb; omega
  have key : ∀ x y ε : ℤ, (β - ε) * ((x * L.ω₁ + y * L.ω₂) / 3) ∈ L.lattice →
      (x * a + y * c - ε * x) % 3 = 0 ∧ (x * b - y * a - ε * y) % 3 = 0 := fun x y ε h => by
    obtain ⟨m, n, h⟩ := mem_lattice.1 h
    obtain ⟨r1, r2⟩ := uniq (x * a + y * c - ε * x - 3 * m) (x * b - y * a - ε * y - 3 * n) (by
      push_cast at h2 ⊢
      linear_combination x * h1 + y * h2 - 3 * h)
    omega
  by_contra hcon
  have k : ∀ x y : ℤ, ((x * a + y * c - 1 * x) % 3 = 0 ∧ (x * b - y * a - 1 * y) % 3 = 0) ∨
      ((x * a + y * c - (-1) * x) % 3 = 0 ∧ (x * b - y * a - (-1) * y) % 3 = 0) := fun x y => by
    by_contra h
    rw [not_or] at h
    refine hcon ⟨(x * L.ω₁ + y * L.ω₂) / 3, mem_lattice.2 ⟨x, y, by ring⟩, fun h' => h.1 (key x y 1 ?_),
      fun h' => h.2 (key x y (-1) ?_)⟩
    · simpa using h'
    · simpa using h'
  have k1 := k 1 0
  have k2 := k 0 1
  have k3 := k 1 1
  omega


[GCF_torsion_02] Exact g3 sign descent and g2 cube-root descent
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/IwasawaDecomposition/ExistsOrderThree.lean#L132-L250
Range SHA256: cdd9136e60727314f0770b2b75cb0979c8cb7c7e9093ba15e80dc7cbe5bf004b
theorem map_g₃ (L : PeriodPair) (hΔ : L.g₂ ^ 3 - 27 * L.g₃ ^ 2 = 1) {w : ℂ} {k : ℤ} (hw : w * (1 - w) = 2 * k)
    (hs : ∀ l ∈ L.lattice, w * l ∈ L.lattice) {t : ℂ} (t2 : 2 * t ∈ L.lattice) (ta : w * t ∉ L.lattice)
    (tb : (1 - w) * t ∉ L.lattice) (σ : ℂ ≃+* ℂ) (hσ : σ w = w) (h2 : σ (L.g₂ ^ 3) = L.g₂ ^ 3)
    (h3 : σ (L.g₃ ^ 2) = L.g₃ ^ 2) : σ L.g₃ = L.g₃ := by
  obtain ⟨η, -, e3, h⟩ := exists_action L σ h2 h3
  obtain ⟨t', h⟩ := h t
  have hs' : ∀ l ∈ L.lattice, (1 - w) * l ∈ L.lattice := fun l hl => by
    rw [sub_mul, one_mul]; exact sub_mem hl (hs l hl)
  obtain ⟨a1, a2⟩ := h w hσ hs
  obtain ⟨b1, b2⟩ := h (1 - w) (by rw [map_sub, map_one, hσ]) hs'
  obtain ⟨c1, -⟩ := h 2 (map_ofNat σ 2) fun l hl => by rw [two_mul]; exact add_mem hl hl
  have hlat : GlobalTheory.ThetaConj.IsLat L.lattice.toAddSubgroup := by
    refine ⟨L.ω₁, L.ω₂, L.indep, ?_⟩
    ext z
    rw [AddSubgroup.mem_closure_pair, Submodule.mem_toAddSubgroup, PeriodPair.mem_lattice]
    simp only [zsmul_eq_mul]
  have hu : t - t' ∈ L.lattice := GlobalTheory.ThetaConj.two_torsion_unique hlat w k hw t2 ta tb (c1.1 t2)
    (mt a1.2 ta) (mt b1.2 tb) hs
  have ea : ℘[L] (w * t) = ℘[L] (w * t') :=
    (weierstrassP_eq_iff L ta (mt a1.2 ta)).2 (.inl (by rw [← mul_sub]; exact hs _ hu))
  have eb : ℘[L] ((1 - w) * t) = ℘[L] ((1 - w) * t') :=
    (weierstrassP_eq_iff L tb (mt b1.2 tb)).2 (.inl (by rw [← mul_sub]; exact hs' _ hu))
  have fa := a2 ta
  have fb := b2 tb
  rw [← ea] at fa
  rw [← eb] at fb
  have ha2 : w * t + w * t ∈ L.lattice := by convert hs _ t2 using 1; ring
  have hb2 : (1 - w) * t + (1 - w) * t ∈ L.lattice := by convert hs' _ t2 using 1; ring
  have hne : ℘[L] (w * t) ≠ ℘[L] ((1 - w) * t) := fun e => by
    have ht : t ∈ L.lattice := by
      rcases (weierstrassP_eq_iff L ta tb).1 e with e | e
      · convert add_mem e hb2 using 1; ring
      · convert e using 1; ring
    exact ta (hs _ ht)
  have hd := cubic_discr (cubic_eq_zero L ta ha2) (cubic_eq_zero L tb hb2) hne
  rw [hΔ] at hd
  generalize ℘[L] (w * t) = x at *
  generalize ℘[L] ((1 - w) * t) = y at *
  have hP : σ (4 * ((x - y) * (2 * x + y) * (x + 2 * y))) = η ^ 3 * (4 * ((x - y) * (2 * x + y) * (x + 2 * y))) := by
    simp only [map_mul, map_add, map_sub, map_ofNat, fa, fb]; ring
  have : η ^ 3 = 1 := by
    rcases sq_eq_sq_iff_eq_or_eq_neg.1 (hd.trans (one_pow 2).symm) with e | e <;> rw [e] at hP
    · simpa using hP.symm
    · simpa using hP.symm
  rw [e3, this, one_mul]

theorem exists_map_g₂ (L : PeriodPair) (hΔ : L.g₂ ^ 3 - 27 * L.g₃ ^ 2 = 1) {β : ℂ} {d : ℕ} (hd : d % 3 ≠ 0)
    (hβ : β ^ 2 = -d) (hs : ∀ l ∈ L.lattice, β * l ∈ L.lattice) :
    ∃ u : ℂ, u ^ 3 = 1 ∧ ∀ σ : ℂ ≃+* ℂ, σ β = β → σ (L.g₂ ^ 3) = L.g₂ ^ 3 → σ (L.g₃ ^ 2) = L.g₃ ^ 2 →
      σ (u * L.g₂) = u * L.g₂ := by
  have hββ : ∀ t, 3 * t ∈ L.lattice → β * (β * t) - t ∈ L.lattice ∨ β * (β * t) + t ∈ L.lattice := fun t ht => by
    obtain ⟨n, hn | hn⟩ : ∃ n : ℕ, d + 1 = 3 * n ∨ d = 3 * n + 1 := by
      rcases (show d % 3 = 1 ∨ d % 3 = 2 by omega) with h | h
      · exact ⟨d / 3, .inr (by omega)⟩
      · exact ⟨d / 3 + 1, .inl (by omega)⟩
    · have hn' : (d : ℂ) + 1 = 3 * n := by exact_mod_cast hn
      left
      convert neg_mem (nsmul_mem ht n) using 1
      rw [nsmul_eq_mul]; linear_combination t * hβ - t * hn'
    · have hn' : (d : ℂ) = 3 * n + 1 := by exact_mod_cast hn
      right
      convert neg_mem (nsmul_mem ht n) using 1
      rw [nsmul_eq_mul]; linear_combination t * hβ - t * hn'
  have h3β : ∀ t, 3 * t ∈ L.lattice → 3 * (β * t) ∈ L.lattice := fun t ht => by
    rw [mul_left_comm]; exact hs _ ht
  have hnβ : ∀ t, 3 * t ∈ L.lattice → t ∉ L.lattice → β * t ∉ L.lattice := fun t ht hn h => hn <| by
    rcases hββ t ht with e | e
    · simpa using sub_mem (hs _ h) e
    · simpa using sub_mem e (hs _ h)
  have hA : ∀ t, 3 * t ∈ L.lattice → t ∉ L.lattice → ℘[L] (β * (β * t)) = ℘[L] t := fun t ht hn =>
    (weierstrassP_eq_iff L (hnβ _ (h3β t ht) (hnβ t ht hn)) hn).2 (hββ t ht)
  have hB : ∀ s t, 3 * s ∈ L.lattice → s ∉ L.lattice → 3 * t ∈ L.lattice → t ∉ L.lattice → ℘[L] s = ℘[L] t →
      ℘[L] (β * s) = ℘[L] (β * t) := fun s t s3 sn t3 tn e => by
    rw [weierstrassP_eq_iff L (hnβ s s3 sn) (hnβ t t3 tn), ← mul_sub, ← mul_add]
    exact ((weierstrassP_eq_iff L sn tn).1 e).imp (hs _) (hs _)
  have hs1 : ∀ l ∈ L.lattice, (β - 1) * l ∈ L.lattice := fun l hl => by
    rw [sub_mul, one_mul]; exact sub_mem (hs l hl) hl
  have hs2 : ∀ l ∈ L.lattice, (β + 1) * l ∈ L.lattice := fun l hl => by
    rw [add_mul, one_mul]; exact add_mem (hs l hl) hl
  have hne : ∀ t, 3 * t ∈ L.lattice → (β - 1) * t ∉ L.lattice → (β + 1) * t ∉ L.lattice →
      ℘[L] t ≠ ℘[L] (β * t) := fun t ht a b e => by
    have tn : t ∉ L.lattice := fun h => a (hs1 _ h)
    rcases (weierstrassP_eq_iff L (hnβ t ht tn) tn).1 e.symm with e | e
    · exact a (by convert e using 1; ring)
    · exact b (by convert e using 1; ring)
  obtain ⟨t, t3, ta, tb⟩ := exists_order_three L (Nat.pos_of_ne_zero (by rintro rfl; simp at hd)) hβ hs
  have tn : t ∉ L.lattice := fun h => ta (hs1 _ h)
  have hx := quartic_eq_zero L tn t3
  have hy := quartic_eq_zero L (hnβ t t3 tn) (h3β t t3)
  have hv := quartic_discr hx hy (hne t t3 ta tb)
  rw [hΔ] at hv
  refine ⟨(L.g₂ - 3 * (℘[L] t + ℘[L] (β * t)) ^ 2)⁻¹, by rw [inv_pow, hv, inv_one], fun σ hσ h2 h3 => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨η, e2, -, h⟩ := exists_action L σ h2 h3
  obtain ⟨t', h⟩ := h t
  have t3' := (h 3 (map_ofNat σ 3) fun l hl => by simpa using nsmul_mem hl 3).1.1 t3
  have ta' := mt (h (β - 1) (by rw [map_sub, map_one, hσ]) hs1).1.2 ta
  have tb' := mt (h (β + 1) (by rw [map_add, map_one, hσ]) hs2).1.2 tb
  have tn' : t' ∉ L.lattice := fun h => ta' (hs1 _ h)
  have fx := (h 1 (map_one σ) fun l hl => by simpa using hl).2 (by simpa using tn)
  rw [one_mul, one_mul] at fx
  have fy := (h β hσ hs).2 (hnβ t t3 tn)
  have hsq : (℘[L] t' + ℘[L] (β * t')) ^ 2 = (℘[L] t + ℘[L] (β * t)) ^ 2 := by
    by_cases c1 : ℘[L] t = ℘[L] t'
    · rw [hB _ _ t3 tn t3' tn' c1, c1]
    by_cases c2 : ℘[L] t = ℘[L] (β * t')
    · rw [hB _ _ t3 tn (h3β _ t3') (hnβ _ t3' tn') c2, hA _ t3' tn', c2, add_comm]
    have c3 : ℘[L] (β * t) ≠ ℘[L] t' := fun e => c2 (by
      rw [← hB _ _ (h3β _ t3) (hnβ _ t3 tn) t3' tn' e, hA _ t3 tn])
    have c4 : ℘[L] (β * t) ≠ ℘[L] (β * t') := fun e => c1 (by
      rw [← hA _ t3 tn, hB _ _ (h3β _ t3) (hnβ _ t3 tn) (h3β _ t3') (hnβ _ t3' tn') e, hA _ t3' tn'])
    rw [quartic_sum hx hy (quartic_eq_zero L tn' t3') (quartic_eq_zero L (hnβ _ t3' tn') (h3β _ t3'))
      (hne t t3 ta tb) c1 c2 c3 c4 (hne t' t3' ta' tb'), neg_sq]
  have hσv : σ (L.g₂ - 3 * (℘[L] t + ℘[L] (β * t)) ^ 2) = η ^ 2 * (L.g₂ - 3 * (℘[L] t + ℘[L] (β * t)) ^ 2) := by
    rw [map_sub, map_mul, map_pow, map_add, map_ofNat, fx, fy, e2, ← hsq]; ring
  generalize L.g₂ - 3 * (℘[L] t + ℘[L] (β * t)) ^ 2 = v at hv hσv
  have hv0 : v ≠ 0 := by rintro rfl; simp at hv
  have hη : η ^ 2 ≠ 0 := left_ne_zero_of_mul (hσv ▸ (map_ne_zero σ).2 hv0)
  rw [map_mul, map_inv₀, hσv, e2, mul_inv, mul_mul_mul_comm, inv_mul_cancel₀ hη, one_mul]



[GCF_torsion_03] Original baseLat, FieldOK, half-point predicate, chosen generator and trueGx
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Definitions/AutomorphicMembership/IsHalfPtIff.lean#L18-L55
Range SHA256: 6646286aa108048defe1b274d42fc609e93d0a9bcef6084f4e23a8558712f0a3
def jLat (Λ : AddSubgroup ℂ) : ℂ := 1728 * g2 Λ ^ 3 / discr Λ

variable {R : Ranks} (H : Host R) {LL : Type} [Field LL] [NumberField LL]

def baseLat : AddSubgroup ℂ :=
  AddSubgroup.closure {w | ∃ c ∈ H.ccK, w = H.φ₀ (((c : H.K) : H.E))}

def IsConjLat (emb s : LL →+* ℂ) (Λ Λ' : AddSubgroup ℂ) : Prop :=
  discr Λ' = 1 ∧ ∃ a : LL, emb a = jLat Λ ∧ s a = jLat Λ'

def conjLat (emb s : LL →+* ℂ) : AddSubgroup ℂ := Classical.epsilon (IsConjLat emb s (baseLat H))

def ellLat (embE : H.E →+* LL) (g : R.GG) : Submodule (𝓞 LL) LL :=
  Submodule.span (𝓞 LL) {y | ∃ a ∈ (H.AA0 : Submodule (𝓞 H.E) H.E),
    ∃ b ∈ differentIdeal (𝓞 H.K) (𝓞 H.E), y = embE (H.sigmaOf g (a * (b : H.E)))}

def IsGen (embE : H.E →+* LL) (g : R.GG) (x : LL) : Prop :=
  Submodule.span (𝓞 LL) {x} = ellLat H embE g

def gen (embE : H.E →+* LL) (g : R.GG) : LL := Classical.epsilon (IsGen H embE g)

def FieldOK (emb : LL →+* ℂ) (embE : H.E →+* LL) : Prop :=
  (∃ a : LL, emb a = jLat (baseLat H)) ∧ ∀ g, ∃ x, IsGen H embE g x

def IsHalfPt (d : ℕ) (Λ : AddSubgroup ℂ) (t : ℂ) : Prop :=
  2 * t ∈ Λ ∧ ((1 + Complex.I * (Real.sqrt d : ℂ)) / 2) * t ∉ Λ ∧ ((1 - Complex.I * (Real.sqrt d : ℂ)) / 2) * t ∉ Λ

def omAt (emb s : LL →+* ℂ) (x : LL) : ℝ := area (conjLat H emb s) / (Real.pi * ‖s x‖ ^ 2)

def thAt (emb s : LL →+* ℂ) : ℝ :=
  Real.sqrt (StandardLatticesGL3.nrm1Sq (conjLat H emb s)
    (GL3Theta.th0 (conjLat H emb s) (Classical.epsilon (IsHalfPt H.d (conjLat H emb s)))))

def trueGx (emb : LL →+* ℂ) (embE : H.E →+* LL) {I : Type} (e : I ≃ R.Psi) : GlobalExtra LL I where
  Om τ s := posOr1 (omAt H emb s (gen H embE (e τ).1))
  Om_pos _ _ := posOr1_pos _
  thOne s := posOr1 (thAt H emb s)
  thOne_pos _ := posOr1_pos _


[GCF_period_data_01] Full exists_delta_data on the same coefficient image field
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/IwasawaDecomposition/ExistsOrderThree.lean#L255-L311
Range SHA256: 21a55ae21a8911d53e7ef53d28c28221b065c7872319e7c9bf9f1b433c7c8de8
theorem exists_delta_data (D : D7) (hF : FieldOK D.host D.what₂.embLL D.embE) :
    ∃ δ, IsDiscriminantTwelfthRootOfIdealLattice D D.host.ccK δ ∧ g2 (lamOf D D.host.ccK δ) ∈ coefM D ∧ g3 (lamOf D D.host.ccK δ) ∈ coefM D := by
  obtain ⟨δ, h⟩ := exists_discriminantTwelfthRoot_of_ideal D
  have hc : Crv D D.host.ccK δ := ⟨isCurveIdeal_ccK D, h⟩
  obtain ⟨-, m2, m3⟩ := data_of_fieldOK (M := coefM D) hF h fun x => ⟨x, rfl⟩
  obtain ⟨hlat, -, hΔ, -, hO⟩ :=
    GlobalTheory.CurveLattice.lattice_of_curve D (GlobalTheory.InducedType.induced_type D).1 _ δ hc
  obtain ⟨L, hL⟩ := GlobalTheory.ThetaConj.IsLat.exists_periodPair hlat
  obtain ⟨e2, e3, -⟩ := GlobalTheory.LatticeInvariants.invariants_dictionary L _ hL
  rw [TaylorLogMetric.discr, e2, e3] at hΔ
  rw [e2] at m2
  rw [e3] at m3
  obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := GlobalTheory.NormSum.exists_integer_map_eq_w0 D
  have mw : w0 D ∈ coefM D := ⟨D.embE _, by rw [← ha, ← D.embE_φ₀]; rfl⟩
  have hs : ∀ l ∈ L.lattice, w0 D * l ∈ L.lattice := fun l hl => (hL _).1 (ha ▸ hO a l ((hL l).2 hl))
  have key : ∀ x : ℂ, (∀ σ : ℂ ≃+* ℂ, (∀ y ∈ coefM D, σ y = y) → σ x = x) → x ∈ coefM D := fun x hx => by
    obtain ⟨y, rfl⟩ := GlobalTheory.GaloisDescent.complex_mem_range_of_forall_algEquiv (K := coefM D) x
      fun σ => hx σ.toRingEquiv fun y hy => σ.commutes ⟨y, hy⟩
    exact y.2
  obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := GlobalTheory.ThetaConj.weylMatrix_mul_apply D
  obtain ⟨t, ht⟩ := (GlobalTheory.TwoTorsionPt.exists_point D _ δ hc).1
  rw [GlobalTheory.ThetaConj.twoTorsionCMPoint_iff, hL, hL, hL] at ht
  have n3 : L.g₃ ∈ coefM D := key _ fun σ hσ =>
    map_g₃ L hΔ hk hs ht.1 ht.2.1 ht.2.2 σ (hσ _ mw) (hσ _ m2) (hσ _ m3)
  have hd : D.host.d % 3 ≠ 0 := fun h3 => by
    have := (Nat.prime_dvd_prime_iff_eq Nat.prime_three D.host.hyp.2.1).1 (Nat.dvd_of_mod_eq_zero h3)
    have := D.host.hyp.2.2.2.1
    omega
  have hsq : ((Real.sqrt D.host.d : ℝ) : ℂ) ^ 2 = D.host.d := by
    rw [← Complex.ofReal_pow, Real.sq_sqrt (Nat.cast_nonneg _)]; simp
  obtain ⟨u, hu, hσu⟩ := exists_map_g₂ L hΔ (β := 2 * w0 D - 1) hd
    (by unfold w0; linear_combination ((Real.sqrt D.host.d : ℝ) : ℂ) ^ 2 * Complex.I_sq - hsq)
    fun l hl => by
      convert sub_mem (add_mem (hs l hl) (hs l hl)) hl using 1; ring
  have n2 : u * L.g₂ ∈ coefM D := key _ fun σ hσ =>
    hσu σ (by rw [map_sub, map_mul, map_ofNat, map_one, hσ _ mw]) (hσ _ m2) (hσ _ m3)
  have hu0 : u ^ 2 ≠ 0 := by
    refine pow_ne_zero _ ?_
    rintro rfl; simp at hu
  have hmap : lamOf D D.host.ccK (u ^ 2 * δ) = (lamOf D D.host.ccK δ).map (AddMonoidHom.mulLeft (u ^ 2)) := by
    rw [lamOf_eq_map, lamOf_eq_map, AddSubgroup.map_map]
    congr 1
    ext; simp [mul_assoc]
  refine ⟨u ^ 2 * δ, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · unfold IsDiscriminantTwelfthRootOfIdealLattice at h ⊢
    rw [mul_pow, h, show (u ^ 2) ^ 12 = (u ^ 3) ^ 8 by ring, hu, one_pow, one_mul]
  · rw [← e2] at n2
    convert n2 using 1
    unfold g2
    rw [hmap, Model.eisG_map _ _ hu0, inv_eq_of_mul_eq_one_right (b := u)
      (by rw [show (u ^ 2) ^ (2 * 2) * u = (u ^ 3) ^ 3 by ring, hu, one_pow])]
    ring
  · rw [← e3] at n3
    convert n3 using 1
    unfold g3
    rw [hmap, Model.eisG_map _ _ hu0, show (u ^ 2) ^ (2 * 3) = (u ^ 3) ^ 4 by ring, hu]
    simp


[GCF_period_data_02] Unit homothety, discriminant-one lattice dictionary and exact twelfth-root ambiguity
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/IwasawaDecomposition/MeanSquareEvenThetaTwelfthRootInvariant.lean#L15-L80
Range SHA256: 370e6016b73ba11e0277bde0d46012577d5e20d1474a1acea065a1404fdfbce4
theorem area_map_unit (Λ : AddSubgroup ℂ) {ζ : ℂ} (hζ : ‖ζ‖ = 1) :
    area (Λ.map (AddMonoidHom.mulLeft ζ)) = area Λ := by
  have e : ∀ x y : ℂ, (starRingEnd ℂ) (ζ * x) * (ζ * y) = (starRingEnd ℂ) x * y := fun x y => by
    have : (starRingEnd ℂ) ζ * ζ = 1 := by
      rw [mul_comm, Complex.mul_conj, Complex.normSq_eq_norm_sq, hζ]; simp
    rw [map_mul]; linear_combination ((starRingEnd ℂ) x * y) * this
  unfold area
  congr 1
  ext a
  simp only [Set.mem_setOf_eq]
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨ha, _, ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩, _, ⟨y, hy, rfl⟩, rfl⟩
    exact ⟨ha, x, hx, y, hy, congrArg (fun z : ℂ => |z.im|) (e x y)⟩
  · rintro ⟨ha, x, hx, y, hy, rfl⟩
    exact ⟨ha, ζ * x, ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩, ζ * y, ⟨y, hy, rfl⟩, congrArg (fun z : ℂ => |z.im|) (e x y).symm⟩

theorem isLat_of_discr_one {Λ : AddSubgroup ℂ} (h : TaylorLogMetric.discr Λ = 1) : GlobalTheory.ThetaConj.IsLat Λ := by
  obtain ⟨L, hL⟩ := GlobalTheory.LatticeInvariants.exists_periodPair_of_discr_ne_zero (Λ := Λ) (by rw [h]; exact one_ne_zero)
  refine ⟨L.ω₁, L.ω₂, L.indep, ?_⟩
  ext z
  rw [hL, AddSubgroup.mem_closure_pair, PeriodPair.mem_lattice]
  simp only [zsmul_eq_mul]

theorem exists_root_of_jLat_eq {Λ Λ' : AddSubgroup ℂ} (h : TaylorLogMetric.discr Λ = 1) (h' : TaylorLogMetric.discr Λ' = 1)
    (hj : jLat Λ = jLat Λ') : ∃ ζ : ℂ, ζ ^ 12 = 1 ∧ Λ' = Λ.map (AddMonoidHom.mulLeft ζ) := by
  have h2 : g2 Λ ^ 3 = g2 Λ' ^ 3 := by
    unfold jLat at hj
    rw [h, h'] at hj
    linear_combination hj / 1728
  have h3 : g3 Λ ^ 2 = g3 Λ' ^ 2 := by
    unfold TaylorLogMetric.discr at h h'
    linear_combination (h' - h + h2) / 27
  obtain ⟨u, hu, hu2⟩ : ∃ u : ℂ, u ^ 3 = 1 ∧ g2 Λ = u * g2 Λ' := by
    by_cases h0 : g2 Λ' = 0
    · refine ⟨1, one_pow _, ?_⟩
      rw [h0] at h2 ⊢
      simpa using h2
    · exact ⟨g2 Λ / g2 Λ', by rw [div_pow, h2, div_self (pow_ne_zero _ h0)], by field_simp⟩
  obtain ⟨ζ, h4, h6⟩ : ∃ ζ : ℂ, ζ ^ 4 = u ∧ g3 Λ = ζ ^ 6 * g3 Λ' := by
    rcases sq_eq_sq_iff_eq_or_eq_neg.1 h3 with e | e
    · exact ⟨u, by linear_combination u * hu, by rw [e]; linear_combination (-(u ^ 3 + 1) * g3 Λ') * hu⟩
    · have i4 : Complex.I ^ 4 = 1 := Complex.I_pow_four
      have i6 : Complex.I ^ 6 = -1 := by
        rw [show (6 : ℕ) = 4 + 2 from rfl, pow_add, i4, Complex.I_sq, one_mul]
      refine ⟨u * Complex.I, ?_, ?_⟩
      · rw [mul_pow, i4]; linear_combination u * hu
      · rw [mul_pow, i6, e]; linear_combination ((u ^ 3 + 1) * g3 Λ') * hu
  have h12 : ζ ^ 12 = 1 := by rw [show ζ ^ 12 = (ζ ^ 4) ^ 3 by ring, h4, hu]
  have h0 : ζ ≠ 0 := by rintro rfl; simp at h12
  refine ⟨ζ, h12, ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨L, hL⟩ := (isLat_of_discr_one h').exists_periodPair
  refine ((GlobalTheory.LatticeInvariants.invariants_dictionary L Λ' hL).2.2.2.2.2.1 _ ?_ ?_).symm
  · rw [← h4] at hu2
    unfold g2 at hu2 ⊢
    rw [Model.eisG_map _ _ h0]
    field_simp
    linear_combination hu2 / 60
  · unfold g3 at h6 ⊢
    rw [Model.eisG_map _ _ h0]
    field_simp
    linear_combination h6 / 140

theorem norm_eq_one_of_pow {ζ : ℂ} (h : ζ ^ 12 = 1) : ‖ζ‖ = 1 := by
  have := congrArg norm h
  rw [norm_pow, norm_one] at this
  exact (pow_eq_one_iff_of_nonneg (norm_nonneg _) (by norm_num)).1 this


[GCF_period_data_03] Conjugate lattice, full DataIn, actual different-ideal generator and Omega identity
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/IwasawaDecomposition/MeanSquareEvenThetaTwelfthRootInvariant.lean#L134-L235

theorem discr_baseLat_ne_zero (D : D7) : TaylorLogMetric.discr (baseLat D.host) ≠ 0 := by
  obtain ⟨L, hL⟩ := GlobalTheory.Mem.Per.ConjCM.exists_periodPair_baseLat D.host
  exact (GlobalTheory.LatticeInvariants.invariants_dictionary L _ hL).2.2.1

theorem delta_ne_zero (h : IsDiscriminantTwelfthRootOfIdealLattice D D.host.ccK δ) : δ ≠ 0 := by
  rintro rfl
  apply discr_baseLat_ne_zero D
  rw [← lamOf_one, ← h]; simp

theorem discr_lamOf (h : IsDiscriminantTwelfthRootOfIdealLattice D D.host.ccK δ) : TaylorLogMetric.discr (lamOf D D.host.ccK δ) = 1 := by
  rw [lamOf_eq_map, Model.discr_map _ _ (delta_ne_zero h), ← lamOf_one, ← h,
    inv_mul_cancel₀ (pow_ne_zero _ (delta_ne_zero h))]

theorem jLat_lamOf (h : IsDiscriminantTwelfthRootOfIdealLattice D D.host.ccK δ) : jLat (lamOf D D.host.ccK δ) = jLat (baseLat D.host) := by
  rw [lamOf_eq_map, Model.jLat_map _ _ (delta_ne_zero h)]

theorem lamConj_spec {Λ : AddSubgroup ℂ} (h : TaylorLogMetric.discr Λ = 1) (h2 : g2 Λ ∈ M) (h3 : g3 Λ ∈ M) (ρ : M →ₐ[ℚ] ℂ) :
    g2 (lamConj M ρ Λ) = ρ ⟨_, h2⟩ ∧ g3 (lamConj M ρ Λ) = ρ ⟨_, h3⟩ ∧ TaylorLogMetric.discr (lamConj M ρ Λ) = 1 := by
  have h1 : ρ ⟨_, h2⟩ ^ 3 - 27 * ρ ⟨_, h3⟩ ^ 2 = 1 := by
    have : (⟨_, h2⟩ : M) ^ 3 - 27 * (⟨_, h3⟩ : M) ^ 2 = 1 := Subtype.ext h
    have e := congrArg ρ this
    rwa [map_sub, map_mul, map_pow, map_pow, map_ofNat, map_one] at e
  obtain ⟨L, e2, e3⟩ := GlobalTheory.LatticeInvariants.exists_periodPair_of_invariants (a := ρ ⟨_, h2⟩) (b := ρ ⟨_, h3⟩)
    (by rw [h1]; exact one_ne_zero)
  have hd := GlobalTheory.LatticeInvariants.invariants_dictionary L L.lattice.toAddSubgroup (fun _ => Iff.rfl)
  have l2 : liftM M (g2 Λ) = ⟨_, h2⟩ := dif_pos h2
  have l3 : liftM M (g3 Λ) = ⟨_, h3⟩ := dif_pos h3
  have hs := Classical.epsilon_spec (p := fun Λ' : AddSubgroup ℂ =>
    g2 Λ' = ρ (liftM M (g2 Λ)) ∧ g3 Λ' = ρ (liftM M (g3 Λ)))
    ⟨L.lattice.toAddSubgroup, by rw [hd.1, e2, l2], by rw [hd.2.1, e3, l3]⟩
  have a2 : g2 (lamConj M ρ Λ) = ρ ⟨_, h2⟩ := hs.1.trans (by rw [l2])
  have a3 : g3 (lamConj M ρ Λ) = ρ ⟨_, h3⟩ := hs.2.trans (by rw [l3])
  exact ⟨a2, a3, by rw [TaylorLogMetric.discr, a2, a3, h1]⟩

def into (D : D7) (M : IntermediateField ℚ ℂ) (hL : ∀ x, D.what₂.embLL x ∈ M) : D.what₂.LL →+* M :=
  D.what₂.embLL.codRestrict M hL

theorem conjLat_eq (hF : FieldOK D.host D.what₂.embLL D.embE) (h : IsDiscriminantTwelfthRootOfIdealLattice D D.host.ccK δ)
    (hL : ∀ x, D.what₂.embLL x ∈ M) (hM : DataIn D (tor0 D D.host.ccK δ) M) (ρ : M →ₐ[ℚ] ℂ)
    (s : D.what₂.LL →+* ℂ) (hs : ∀ x, s x = ρ (into D M hL x)) :
    ∃ ζ : ℂ, ζ ^ 12 = 1 ∧ conjLat D.host D.what₂.embLL s =
      (lamConj M ρ (lamOf D D.host.ccK δ)).map (AddMonoidHom.mulLeft ζ) := by
  obtain ⟨c1, a, ha, ha'⟩ := Model.conjLat_spec D.host hF s
  obtain ⟨a2, -, a1⟩ := lamConj_spec (discr_lamOf h) hM.2.1 hM.2.2 ρ
  refine exists_root_of_jLat_eq a1 c1 ?_
  have : into D M hL a = 1728 * (⟨_, hM.2.1⟩ : M) ^ 3 := by
    apply Subtype.ext
    change D.what₂.embLL a = 1728 * g2 (lamOf D D.host.ccK δ) ^ 3
    rw [ha, ← jLat_lamOf h, jLat, discr_lamOf h, div_one]
  rw [← ha', hs, this, jLat, a1, a2, div_one, map_mul, map_pow, map_ofNat]
  rfl

theorem data_of_fieldOK (hF : FieldOK D.host D.what₂.embLL D.embE) (h : IsDiscriminantTwelfthRootOfIdealLattice D D.host.ccK δ)
    (hL : ∀ x, D.what₂.embLL x ∈ M) :
    (∀ τ x, embT D τ x ∈ M) ∧ g2 (lamOf D D.host.ccK δ) ^ 3 ∈ M ∧ g3 (lamOf D D.host.ccK δ) ^ 2 ∈ M := by
  obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := hF.1
  have h1 := discr_lamOf h
  have h2 : g2 (lamOf D D.host.ccK δ) ^ 3 = D.what₂.embLL (a / 1728) := by
    rw [map_div₀, ha, ← jLat_lamOf h, jLat, h1, map_ofNat]; ring
  refine ⟨fun τ x => ?_, h2 ▸ hL _, ?_⟩
  · have := hL (D.embE (D.host.sigmaOf (D.eTau τ).1 x))
    rwa [← RingHom.comp_apply, D.embE_φ₀] at this
  · have : g3 (lamOf D D.host.ccK δ) ^ 2 = D.what₂.embLL ((a / 1728 - 1) / 27) := by
      rw [map_div₀, map_sub, ← h2, map_one, map_ofNat]
      unfold TaylorLogMetric.discr at h1
      linear_combination -h1 / 27
    exact this ▸ hL _

theorem span_gen [NumberField M] (hF : FieldOK D.host D.what₂.embLL D.embE) (hL : ∀ x, D.what₂.embLL x ∈ M)
    (τ : D.what₂.Tau) :
    Submodule.span (𝓞 M) {into D M hL (gen D.host D.embE (D.eTau τ).1)} = ellT D M τ := by
  rw [GlobalTheory.Mem.EigenLine.ellT_eq_ellLat D M ((into D M hL).comp D.embE) (fun x => by rw [← D.embE_φ₀]; rfl) τ]
  have hg := Model.gen_spec D.host hF (D.eTau τ).1
  have key : ∀ (S : Set D.what₂.LL) (N : Submodule (𝓞 M) M), (∀ y ∈ S, into D M hL y ∈ N) →
      ∀ y ∈ Submodule.span (𝓞 D.what₂.LL) S, into D M hL y ∈ N := by
    intro S N hS y hy
    induction hy using Submodule.span_induction with
    | mem y hy => exact hS y hy
    | zero => rw [map_zero]; exact N.zero_mem
    | add x y _ _ hx hy => rw [map_add]; exact N.add_mem hx hy
    | smul r y _ hy =>
      have := N.smul_mem (RingOfIntegers.mapRingHom (into D M hL) r) hy
      rw [Algebra.smul_def, map_mul]
      exact this
  apply le_antisymm
  · rw [Submodule.span_singleton_le_iff_mem]
    refine key _ _ ?_ _ (hg.le (Submodule.mem_span_singleton_self _))
    rintro _ ⟨a, ha, b, hb, rfl⟩
    exact Submodule.subset_span ⟨a, ha, b, hb, rfl⟩
  · refine Submodule.span_le.2 ?_
    rintro _ ⟨a, ha, b, hb, rfl⟩
    refine key {gen D.host D.embE (D.eTau τ).1} _ ?_ _ (hg.ge (Submodule.subset_span ⟨a, ha, b, hb, rfl⟩))
    rintro _ rfl
    exact Submodule.mem_span_singleton_self _

theorem om_eq (hF : FieldOK D.host D.what₂.embLL D.embE) (h : IsDiscriminantTwelfthRootOfIdealLattice D D.host.ccK δ)
    (hL : ∀ x, D.what₂.embLL x ∈ M) (hM : DataIn D (tor0 D D.host.ccK δ) M) (ρ : M →ₐ[ℚ] ℂ)
    (s : D.what₂.LL →+* ℂ) (hs : ∀ x, s x = ρ (into D M hL x)) (τ : D.what₂.Tau) :
    (retie D).gx.Om τ s = Real.exp (-logMet M (lamOf D D.host.ccK δ) ρ
      (into D M hL (gen D.host D.embE (D.eTau τ).1))) := by
  obtain ⟨ζ, hζ, e⟩ := conjLat_eq hF h hL hM ρ s hs
  have h0 := (retie D).gx.Om_pos τ s


[GCF_period_data_04] ties_of_data: the original OmegaCompatible consequence, distinct from its extra theta consequence
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/IwasawaDecomposition/ExistsOrderThree.lean#L353-L371

theorem ties_of_data {D : D7} {δ : ℂ} (hF : FieldOK D.host D.what₂.embLL D.embE) (h : IsDiscriminantTwelfthRootOfIdealLattice D D.host.ccK δ)
    (h2 : g2 (lamOf D D.host.ccK δ) ∈ coefM D) (h3 : g3 (lamOf D D.host.ccK δ) ∈ coefM D) :
    OmegaCompatible (retie D) δ ∧ DataIn D (tor0 D D.host.ccK δ) (coefM D) ∧ ∀ s : D.what₂.LL →+* ℂ,
      ∃ G, IsTheta0 D.host.d (lamConj (coefM D) (conjOf (imEquiv D.what₂.embLL).toRingEquiv s)
          (lamOf D D.host.ccK δ)) G ∧
        (retie D).gx.thOne s ^ 2 = normSq1 (lamConj (coefM D) (conjOf (imEquiv D.what₂.embLL).toRingEquiv s)
          (lamOf D D.host.ccK δ)) G := by
  obtain ⟨hc, hM, -, hth⟩ := perTie_retie hF h h2 h3
  refine ⟨⟨coefM D, inferInstance, (imEquiv D.what₂.embLL).toRingEquiv, fun _ => rfl, hM, fun τ => ?_⟩, hM,
    fun s => ?_⟩
  · have hL : ∀ x, D.what₂.embLL x ∈ coefM D := fun x => ⟨x, rfl⟩
    refine ⟨_, (span_gen hF hL τ).symm, fun s : D.what₂.LL →+* ℂ => ?_⟩
    have hs : ∀ x, s x = rhoOf D.what₂.embLL s (into D (coefM D) hL x) := fun x =>
      (rhoOf_imEquiv D.what₂.embLL s x).symm
    obtain ⟨ζ, hζ, e⟩ := conjLat_eq hF h hL hM _ s hs
    have h1 : (retie D).gx.Om τ s = _ := Model.om_eq D.host hF D.eTau τ s
    rw [e, area_map_unit _ (norm_eq_one_of_pow hζ), hs] at h1
    rw [h1, inv_div]
    rfl


[GCF_period_endpoint_01] All literal OmegaCompatible fields and the exact pi/norm/area normalization
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/LocalToGlobal/GlobalTheory/LinkTiesOm.lean#L63-L78
Range SHA256: fcb506a80c66e3894c69bacd4b3198b46106df855243e75b6359106b07f67b85
def conjOf {D : D7} {M : IntermediateField ℚ ℂ} (e : D.what₂.LL ≃+* M) (s : D.what₂.LL →+* ℂ) : M →ₐ[ℚ] ℂ :=
  (s.comp (e.symm : M →+* D.what₂.LL)).toRatAlgHom

def conjEquiv {D : D7} {M : IntermediateField ℚ ℂ} (e : D.what₂.LL ≃+* M) : (D.what₂.LL →+* ℂ) ≃ (M →ₐ[ℚ] ℂ) where
  toFun := conjOf e
  invFun ρ := (ρ : M →+* ℂ).comp (e : D.what₂.LL →+* M)
  left_inv s := by ext y; simp [conjOf]
  right_inv ρ := by ext y; simp [conjOf]

def OmegaCompatible (D : D7) (δ : ℂ) : Prop :=
  ∃ (M : IntermediateField ℚ ℂ) (_ : NumberField M) (e : D.what₂.LL ≃+* M),
    (∀ y, ((e y : M) : ℂ) = D.what₂.embLL y) ∧
    TaylorLogMetric.DataIn D (TaylorLogMetric.tor0 D D.host.ccK δ) M ∧
    ∀ τ, ∃ d : M, TaylorLogMetric.ellT D M τ = Submodule.span (𝓞 M) {d} ∧
      ∀ s : D.what₂.LL →+* ℂ, D.gx.Om τ s =
        (Real.pi * ‖conjOf e s d‖ ^ 2 / TaylorLogMetric.area (TaylorLogMetric.lamConj M (conjOf e s) (TaylorLogMetric.lamOf D D.host.ccK δ)))⁻¹


[GCF_period_endpoint_02] Given period root, actual third roots of unity in Base, omTied_retie_of_cube and omTied_withTrue

theorem omTied_retie_of_cube {D : D7} (hF : FieldOK D.host D.what₂.embLL D.embE) (h3 : ∀ z : ℂ, z ^ 3 = 1 → z ∈ coefM D) {δ : ℂ}
    (h : IsDiscriminantTwelfthRootOfIdealLattice D D.host.ccK δ) : Formalism.L2.GlobalTheoryTheory.OmegaCompatible (retie D) δ := by
  obtain ⟨δ', h', m2, m3⟩ := exists_delta_data D hF
  have h0 := delta_ne_zero h'
  have hu0 : δ / δ' ≠ 0 := div_ne_zero (delta_ne_zero h) h0
  have hu : (δ / δ') ^ 12 = 1 := by
    unfold IsDiscriminantTwelfthRootOfIdealLattice at h h'
    rw [div_pow, h, ← h', div_self (pow_ne_zero _ h0)]
  have hmap : lamOf D D.host.ccK δ = (lamOf D D.host.ccK δ').map (AddMonoidHom.mulLeft (δ / δ')) := by
    rw [lamOf_eq_map, lamOf_eq_map, AddSubgroup.map_map]
    congr 1
    ext; simp [← mul_assoc, div_mul_cancel₀ _ h0]
  generalize δ / δ' = u at hu0 hu hmap
  refine (ties_of_data hF h ?_ ?_).1
  · have e : g2 (lamOf D D.host.ccK δ) = (u ^ 4)⁻¹ * g2 (lamOf D D.host.ccK δ') := by
      unfold g2
      rw [hmap, Model.eisG_map _ _ hu0]; ring
    rw [e]
    exact mul_mem (inv_mem (h3 _ (by rw [← pow_mul, hu]))) m2
  · have e : g3 (lamOf D D.host.ccK δ) = (u ^ 6)⁻¹ * g3 (lamOf D D.host.ccK δ') := by
      unfold g3
      rw [hmap, Model.eisG_map _ _ hu0]; ring
    rw [e]
    refine mul_mem (inv_mem ?_) m3
    rcases sq_eq_one_iff.1 (show (u ^ 6) ^ 2 = 1 by rw [← pow_mul, hu]) with e | e <;> rw [e]
    · exact one_mem _
    · exact neg_mem (one_mem _)

theorem omTied_withTrue {B : Base} {C : Cols B} (hF : FieldOK B.host B.embLL B.embE) (hc : B.host.ccK = ⊤) {δ : ℂ}
    (h : Th.IsDiscriminantTwelfthRootOfIdealLattice B.host ⊤ δ) (Z : Later B C) (hZ : Z.G.gx = Per.trueGx B.host B.embLL B.embE (Equiv.refl _)) :
    Formalism.L2.GlobalTheoryTheory.OmegaCompatible (recOf Z) δ := by
  have h1 : IsDiscriminantTwelfthRootOfIdealLattice (recOf Z) (recOf Z).host.ccK δ := by
    have : (recOf Z).host.ccK = ⊤ := hc
    rw [this]; exact h
  have h2 := omTied_retie_of_cube (D := recOf Z) hF (fun z hz => B.fixedField_le_coefficientField (B.KsC_le_LL0C (B.rootsField_le_KsC
    ((le_sup_right.trans le_sup_left : Fld.cycField (3 ^ 4) ≤ Fld.rootsField B.toPre)
      (Fld.mem_cycField (by norm_num) (by rw [show 3 ^ 4 = 3 * 27 by norm_num, pow_mul, hz, one_pow])))))) h1
  obtain ⟨W, V, S, N, G⟩ := Z
  obtain ⟨M, i, e, a, b, c⟩ := h2
  refine ⟨M, i, e, a, b, fun τ => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨d, hd, hs⟩ := c τ
  refine ⟨d, hd, fun s => ?_⟩
  have : (recOf (⟨W, V, S, N, G⟩ : Later B C)).gx = G.gx := rfl
  rw [this, show G.gx = _ from hZ]
  exact hs s

def RingCompatibleAt (D : D7) (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl)
    (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) (v : Fin (D.gat u w hg).P.nV) : Prop :=
  ∃ (eW : WQ (D.what₂.places.resChar w) ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v) ≃+* (D.gat u w hg).P.W v)
    (eQ : QQ (D.what₂.places.resChar w) ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v) ≃+* (D.gat u w hg).P.Qq v)
    (eO : ORec D w ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v) ≃+* (D.gat u w hg).O)
    (eK : KfRec D w ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v) ≃+* (D.gat u w hg).Kf),
    (∀ x, eQ (algebraMap _ _ x) = algebraMap ((D.gat u w hg).P.W v) ((D.gat u w hg).P.Qq v) (eW x)) ∧
    (∀ x, eK (algebraMap _ _ x) = algebraMap (D.gat u w hg).O (D.gat u w hg).Kf (eO x)) ∧
    (∀ y, (D.gat u w hg).P.ι v (eQ y) =
      eK ((extRec D w ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v)).φK (iota (D.what₂.places.resChar w) ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v) y))) ∧
    (∀ x, (D.gat u w hg).P.j v (eW x) =
      eO ((extRec D w ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v)).φ (jO (D.what₂.places.resChar w) ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v) x))) ∧
    eO ((extRec D w ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v)).φ (wU (D.what₂.places.resChar w) ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v))) = (D.gat u w hg).P.w

theorem stdSubAt_of_ring (D : D7) (h : ∀ u w hg v, RingCompatibleAt D u w hg v) : Joints.StdSubAt D := by
  intro u w hg v
  obtain ⟨eW, eQ, eO, eK, hWQ, hOK, hι, hj, hw⟩ := h u w hg v
  have hD := (D.gat u w hg).P.setting v
  exact stdSub_of_iso hD eW eQ hWQ
    ((extRec D w ((D.gat u w hg).P.n' v)).map eO eK hOK hD.frac_O hD.complete_O) hι hj hw

def GatIs (D : D7) (H : LevelData D) (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl)
    (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) : Prop :=
  ∃ (W Qq O Kf : Type) (_ : CommRing W) (_ : IsDomain W) (_ : IsDiscreteValuationRing W) (_ : Field Qq) (_ : Algebra W Qq)
    (_ : CommRing O) (_ : IsDomain O) (_ : IsDiscreteValuationRing O) (_ : Field Kf) (_ : Algebra O Kf)
    (n' : ℕ) (ι : Qq →+* Kf) (σ : Qq ≃+* Qq) (j : W →+* O) (w0 : O)
    (hS : Formalism.MainNotions.G.HidaSetting (D.what₂.places.resChar w) n' W Qq O Kf ι σ j w0) (nV r m : ℕ)
    (lam0 : Fin nV → Fin n' → Fin (m + 1) → ℤ) (dom : ∀ v, IsDominant (lam0 v)) (hk : m + 1 = D.R.k)
    (I : LocId (H u) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) nV W Qq) (ψ : Formalism.L2.Herm.awayFrom₂ (H u) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) →* Oˣ)
    (e : ℕ), Cmp.HypAtP I ∧ D.gat u w hg = gatOfLevelP (H u) hS nV r lam0 dom hk I ψ e

theorem gatE_of_gatIs (D : D7) (H : LevelData D) (hT : ∀ u, Cmp.TrivStabU (H u))
    (h : ∀ u w hg, GatIs D H u w hg) : TrivialStabiliserFiniteClasses D := by
  intro u w hg
  obtain ⟨W, Qq, O, Kf, _, _, _, _, _, _, _, _, _, _, n', ι, σ, j, w0, hS, nV, r, m, lam0, dom, hk, I, ψ, e, hH, heq⟩ :=
    h u w hg
  rw [heq]
  exact ⟨Cmp.trivStab hS r lam0 dom hk I ψ e (isUnit_det_gram0 D.sp) hH (hT u),
    Cmp.finClasses hS r lam0 dom hk I ψ e (isUnit_det_gram0 D.sp) hH⟩

structure GroupCompatibilityAll (D : D7) (Gt : GatTies D) (c : LatCmp D) where

  herm : Formalism.L2.Herm.Herm0 D

  forms : ∀ u : D.what₂.Word, Forms0 D u

  cmp : Cmp.CmpTied D herm forms Gt c

  ring : ∀ u w hg v, RingCompatibleAt D u w hg v

  gat : ∀ u w hg, GatIs D herm.lev0 u w hg

def GroupCompatibilityFamily {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Prop :=
  Ev Fm fun x i => ∀ hx : Fm.x1 < x, Nonempty (GroupCompatibilityAll (Fm.D x i) (Fm.gatT x i) (Fm.ex x hx i).cmp)

variable (D : D7) (L0 : LevelData D)

def Companion : Prop :=
  ∀ u, ∃ 𝔞 : Ideal (𝓞 D.host.E), (L0 u).Lb ⊔ 𝔞 • Submodule.span (𝓞 D.host.E)
    (dualSet (D.sp.cm.sig : D.host.E →+* D.host.E) D.sp.gram0 (L0 u).Lb) = D.sp.crdLat0 (D.sp.X 0).L0

def stdBasis (hS : Formalism.L2.Herm.StdTied D) : GL (Fin D.R.k) D.host.E := Classical.choose (exists_stdB_of_tied D hS)

theorem stdBasis_std (hS : Formalism.L2.Herm.StdTied D) : Cmp.StdB D (stdBasis D hS) := Classical.choose_spec (exists_stdB_of_tied D hS)

def placeId (hS : Formalism.L2.Herm.StdTied D) (hC : Companion D L0) (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl)
    (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) :
    LocId (L0 u) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) (nOrb (zH D.R.toFrame (hP D (D.what₂.places.resChar w))))
      (WQ (D.what₂.places.resChar w) (nFr D (D.what₂.places.resChar w)))
      (QQ (D.what₂.places.resChar w) (nFr D (D.what₂.places.resChar w))) :=
  locIdStd D u w hg (Classical.choose (GlobalTheory.PlaceOrbit.exists_locId D u (L0 u) w hg)) (stdBasis D hS) (stdBasis_std D hS)
    (Classical.choose (hC u)) (Classical.choose_spec (hC u))

def embK (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) :
    KfRec D w (nFr D (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) →+* ℂ_[D.what₂.places.resChar w] :=
  Classical.choose (Classical.choose_spec (GlobalTheory.PlaceOrbit.exists_locId D u (L0 u) w hg))

def embAlg (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (hg : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) :
    (algebraicClosure ℚ ℂ) →+* ℂ_[D.what₂.places.resChar w] :=
  Classical.choose (Classical.choose_spec (Classical.choose_spec (GlobalTheory.PlaceOrbit.exists_locId D u (L0 u) w hg)))

    (z : D.what₂.LL) : embK D L0 u w hg (embRec D w (nFr D (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) z) = lamV (embAlg D L0 u w hg) (D.what₂.embLL z) :=

    (sc : Scale) (u : D.what₂.Word) (w : D.what₂.places.Pl) (h : GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) :
    GAtP (D.what₂.places.resChar w) :=
  (packC D (fun u => L0 u) I ψ u w h).gat (rSc sc D.R.k (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) (epSc D.R sc 1 (D.what₂.places.resChar w))

  exact e

    (g : ∀ u (w : D.what₂.places.Pl), GIdx D.host (D.mem u).A.T (D.what₂.places.resChar w) → GAtP (D.what₂.places.resChar w)) : Prop :=

        (Kf := KfRec D w (nFr D (D.what₂.places.resChar w)))
        ((T u).cls0).lev (L0 u) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) (rank_eq_first_level D.R) D.what₂.embLL
        (embRec D w (nFr D (D.what₂.places.resChar w))) (ψ u w hg)) ∧
    g = gatOf D L0 I ψ sc

theorem exists_isLocalFamily (sc : Scale) {D : D7} {L0 : LevelData D} (T : ∀ u : D.what₂.Word, Forms0 D u)
    (hS : Formalism.L2.Herm.StdTied D) (hC : Companion D L0) (hJ : ∀ u, Joint0 L0 u (T u)) (hψ : ∀ u, LevelZeroCharacterRational T u) :

    fun u w hg => Classical.choose_spec (GlobalTheory.Mem.AwayChar.exists_psi D L0 T hJ hψ u w hg), rfl⟩

theorem isRecG_of_isLocalFamily {sc : Scale} {D : D7} {L0 : LevelData D} {T : ∀ u : D.what₂.Word, Forms0 D u}
    (hJ : ∀ u, Joint0 L0 u (T u)) (hψ : ∀ u, LevelZeroCharacterRational T u) {g} (hd : ∀ u w h, (g u w h).d = Module.finrank ℂ (D.mem u).A.Mu0)
    (h : IsLocalFamily sc D L0 T g) :
    ∃ (Gt : GatTies (setGat D g hd)) (c : LatCmp (setGat D g hd)) (G : GroupCompatibilityAll (setGat D g hd) Gt c),
      IsRecG sc (setGat D g hd) Gt c G.herm G.forms ∧ (∀ u, G.herm.lev0 u = L0 u) ∧ (∀ u, G.forms u = forms0Set D g hd (T u)) ∧
      ∀ u w hg (v : Fin ((setGat D g hd).gat u w hg).P.nV) (s : Fin (((setGat D g hd).gat u w hg).P.n' v)) (x : 𝓞 D.host.F),
        x ∈ ((G.herm.g u w hg).pl v).1.asIdeal ↔
          (0 : WithTop ℤ) < D.what₂.places.val w (tauLL (setGat D g hd) (Gt.gat_pos u w hg ⟨v, s⟩)
            (algebraMap D.host.F D.host.E (x : D.host.F))) := by
  obtain ⟨I, ψ, B, iK, lam, hBs, hB, hK, hPos, hψ', rfl⟩ := h
  let H0 : Formalism.L2.Herm.Herm0 (recC D sc 1 (fun u => L0 u) I ψ hd) :=
    ⟨fun u => L0 u, levTieG D sc 1 (fun u => L0 u) (packC D (fun u => L0 u) I ψ) hd⟩
  let T' : ∀ u, Forms0 (recC D sc 1 (fun u => L0 u) I ψ hd) u := fun u => forms0Set D (gatOf D L0 I ψ sc) hd (T u)
  have CH := Test.comparison_statements D sc (fun u => L0 u) I ψ hd H0 (fun _ => rfl) T' B hBs hB iK lam hK hPos hJ hψ hψ'
  have hpos := fun u w hg v s x => pos_C D sc 1 (fun u => L0 u) I ψ hd H0 (fun _ => rfl) B hBs hB iK lam hK hPos u w hg v s x
  exact ⟨gatTiesC D sc 1 (fun u => L0 u) I ψ hd, _,
    groupCompatibilityOfRecursion D sc 1 (fun u => L0 u) I ψ hd H0 (fun _ => rfl) T' B hBs hB (CH.toHyp0 hpos).toHyp
      (fun u w h => isHyperbolicAt_rec_of_placeIdentification D u w h (I u w h)),
    LayerTwoModel.isRecG_retie ⟨D, fun u => L0 u, I, ψ, hd, H0, fun _ => rfl, T', B, hBs, hB, iK, lam, hK, hPos, CH, rfl⟩ _,
    fun _ => rfl, fun _ => rfl,
    fun u w hg v s x => GlobalTheory.T.mem_localPlace_asIdeal_iff_val_pos D sc 1 (fun u => L0 u) I ψ hd H0 (fun _ => rfl) B hBs hB iK lam hK hPos u w hg v s x⟩

end GlobalTheory.Mem.Top.Bl.TrueLocal

theorem colLay_colT : ∀ (B : Base) (f : Fr B.toPre) (T : TwDat B) (w : ColW B f T) (Z : w.Lt) (t : ColT w Z),
    ColLay (w.D Z) t.full.1.base.lev0 (Formalism.L2.Herm.toErrXS t.full.1) := by
  intro B f T w Z t
  have e : errOf t.C.herm = Formalism.L2.Herm.toErrXS t.full.1 :=
    GlobalTheory.Mem.Col2.TC.ExtraTie.errX_eq t.full.1 t.C.herm t.full.2 (errOf t.C.herm) (errOf_piG _) (errOf_piT _) (errOf_vPl _)
  have hl : t.full.1.base.lev0 = t.C.herm.base.lev0 :=
    congrArg (fun H : Formalism.L2.Herm.ColsHermS (w.D Z) => H.base.lev0) t.full.2
  rw [hl, ← e]
  exact colLay_of_tie w.I w.l Z t.C

theorem companion_of_colsHermS {D : D7} (H : Formalism.L2.Herm.ColsHermS D) : Companion D H.base.lev0 := fun u =>
  have hi : IsIntegral ℤ (D.host.conj ((H.atT u).piU : D.host.E)) :=
    map_isIntegral_int D.host.conj (RingOfIntegers.isIntegral_coe _)
  ⟨Ideal.span {(⟨_, hi⟩ : 𝓞 D.host.E)}, (H.baseL u ⟨_, hi⟩ rfl).1⟩

theorem exists_gTiedS_heq {D : D7} (H : Formalism.L2.Herm.HermColsCh D) (H0 : Formalism.L2.Herm.Herm0 D) (h : H0.lev0 = H.lev0) :
    ∃ g : Formalism.L2.Herm.SymmetricGroupCompatibility D H, HEq g H0.g := by
  obtain ⟨l, g⟩ := H0
  dsimp only at h
  subst h
  exact ⟨g, HEq.rfl⟩

theorem herm0_eq_to0 {D : D7} (X : Formalism.L2.Herm.FullTiedS D) (H0 : Formalism.L2.Herm.Herm0 D) (h : X.base.lev0 = H0.lev0)
    (hg : HEq X.g H0.g) : H0 = X.to0 := by
  obtain ⟨l, g⟩ := H0
  dsimp only at h hg
  subst h
  obtain rfl := eq_of_heq hg
  rfl

theorem columnLocalData_of_levelZero_concreteData (w : ColW B f T) (Z : w.Lt) (C : ColsTiedSym (w.D Z)) (hC : TC.StdC w.I w.l Z C)
    (hL : ∀ u, TC.StdLvC w.I w.l Z u (C.forms u)) (hV : Formalism.L2.Herm.VolumeCompatible (w.D Z)) (hO : Formalism.L2.GlobalTheoryTheory.OmegaCompatible (w.D Z) w.I.J.δ)
    (Gt : GatTies (w.D Z)) (c : LatCmp (w.D Z)) (G : GroupCompatibilityAll (w.D Z) Gt c) (hR : IsRecG K.s (w.D Z) Gt c G.herm G.forms)
    (hl : G.herm.lev0 = C.herm.base.lev0) (hf : G.forms = fun u => (C.forms u).to0)
    (hpl : ∀ u v hg (a : Fin ((w.D Z).gat u v hg).P.nV) (s : Fin (((w.D Z).gat u v hg).P.n' a)) (x : 𝓞 (w.D Z).host.F),
      x ∈ ((G.herm.g u v hg).pl a).1.asIdeal ↔ (0 : WithTop ℤ) < (w.D Z).what₂.places.val v
        (tauLL (w.D Z) (Gt.gat_pos u v hg ⟨a, s⟩) (algebraMap (w.D Z).host.F (w.D Z).host.E (x : (w.D Z).host.F)))) :
    Nonempty (ColumnDataSplitPrimes K w Z) := by
  obtain ⟨g, hg⟩ := exists_gTiedS_heq C.herm.base G.herm hl
  obtain ⟨Fu, h1, h2⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.exists_fullHermitianColumns_trueColumns w.I w.ok w.pins w.l Z C g hC hV
  have e : Fu.1.base.lev0 = G.herm.lev0 :=
    (congrArg (fun H : Formalism.L2.Herm.ColsHermS (w.D Z) => H.base.lev0) Fu.2).trans hl.symm
  exact ⟨⟨⟨C, hC, hL, Fu, h2, Gt, c, G, herm0_eq_to0 Fu.1 G.herm e (h1.trans hg), hf,
    gPlTiedS_of G.herm hpl _ _ e (h1.trans hg)⟩, hR, hO⟩⟩

    (hO : Formalism.L2.GlobalTheoryTheory.OmegaCompatible (w.D { Z with G := { Z.G with gat := g, gat_d := hd } }) w.I.J.δ) :
    Nonempty (ColumnDataSplitPrimes K w { Z with G := { Z.G with gat := g, gat_d := hd } }) := by

  exact columnLocalData_of_levelZero_concreteData w _ (colsRe _ C) (stdC_colsRe w.I w.l _ C hC) (fun u => stdLvC_colsRe w.I w.l _ C u (hL u)) hV hO Gt c G hR
    (funext hl) (funext fun u => (hf u).trans ((forms0Re_setGat g hd _).symm.trans (columnFormsAlongLaterStages_stageZero_eq _ C u).symm)) hpl


[GCF_stage_endpoint_01] Every original ColW input, including Laws, Pins, FieldOK and exact field
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Definitions/AutomorphicMembership/PositionOfIntro.lean#L23-L40
Range SHA256: a637d32bd72adc39a0dbf061d9dbb5761535fde8f9984ad9535bf4dd32bd21ae
structure ColW (B : Base) (f : Fr B.toPre) (T : TwDat B) : Type 1 where
  px : PreX B.toPre
  I : ColIn B px
  ok : I.Ok
  C_eq : I.C = f
  S_eq : I.S = {p | p ∈ offSet B.toPre ∧ p ≠ B.host.pde ∧ p ≠ B.host.pde'}
  xi_range : Set.range I.xi = T.set f
  pins : TC.Pins B px
  l : TC.Laws I
  l0 : TC.Law0 I l
  δ : Fld.FieldDat B.toPre
  exact : B.KsExact δ f
  fld : GlobalTheory.Mem.Per.FieldOK B.host B.embLL B.embE
  val : ∀ C W V S N G, ValLL0 (assemble B C W V S N G) (Fr.at ⟨B, C, W, V, S, N, G⟩ f)

namespace ColW

variable {B : Base} {f : Fr B.toPre} {T : TwDat B} (w : ColW B f T)


[GCF_stage_endpoint_02] Original StepW, all ColT fields and earlier G-stage choice
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Definitions/AutomorphicMembership/PositionOfIntro.lean#L70-L87
Range SHA256: 7a5b8326d89fbd47366024db39b3e6e2f28a66f9129307adf233103be94ad327
def StepW (p q : WP) : Prop :=
  Step KPx (fun _ _ _ _ => True) fun K B f T => ∀ w : ColW B f T, p K B f T w → q K B f T w

structure ColT {B : Base} {f : Fr B.toPre} {T : TwDat B} (w : ColW B f T) (Z : w.Lt) : Type 1 where
  C : Formalism.L2.Forms.ColsTiedSym (w.D Z)
  std : TC.StdC w.I w.l Z C
  stdLv : ∀ u, TC.StdLvC w.I w.l Z u (C.forms u)
  full : Formalism.L2.Forms.ColsFullS C
  stdDt : TC.StdDt w.I w.l Z full.1.base.dt
  Gt : GatTies (w.D Z)
  cmp : LatCmp (w.D Z)
  g : Formalism.L2.G.GroupCompatibilityAll (w.D Z) Gt cmp
  herm_eq : g.herm = full.1.to0
  forms_eq : g.forms = fun u => (C.forms u).to0
  gpl : Formalism.L2.Herm.SymmetricGroupCompatibleAtPlaces (w.D Z) full.1.base.toHermColsCh full.1.g Gt

def GIs (K : Ctx) {B : Base} {f : Fr B.toPre} {T : TwDat B} (w : ColW B f T) (G : GJStage B w.C) : Prop :=
  ∀ W V S N, Formalism.L2.Theta.VolTieG (w.D ⟨W, V, S, N, G⟩) ∧ GatNum K.s (w.D ⟨W, V, S, N, G⟩) ∧ Nonempty (ColT w ⟨W, V, S, N, G⟩)

theorem StepW.of_all {p q : WP} (h : ∀ K B f T w, K.Over B.toPre → Forced K B T → p K B f T w → q K B f T w) : StepW p q :=
  Step.of_all fun K B f T a _ b _ w => h K B f T w a b

def GatNum (s : Scale) (D : D7) : Prop :=
  ∀ u w hg, (D.gat u w hg).P.r = (if (D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℝ) < s.p0 D.R.k then G.rG s else 1) ∧
    (D.gat u w hg).e = ⌈(1 : ℝ) * (D.R.f : ℝ) * (D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * Real.logb (D.what₂.places.resChar w) s.a⌉₊

def GJStage.withTrue {B : Base} {C : Cols B} (G : GJStage B C) : GJStage B C :=
  letI := B.host.cmStar
  { G with
    vol := fun W => Vol.trueVol B.sp.cm (fun g : Tau B.R => B.host.emb g.1) G.cd W
    vol_pos := fun W => Vol.trueVol_pos B.sp.cm (fun g : Tau B.R => B.host.emb g.1) G.cd W
    gx := Per.trueGx B.host B.embLL B.embE (Equiv.refl _) }

theorem later (l : I.Laws) (hB : StagesInhab.HasLetters B.toPre) : Nonempty (TC.Later B (I.cols l)) := by
  obtain ⟨Del, letter, hD, hinj, gap, hu⟩ := hB
  let W := RowsRestModel.model (fits l) 1 1 1
  obtain ⟨L⟩ := locStage_inhabited
  obtain ⟨N, -⟩ := GaloisStage.exists_nStage_adapt B _ W L
  obtain ⟨V, -⟩ := DivInhab.exists_divStage B _ (GaloisStage.adapt W)
  obtain ⟨S⟩ := SStageInhab.nonempty_sStage B _ (GaloisStage.adapt W) Del hD gap letter hinj hu
    (fun u x _ y _ => ipC_herm l u x y) (fun u x hx h => ipC_pos l u x hx h) (show 1 ≤ 2 * 1 by omega)
  obtain ⟨G⟩ := GJInhab.nonempty_stage B (I.cols l)
  exact ⟨⟨GaloisStage.adapt W, V, S, N, G⟩⟩


[GCF_stage_endpoint_08] HasLetters from the original Over guard
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Definitions/AutomorphicMembership/ExistsStagesClauses.lean#L474-L486
Range SHA256: 299895176ed925189c6ce02dd0e77d685c412c0265a071f6b89846d8f15f4344
theorem hasLetters_of_over (K : Ctx) (P : Pre) (hO : K.Over P) : StagesInhab.HasLetters P := by
  obtain ⟨sp, hT, hY, hA, hV, hz, rfl, -⟩ := hO
  have hx : 0 < K.s.x := by linarith [K.s.one_lt_alt]
  refine ⟨(Delv K.s K.cD : ℚ), K.c, ?_, K.ok.inj, fun u τ l h => ?_, K.ok.code⟩
  · exact_mod_cast Nat.ceil_pos.2 (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hx _)
  · have a := (K.ok.hc u).1 τ ⟨l, by exact lt_of_le_of_lt (Nat.le_succ _) h⟩
    have b := (K.ok.hc u).1 τ ⟨l + 1, h⟩
    have h' : l + 1 < K.P.R.k - 1 := h
    have e : ((K.P.R.k - 2 - l : ℕ) : ℚ) = ((K.P.R.k - 2 - (l + 1) : ℕ) : ℚ) + 1 := by
      exact_mod_cast (by omega : K.P.R.k - 2 - l = K.P.R.k - 2 - (l + 1) + 1)
    refine (congrArg₂ (· - ·) a b).trans ?_
    simp only [e]
    ring


[GCF_stage_endpoint_09] Full exists_trueVolume_stage_of_any_column_choice and Pinned specialization
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Proofs/IwasawaDecomposition/ExistsGIs.lean#L146-L165
Range SHA256: 6b652a83dfc5b4f08563c80d54632118272a1b4c3af586e7bd23573875ee0257
theorem exists_trueVolume_stage_of_any_column_choice (p : WP) : StepW p fun K B f T w => ∃ G : GJStage B w.C, GIs2 K w G := by
  refine StepW.of_all fun K B f T w hO _ _ => ?_
  obtain ⟨Z₀⟩ := TrueColsTest.later w.l (JetEx.hasLetters_of_over hO)
  obtain ⟨C, hC, hL⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.exists_standardColumnComparisons_trueColumns w.I w.ok w.pins w.l Z₀
  obtain ⟨g, hd, h⟩ := exists_isLocalFamily K.s (D := w.D Z₀) (fun u => (C.forms u).to0) C.herm.std (companion_of_colsHermS C.herm)
    C.joint0 fun u => GlobalTheory.T.exists_psi0_eq_embLL w.I w.ok w.pins w.l Z₀ (C.forms u).gl (hC u).gl
  refine ⟨{ Z₀.G.withTrue with gat := g, gat_d := hd }, (LayerTwoModel.gIs2_iff w _).2 fun W V S N => ?_⟩
  have hV : Formalism.L2.Theta.VolTieG (w.D ⟨W, V, S, N, { Z₀.G.withTrue with gat := g, gat_d := hd }⟩) :=
    GlobalTheory.T.volumesAgreeAdelic_of_trueVolume _ fun _ => rfl
  refine ⟨hV, nonempty_columnLocalData_of_standardConcreteFamily w ⟨W, V, S, N, Z₀.G.withTrue⟩ (colsRe _ C) (stdC_colsRe w.I w.l _ C hC)
    (fun u => stdLvC_colsRe w.I w.l _ C u (hL u)) g hd ?_ hV.hermVolTied
    (omTied_withTrue w.fld w.px.ccK_top w.ok.amb_ok.delta _ rfl)⟩
  have h' := isLocalFamily_stages K.s Z₀ ⟨W, V, S, N, Z₀.G.withTrue⟩ _ _ g h
  exact (congrArg (fun F => IsLocalFamily K.s (recOf ⟨W, V, S, N, Z₀.G.withTrue⟩) C.herm.base.lev0 F g)
    (funext (columnFormsAlongLaterStages_stageZero_eq ⟨W, V, S, N, Z₀.G.withTrue⟩ C))).mpr h'

theorem exists_trueVolumeStage_columnChoiceStep : StepW Pinned fun K B f T w => ∃ G : GJStage B w.C, GIs2 K w G := exists_trueVolume_stage_of_any_column_choice Pinned

theorem latCmpC_setDiv {T : ∀ u : D.what₂.Word, Forms0 D u} {Gt : GatTies D} {I : Cmp.CmpIn D X} (H : Cmp.CmpHyp T Gt I) :
    cmpTr N h (Cmp.latCmpC T Gt I H) =
      Cmp.latCmpC (D := setDiv D N h) (fun u => DivTr.forms0S N h (T u)) (gatTr N h Gt) (cmpInS N h I) (cmpHyp_setDiv N h H) := rfl

theorem sigma_setDiv {T : ∀ u : D.what₂.Word, Forms0 D u} {Gt : GatTies D} {I : Cmp.CmpIn D X} (H : Cmp.CmpHyp T Gt I)
    (H0 : Formalism.L2.Herm.Herm0 D) :
    (⟨setDiv D N h, gatTr N h Gt, cmpTr N h (Cmp.latCmpC T Gt I H), DivTr.herm0S N h H0, fun u => DivTr.forms0S N h (T u)⟩ :
      Σ D : D7, GatTies D × LatCmp D × Formalism.L2.Herm.Herm0 D × ∀ u, Forms0 D u) =
    ⟨setDiv D N h, gatTr N h Gt,
      Cmp.latCmpC (D := setDiv D N h) (fun u => DivTr.forms0S N h (T u)) (gatTr N h Gt) (cmpInS N h I) (cmpHyp_setDiv N h H),
      DivTr.herm0S N h H0, fun u => DivTr.forms0S N h (T u)⟩ := by
  rw [latCmpC_setDiv]

end Gen

attribute [local instance_reducible] tauRec hP frobSet lamRec packC locPackC recG gatTiesG extRec embRec cmpInC hermitianLevelZeroOfRecursion levTieG gatRecP_lev gatRec_lev gatRec_pos
  DivTr.herm0S DivTr.forms0S DivTr.atTTS cmpInS LocPack.posOK

theorem isRecG_setDiv : ∀ (sc : Scale) (D : D7) (Gt : GatTies D) (c : LatCmp D) (H : Formalism.L2.Herm.Herm0 D)
    (F : ∀ u, Formalism.L2.Forms.Forms0 D u) (N : ∀ u, Submodule (𝓞 D.what₂.LL) (D.div.Nu u).V) (h : D.what₂.Word → ℝ),
    IsRecG sc D Gt c H F → IsRecG sc (setDiv D N h) (gatTr N h Gt) (cmpTr N h c) (DivTr.herm0S N h H)
      (fun u => DivTr.forms0S N h (F u)) := by
  intro sc D Gt c H F N h hR
  obtain ⟨D0, Lc, I, ψ, hd, H0, hL, T, B, hBs, hB, iK, lam, hK, hPos, CH, eq⟩ := hR
  cases eq
  refine IsRecG.intro (setDiv D0 N h) Lc I ?a1 ?a2 ?a3 ?a4 ?a5 B ?a6 ?a7 ?a8 ?a9 ?a10 ?a11 ?a12 ?a13
  case a1 => with_reducible_and_instances exact ψ
  case a2 => with_reducible_and_instances exact hd
  case a3 => with_reducible_and_instances exact DivTr.herm0S N h H0
  case a4 => with_reducible_and_instances exact hL
  case a5 => with_reducible_and_instances exact fun u => DivTr.forms0S N h (T u)
  case a6 => with_reducible_and_instances exact stdB_setDiv N h hBs
  case a7 => with_reducible_and_instances exact hB
  case a8 => with_reducible_and_instances exact iK
  case a9 => with_reducible_and_instances exact lam
  case a10 => with_reducible_and_instances exact hK
  case a11 => with_reducible_and_instances exact hPos
  case a12 => with_reducible_and_instances exact cmpHyp0P_setDiv N h CH
  case a13 =>
    with_reducible_and_instances refine Eq.trans (sigma_setDiv N h _ H0) ?_
    with_reducible_and_instances exact Eq.refl _

def atTTS {u : D.what₂.Word} (A : AtTTied (D := D) u) : AtTTied (D := setDiv D N h) u := { A with }

def forms0S {u : D.what₂.Word} (F : Forms0 D u) : Forms0 (setDiv D N h) u :=
  { F with
    atT := atTTS N h F.atT
    cls0 := { F.cls0 with }
    val0 := { F.val0 with }
    lv0 := { F.lv0 with } }

  { X with
    base := { X.base with }
    atT := fun u => { X.atT u with }
    ad := { X.ad with } }

def gS {Gt : GatTies D} {c : LatCmp D} (g : Formalism.L2.G.GroupCompatibilityAll D Gt c) :
    Formalism.L2.G.GroupCompatibilityAll (setDiv D N h) (gatTr N h Gt) (cmpTr N h c) where
  herm := herm0S N h g.herm
  forms := fun u => forms0S N h (g.forms u)
  cmp := { g.cmp with }
  ring := g.ring
  gat := g.gat

end Gen

variable {B : Base} {f : Fr B.toPre} {T : TwDat B}

theorem fullS_cols (X : Formalism.L2.Herm.FullTiedS (w.D Z)) :
    (fullS (D := w.D Z) N h X).cols = hermRe (setDivZ Z N h) X.cols := rfl

theorem colsRe_to0S (C : ColsTiedSym (w.D Z)) (u : B.Word) :
    ((colsRe (setDivZ Z N h) C).forms u).to0 = forms0S (D := w.D Z) N h (C.forms u).to0 := rfl

set_option maxHeartbeats 8000000 in

theorem stdC_tr (C : ColsTiedSym (w.D Z)) (hC : TC.StdC w.I w.l Z C) :
    TC.StdC w.I w.l (setDivZ Z N h) (colsRe (setDivZ Z N h) C) := fun u =>
  { hC u with gl := { (hC u).gl with } }

set_option maxHeartbeats 8000000 in

def colTTr (t : ColT w Z) : ColT w (setDivZ Z N h) where
  C := colsRe (setDivZ Z N h) t.C
  std := stdC_tr w Z N h t.C t.std
  stdLv := fun u => t.stdLv u
  full := ⟨fullS (D := w.D Z) N h t.full.1, (fullS_cols w Z N h t.full.1).trans (congrArg (hermRe (setDivZ Z N h)) t.full.2)⟩
  stdDt := t.stdDt
  Gt := gatTr (D := w.D Z) N h t.Gt
  cmp := cmpTr (D := w.D Z) N h t.cmp
  g := gS (D := w.D Z) N h t.g
  herm_eq := (congrArg (herm0S (D := w.D Z) N h) t.herm_eq).trans (fullS_to0 (D := w.D Z) N h t.full.1).symm
  forms_eq := (congrArg (fun F u => forms0S (D := w.D Z) N h (F u)) t.forms_eq).trans
    (funext fun u => (colsRe_to0S w Z N h t.C u).symm)
  gpl := t.gpl

end GlobalTheory.Mem.Db.DivTr

[GCF_given_extra_realization_01] All Wit inputs, single dependent (Gt,cmp,tw,ker) equality, WPt and joint (D,O) realization
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Definitions/AutomorphicMembership/DsigEq.lean#L24-L66

structure Wit (𝒮 : Slots) (K : Ctx) (w : ColW B f T) (Z : w.Lt) (O : Obj2 (w.D Z)) : Type 1 where
  isOver : K.Over B.toPre
  forced : Forced K B T
  Wt : Set B.places.Pl
  pin : ColPin w
  coh : Coh B T
  vol : Formalism.L2.Theta.VolTieG (w.D Z)
  col : ColumnDataSplitPrimes K w Z
  what₂ : RowsT K w Z Wt O.p0
  rx : 𝒮.Rx K B f T w Z Wt O.p0 what₂
  lev : O.lev = col.full.1.base.lev0
  errX : O.errX = Formalism.L2.Herm.toErrXS col.full.1
  n : NchT w Z Wt O.nch
  nx : 𝒮.Nx K B f T w Z Wt O.p0 what₂ O.nch n O.xd
  sx : 𝒮.Sx K B f T w Z Wt O.p0 what₂ col.toColT
  ker0 : KerFam (w.D Z) col.Gt col.cmp (w.tw Z) O.Gy
  ker_eq : (⟨O.gatT, O.cmp, O.tw, O.ker⟩ : Σ (Gt : GatTies (w.D Z)) (cmp : LatCmp (w.D Z)) (tw : TwForms (w.D Z)),
    KerFam (w.D Z) Gt cmp tw O.Gy) = ⟨col.Gt, col.cmp, w.tw Z, ker0⟩
  ker : KerT col.toColT O.X O.Gy ker0
  div : DivT n O.X
  th : ThT n O.th
  obj : ObjT O what₂
  ox : 𝒮.Ox K B f T w Z Wt O what₂
  real : Formalism.L2.Theta.RealTied (w.D Z)
  dsig_eq : ∀ τ, Z.S.dsig τ = Vol.trueDsig B.R (B.hc τ)
  gauge : O.gauge.IsGauge
  good : GoodO (w.D Z) O.nch O.xd

def Built.wit {𝒮 : Slots} {K : Ctx} {w : ColW B f T} {Z : w.Lt} {c : V4.Core K (w.st Z) f T} (b : Built 𝒮 K w Z c)
    (h : GoodO (w.D Z) c.O.nch c.O.xd) : Wit 𝒮 K w Z c.O :=
  { b with isOver := c.isOver, forced := c.forced, Wt := c.Wt, vol := (b.g Z.W Z.V Z.S Z.N).1, real := c.real, dsig_eq := c.dsig_eq,
           gauge := c.gauge_def, good := h }

structure WPt (𝒮 : Slots) (K : Ctx) : Type 1 where
  B : Base
  f : Fr B.toPre
  T : TwDat B
  w : ColW B f T
  Z : w.Lt
  O : Obj (w.D Z)
  x : Wit 𝒮 K w Z (ofObj O)

def WPt.Is {𝒮 : Slots} {K : Ctx} (p : WPt 𝒮 K) (D : D7) (O : Obj D) : Prop := (⟨D, O⟩ : Σ D : D7, Obj D) = ⟨p.w.D p.Z, p.O⟩

def TSelF {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Type _ :=
  Σ' x0 : ℝ, ∀ x (hx : Fm.x1 < x), x0 ≤ x → ∀ i : Fm.Ix x, RecT (Fm.ex x hx i)

def TSel (𝒞 : FamClass) : Type _ := ∀ {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps), 𝒞 Fm → TSelF Fm

theorem TSelF.tiedRec {eps : ℝ} {Fm : FamP eps} (s : TSelF Fm) :
    ∃ x0 : ℝ, ∀ x (hx : Fm.x1 < x), x0 ≤ x → ∀ i : Fm.Ix x, TiedRec (Fm.ex x hx i) :=
  ⟨s.1, fun x hx h i => ⟨s.2 x hx h i⟩⟩

abbrev WithChN (𝒞 : FamClass) : FamClass :=
  fun Fm => 𝒞 Fm ∧ DGamDvdF Fm ∧ TameDistinctF Fm ∧ BoxesF Fm ∧ QDRowsF Fm ∧ Fm.CYL ≤ 9

theorem withSm_of_withChN {𝒞 : FamClass} {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (h : WithChN 𝒞 Fm) : WithSm (WithChN 𝒞) Fm :=
  ⟨h, J.dGamSmooth_of_dvd Fm h.2.1⟩

theorem designChoices_upN {𝒞 : FamClass} {C0 : ℝ} {cT : ℝ → ℝ → ℝ} (h : DesignChoices (WithTw 𝒞) C0 cT) :
    DesignChoices (WithTw (WithSm (WithChN 𝒞))) C0 cT := by
  obtain ⟨ε0, h0, h⟩ := h
  refine ⟨ε0, h0, fun eps he hle K => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨Fm, CH, C5, Cb, hc, hCH, hL, h5, hb, a1, a2, a3, a4, a5, a6, a7, a8, a9, a10, a11, a12, a13⟩ := h eps he hle K
  exact ⟨Fm, CH, C5, Cb, ⟨withSm_of_withChN Fm ⟨hc.1, a8, a10, a6, a7, a13⟩, hc.2⟩, hCH, hL, h5, hb, a1, a2, a3,
    a4, a5, a6, a7, a8, a9, a10, a11, a12, a13⟩

abbrev D3 (C0 : ℝ) (𝒜 : FamClass) : FamClass := Des (WithChN 𝒜) C0

theorem d3_a {C0 : ℝ} {𝒜 : FamClass} {eps : ℝ} {Fm : FamP eps} (h : D3 C0 𝒜 Fm) : 𝒜 Fm := h.1.1.1.1

theorem closed_eps_le_one (𝒞 : FamClass) : Closed 𝒞 fun {eps} _ _ => eps ≤ 1 := by
  have K : TransportedAlt := by constructor <;> exact 0
  exact ⟨1, one_pos, fun _ _ hle => ⟨K, fun _ _ _ _ => hle⟩⟩

theorem closed_of_mem {𝒞 : FamClass} {P : ∀ {eps : ℝ}, FamP eps → ℝ → Prop} (h : Closed 𝒞 fun Fm CH => 𝒞 Fm → P Fm CH) :
    Closed 𝒞 P := by
  obtain ⟨a, ha, h⟩ := h
  exact ⟨a, ha, fun e he hle => let ⟨K, hK⟩ := h e he hle; ⟨K, fun Fm CH hc hL => hK Fm CH hc hL hc⟩⟩

theorem tied_families_of_choice {eps : ℝ} {Fm : FamP eps} (s : Formalism.L2.TSelF Fm) : Formalism.L2.LinkedFamiliesLevelClass Fm :=
  ⟨s.1, fun x hx h i => ⟨(s.2 x hx h i).T.toTiedDataS⟩⟩

abbrev Des (𝒞 : FamClass) (C0 : ℝ) : FamClass :=
  fun Fm => ((𝒞 Fm ∧ DGamSmooth Fm) ∧ TwGood Fm) ∧ (∃ CH : ℝ, ChosenE Fm C0 CH) ∧ TuSize Fm ∧ DGamSize Fm

abbrev WithSm (𝒞 : FamClass) : FamClass := fun Fm => 𝒞 Fm ∧ DGamSmooth Fm

end Formalism.MainNotions


[GCF_selected_tops_and_gain_02] Exact ChosenTT, ChosenBad and ChosenE selected-subset fields
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GlobalSchurBounds/Chosen.lean#L18-L90
Range SHA256: 5615599d2642f48516efda11bdac1badf3b3d0e641d0cbee99d1b455f2828c0c
def ChTT_at : Prop :=
  L.sc.delta1 * D.R.n / 2 ≤ (D.host.TT.card : ℝ) ∧ (D.host.TT.card : ℝ) ≤ 2 * L.sc.delta1 * D.R.n

open Classical in

def largeOrderResiduePrimes : Finset ℕ :=
  (Finset.range ⌈L.sc.X'⌉₊).filter (fun p =>
    p.Prime ∧ G.IsResidueClassZero D p ∧ L.sc.x ^ (L.sc.eps / 4) ≤ (addOrderOf (G.hP D p) : ℝ))

variable {L}

open Classical in

def badTops (X : G.Inst D L) (p : ℕ) : Finset (G.HH D) :=
  D.host.TT.filter (fun t => ¬ G.Good X p (X.bs (G.stepP D p) t))

def ChBad_at (X : G.Inst D L) (Cb : ℝ) : Prop :=
  ∑ p ∈ largeOrderResiduePrimes L, Real.log p / p * ((badTops X p).card : ℝ) ≤
    4 * (Cb * L.sc.x ^ (-2 : ℝ)) * L.sc.delta1 * D.R.n * ∑ p ∈ largeOrderResiduePrimes L, Real.log p / p

def BoundsAtX (x eps C : ℝ) (f : ℕ → ℂ) (M : ℕ) : Prop :=
  ∀ (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (χ : DirichletCharacter ℂ N), GlobalTheoryTheory.Induces χ f M →
    ‖GlobalTheoryTheory.gChi χ x‖ ≤ x ^ ((1 : ℝ) / 2 + eps) ∧ ‖GlobalTheoryTheory.logDerivativeAtOne χ‖ ≤ C * Real.log x

def ChQD_at (D : D7) (x eps C : ℝ) : Prop :=
  BoundsAtX x eps C (GlobalTheoryTheory.psDir D.host.q) (8 * D.host.q * D.host.d) ∧
  BoundsAtX x eps C (GlobalTheoryTheory.chDir D.host.d) (8 * D.host.q * D.host.d) ∧
  BoundsAtX x eps C (fun a => GlobalTheoryTheory.chDir D.host.d a * GlobalTheoryTheory.psDir D.host.q a) (8 * D.host.q * D.host.d)

def ChThin_at (D : D7) (x eps c : ℝ) : Prop :=
  ∀ (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (χ : DirichletCharacter ℂ N), GlobalTheoryTheory.IsCharED D χ →
    ∀ y : ℝ, Real.exp (x ^ (eps / 3)) / 3 ≤ y → ‖GlobalTheoryTheory.gChi χ y‖ ≤ y * Real.exp (-(c * x ^ (eps / 8)))

variable (L)

def ChProviso_at (C0 : ℝ) : Prop :=
  ∀ u, C0⁻¹ ≤ (D.duS u : ℝ) * GamL L ^ 2 / D.what₂.Dstar ∧ (D.duS u : ℝ) * GamL L ^ 2 / D.what₂.Dstar ≤ C0

def ChCode_at (C0 CH : ℝ) : Prop :=
  C0 * GamL L ^ 2 * L.sc.x ^ CH ≤ (Fintype.card D.what₂.Word : ℝ)

end AtRecord

section Family

variable {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps)

def ThickSetEventually : Prop := ∃ x0 : ℝ, ∀ x ≥ x0, ∀ i : Fm.Ix x, ThickSetLowerBound (Fm.L x i)

def ChosenTT : Prop := ∃ x0 : ℝ, ∀ x ≥ x0, ∀ i : Fm.Ix x, ChTT_at (Fm.L x i)

def ChosenBad (Cb : ℝ) : Prop := ∃ x0 : ℝ, ∀ x ≥ x0, ∀ i : Fm.Ix x, ChBad_at (Fm.Gx x i) Cb

def ChosenQD (C5 : ℝ) : Prop := ∃ x0 : ℝ, ∀ x ≥ x0, ∀ i : Fm.Ix x, ChQD_at (Fm.D x i) x eps C5

def ChosenThin (c6 : ℝ) : Prop := ∃ x0 : ℝ, ∀ x ≥ x0, ∀ i : Fm.Ix x, ChThin_at (Fm.D x i) x eps c6

def ChosenProviso (C0 : ℝ) : Prop := ∃ x0 : ℝ, ∀ x ≥ x0, ∀ i : Fm.Ix x, ChProviso_at (Fm.L x i) C0

def ChosenCode (C0 CH : ℝ) : Prop := ∃ x0 : ℝ, ∀ x ≥ x0, ∀ i : Fm.Ix x, ChCode_at (Fm.L x i) C0 CH

def Chosen (CH C0 C5 c6 Cb : ℝ) : Prop :=
  ThickSetEventually Fm ∧ ChosenTT Fm ∧ ChosenBad Fm Cb ∧ ChosenQD Fm C5 ∧ ChosenThin Fm c6 ∧
    ChosenProviso Fm C0 ∧ ChosenCode Fm C0 CH

def ChosenE (C0 CH : ℝ) : Prop :=
  ∃ C5 c6 Cb : ℝ, 0 < C5 ∧ 0 < c6 ∧ 0 < Cb ∧ Chosen Fm CH C0 C5 c6 Cb

def ChosenEx (CH : ℝ) : Prop := ∃ C0 : ℝ, 1 ≤ C0 ∧ ChosenE Fm C0 CH

end Family

end Formalism.MainNotions

Range SHA256: 0d47ede280913c86990ab5ca79ec448ee4d3c2213d4eafd76960576f611de2ca
def TopsF {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Prop := ChosenTT Fm ∧ ∃ Cb : ℝ, 0 < Cb ∧ ChosenBad Fm Cb

theorem topsF_of_chosenE {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) {C0 CH : ℝ} (h : ChosenE Fm C0 CH) : TopsF Fm := by
  obtain ⟨C5, c6, Cb, -, -, hCb, -, hTT, hBad, -⟩ := h
  exact ⟨hTT, Cb, hCb, hBad⟩


[GCF_selected_tops_and_gain_04] Original same-subset Adm2F and exact harmonic threshold
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/SmoothDesignExistence/AdmissibleTopsMertens.lean#L73-L144
Range SHA256: f33eca91d6d26483b1b22ae2171876ba3e795db23e580e7a15bf917e7b0cde63
theorem admissible_tops_mertens {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (h : TopsF Fm) : G.Adm2F Fm := by
  obtain ⟨⟨xa, hTT⟩, Cb, hCb, xb, hBad⟩ := h
  unfold G.Adm2F G.Ev
  refine ⟨max (max xa xb) (max (Fm.x1 + 1) ((16 * Cb + 2) ^ (3 / eps))), fun x hx i => ?_⟩
  have hxa : xa ≤ x := le_trans (le_trans (le_max_left _ _) (le_max_left _ _)) hx
  have hxb : xb ≤ x := le_trans (le_trans (le_max_right _ _) (le_max_left _ _)) hx
  have hx1' : Fm.x1 + 1 ≤ x := le_trans (le_trans (le_max_left _ _) (le_max_right _ _)) hx
  have hx1 : Fm.x1 < x := by linarith
  have hxc : (16 * Cb + 2) ^ (3 / eps) ≤ x := le_trans (le_trans (le_max_right _ _) (le_max_right _ _)) hx
  obtain ⟨hT1, hT2⟩ := hTT x hxa i
  have hB := hBad x hxb i
  unfold ChBad_at at hB
  have hLx : (Fm.L x i).sc.x = x := Fm.L_x x i hx1
  have hLe : (Fm.L x i).sc.eps = eps := Fm.L_eps x i
  have he' : 0 < (Fm.L x i).sc.eps := (Fm.L x i).sc.eps_pos
  have he : 0 < eps := by rw [← hLe]; exact he'
  have hy1 : 1 < (Fm.L x i).sc.x := (Fm.L x i).sc.one_lt_alt
  have hy0 : 0 < (Fm.L x i).sc.x := by linarith
  have hT0 : 0 ≤ (Fm.L x i).sc.x ^ ((Fm.L x i).sc.eps / 3) := by positivity
  have hTbig : 16 * Cb + 2 ≤ (Fm.L x i).sc.x ^ ((Fm.L x i).sc.eps / 3) := by
    rw [hLx, hLe]
    have hc : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 16 * Cb + 2 := by linarith
    have hne : eps ≠ 0 := he.ne'
    have hm : 3 / eps * (eps / 3) = 1 := by
      first
      | (field_simp; ring)
      | field_simp
    calc 16 * Cb + 2 = ((16 * Cb + 2) ^ (3 / eps)) ^ (eps / 3) := by
          rw [← Real.rpow_mul hc, hm, Real.rpow_one]
      _ ≤ x ^ (eps / 3) := Real.rpow_le_rpow (by positivity) hxc (by positivity)
  have hS := sum_log_div_le_two_mul_mul_add_two (largeOrderResiduePrimes (Fm.L x i)) ((Fm.L x i).sc.x ^ ((Fm.L x i).sc.eps / 3)) hT0
    (fun p hp => by
      simp only [largeOrderResiduePrimes, Finset.mem_filter, Finset.mem_range] at hp
      exact ⟨hp.2.1.one_lt.le, hp.1⟩)
  have hBTT : AuxBlock8.BTT (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) (Fm.Gx x i) ≤
      ∑ p ∈ largeOrderResiduePrimes (Fm.L x i), Real.log p / p * ((badTops (Fm.Gx x i) p).card : ℝ) := by
    unfold AuxBlock8.BTT
    apply Finset.sum_le_sum_of_subset_of_nonneg
    · intro p hp
      simp only [AuxBlock8.primesResidueBelow, largeOrderResiduePrimes, Finset.mem_filter, Finset.mem_range] at hp ⊢
      exact ⟨hp.1.1, hp.1.2.1, hp.1.2.2.2, hp.2⟩
    · intro p _ _
      have hl : 0 ≤ Real.log (p : ℝ) := by
        rcases Nat.eq_zero_or_pos p with h0 | h0
        · simp [h0]
        · exact Real.log_nonneg (by exact_mod_cast h0)
      exact mul_nonneg (div_nonneg hl (Nat.cast_nonneg p)) (Nat.cast_nonneg _)
  have hdn : 0 ≤ (Fm.L x i).sc.delta1 * (Fm.D x i).R.n := by
    have hc0 : (0 : ℝ) ≤ ((Fm.D x i).host.TT.card : ℝ) := Nat.cast_nonneg _
    linarith
  have hA : 0 < (Fm.L x i).sc.x ^ (-2 : ℝ) := by positivity
  have hpow : (Fm.L x i).sc.x ^ (-2 + (Fm.L x i).sc.eps) =
      (Fm.L x i).sc.x ^ (-2 : ℝ) * ((Fm.L x i).sc.x ^ ((Fm.L x i).sc.eps / 3)) ^ 3 := by
    have h3 : (Fm.L x i).sc.eps = (Fm.L x i).sc.eps / 3 * ((3 : ℕ) : ℝ) := by push_cast; ring
    rw [Real.rpow_add hy0, ← Real.rpow_natCast, ← Real.rpow_mul hy0.le, ← h3]
  unfold AuxBlock8.adm2
  refine ⟨⟨by linarith, by linarith⟩, ?_⟩
  rw [hpow]
  obtain ⟨T, hTe⟩ : ∃ T, T = (Fm.L x i).sc.x ^ ((Fm.L x i).sc.eps / 3) := ⟨_, rfl⟩
  obtain ⟨A, hAe⟩ : ∃ A, A = (Fm.L x i).sc.x ^ (-2 : ℝ) := ⟨_, rfl⟩
  rw [← hTe] at hS hTbig hT0 ⊢
  rw [← hAe] at hA hB ⊢
  have k1 : 16 * Cb * (T + 2) ≤ T ^ 2 := by
    nlinarith [mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hTbig hT0]
  have k2 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left k1
    (div_nonneg (mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hdn hA.le) hT0) (by norm_num : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 2))
  have k3 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hS
    (mul_nonneg (by positivity : (0 : ℝ) ≤ 4 * (Cb * A)) hdn)
  have k4 := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hT1 (by positivity : (0 : ℝ) ≤ A * T ^ 3)
  linarith [hBTT, hB, k2, k3, k4]

end Formalism.MainNotions.Pk


[GCF_selected_tops_and_gain_05] Original gTiedF_of_sel, admissible_tops_of_class, linkG_closed and linkG_frame

  Formalism.L2.gTiedF_of_C2S Fm (tied_families_of_choice s)

theorem admissible_tops_of_class {𝒜 : FamClass} {C0 : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (hc : D3 C0 𝒜 Fm) : Formalism.MainNotions.G.Adm2F Fm :=

  Formalism.L2.G.linkG_concrete5 (D3 3 𝒜)
    (Closed.of_class fun Fm _ hc => gTiedF_of_sel Fm (sel Fm (d3_a hc)))
    (Closed.of_class fun Fm _ hc => admissible_tops_of_class Fm hc)

theorem linkG_frame (𝒜 : FamClass) (sel : Formalism.L2.TSel 𝒜) :
    Closed (D3 3 𝒜) fun Fm _ => ∀ _ : D3 3 𝒜 Fm, Formalism.MainNotions.TuSize Fm → Formalism.MainNotions.DGamSize Fm → LinkG Fm := by
  obtain ⟨ε1, hε, H⟩ := linkG_closed 𝒜 sel
  refine ⟨ε1, hε, fun eps he hle => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨K, hK⟩ := H eps he hle
  refine ⟨K, fun Fm CH hc => ?_⟩
  have h1 := hK Fm CH hc
  intro hl
  beta_reduce
  intro _ _ _
  exact h1 hl


[GCF_selected_tops_and_gain_06] Original Closed choice order and pointwise maximum of coefficient bounds
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/ParamsHeightRankConstant.lean#L112-L181

def cLast : List ℝ := [Fm.CkLow, Fm.Ck6, Fm.CfL, Fm.cLL⁻¹, Fm.Crho, Fm.Crho, Fm.CDelG, Fm.Cw, Fm.CDel,

end FamP

structure TransportedAlt where
  pre : ℝ
  t : List ℝ → ℝ
  mid : List ℝ → ℝ
  last : List ℝ → ℝ

def TransportedAlt.le (K K' : TransportedAlt) : Prop :=
  K.pre ≤ K'.pre ∧ (∀ l, K.t l ≤ K'.t l) ∧ (∀ l, K.mid l ≤ K'.mid l) ∧ ∀ l, K.last l ≤ K'.last l

def TransportedAlt.sup (K K' : TransportedAlt) : TransportedAlt :=
  ⟨max K.pre K'.pre, fun l => max (K.t l) (K'.t l), fun l => max (K.mid l) (K'.mid l), fun l => max (K.last l) (K'.last l)⟩

def FamP.Large {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (K : TransportedAlt) (CH : ℝ) : Prop :=
  (∀ c ∈ Fm.cPre, K.pre ≤ c) ∧ True ∧ (∀ c ∈ Fm.cMid CH, K.mid Fm.cOut ≤ c) ∧
    ∀ c ∈ Fm.cLast, K.last (Fm.cOut ++ Fm.cMid CH) ≤ c

theorem FamP.Large.mono {eps : ℝ} {Fm : FamP eps} {K K' : TransportedAlt} {CH : ℝ} (h : Fm.Large K' CH) (hK : K.le K') :
    Fm.Large K CH :=
  ⟨fun c hc => hK.1.trans (h.1 c hc), trivial, fun c hc => (hK.2.2.1 _).trans (h.2.2.1 c hc),
    fun c hc => (hK.2.2.2 _).trans (h.2.2.2 c hc)⟩

theorem FamP.large_sup {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (K K' : TransportedAlt) (CH : ℝ) :
    Fm.Large (K.sup K') CH ↔ Fm.Large K CH ∧ Fm.Large K' CH := by
  constructor
  · intro h
    exact ⟨h.mono ⟨le_max_left _ _, fun _ => le_max_left _ _, fun _ => le_max_left _ _, fun _ => le_max_left _ _⟩,
      h.mono ⟨le_max_right _ _, fun _ => le_max_right _ _, fun _ => le_max_right _ _, fun _ => le_max_right _ _⟩⟩
  · rintro ⟨h, h'⟩
    exact ⟨fun c hc => max_le (h.1 c hc) (h'.1 c hc), trivial,
      fun c hc => max_le (h.2.2.1 c hc) (h'.2.2.1 c hc), fun c hc => max_le (h.2.2.2 c hc) (h'.2.2.2 c hc)⟩

abbrev FamClass : Type 1 := ∀ {eps : ℝ}, FamP eps → Prop

def Closed (𝒞 : FamClass) (P : ∀ {eps : ℝ}, FamP eps → ℝ → Prop) : Prop :=
  ∃ ε1 : ℝ, 0 < ε1 ∧ ∀ eps : ℝ, 0 < eps → eps ≤ ε1 → ∃ K : TransportedAlt, ∀ (Fm : FamP eps) (CH : ℝ),
    𝒞 Fm → Fm.Large K CH → P Fm CH

theorem Closed.and {𝒞 : FamClass} {P Q : ∀ {eps : ℝ}, FamP eps → ℝ → Prop} (hP : Closed 𝒞 P) (hQ : Closed 𝒞 Q) :
    Closed 𝒞 (fun Fm CH => P Fm CH ∧ Q Fm CH) := by
  obtain ⟨a, ha, hP⟩ := hP
  obtain ⟨b, hb, hQ⟩ := hQ
  refine ⟨min a b, lt_min ha hb, fun e he hle => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨K, hK⟩ := hP e he (hle.trans (min_le_left _ _))
  obtain ⟨K', hK'⟩ := hQ e he (hle.trans (min_le_right _ _))
  exact ⟨K.sup K', fun Fm CH hc h =>
    ⟨hK Fm CH hc ((Fm.large_sup K K' CH).1 h).1, hK' Fm CH hc ((Fm.large_sup K K' CH).1 h).2⟩⟩

theorem Closed.imp {𝒞 : FamClass} {P Q : ∀ {eps : ℝ}, FamP eps → ℝ → Prop} (hP : Closed 𝒞 P)
    (h : ∀ {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) (CH : ℝ), 0 < eps → P Fm CH → Q Fm CH) : Closed 𝒞 Q := by
  obtain ⟨a, ha, hP⟩ := hP
  refine ⟨a, ha, fun e he hle => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨K, hK⟩ := hP e he hle
  exact ⟨K, fun Fm CH hc hL => h Fm CH he (hK Fm CH hc hL)⟩

def TuSize {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Prop :=
  ∃ x0 : ℝ, ∀ x ≥ x0, ∀ i : Fm.Ix x, (Fm.L x i).TuGe ∧


[GCF_selected_tops_and_gain_07] Full original linkG_concrete5 with its two class-level antecedents
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/LocalToGlobal/GroupLattices/LinkGConcreteNoKer.lean#L44-L56

      localSystemLowTerm := hh.1.2
      localSystemSizeLowTerm := hh.2 }

theorem linkG_concrete_noKer (𝒞 : FamClass) (h : Loc)
    (hT : Closed 𝒞 fun Fm _ => GroupCompatibilityFamily Fm) (h2 : Closed 𝒞 fun Fm _ => Adm2F Fm) :
    Closed 𝒞 fun Fm _ => LinkG Fm :=
  link_of_local_closed 𝒞 h (hT.imp fun Fm _ _ ht => gatEF_of_tied Fm ht) (hT.imp fun Fm _ _ ht => stdSubF_of_tied Fm ht)
    h2 (hT.imp fun Fm _ _ ht => npDescF_of_tied Fm ht)

theorem linkG_concrete5 (𝒞 : FamClass) (hT : Closed 𝒞 fun Fm _ => GroupCompatibilityFamily Fm) (h2 : Closed 𝒞 fun Fm _ => Adm2F Fm) :
    Closed 𝒞 fun Fm _ => LinkG Fm :=
  linkG_concrete_noKer 𝒞 loc_proved hT h2


[GCF_selected_tops_and_gain_08] All five LinkG fields, original error and same-family normalization
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/GlobalTheoremInputs.lean#L97-L140
Range SHA256: 92b096aa52bdef5669406c0e8ff34c2c77a66e617562a63c80c79821abb28825
def InertK (D : D7) (p : ℕ) : Prop :=
  D.host.cls p = PrimeClass.signal ∨ D.host.cls p = PrimeClass.inertClass

def flG {D : D7} (L : SizeData₂ D) (C : ℝ) (p : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  C * (D.R.f : ℝ) * (D.R.N D.R.m : ℝ) ^ 2 * Real.logb p L.sc.a +
    C * (D.R.f : ℝ) * (Real.logb p L.sc.a) ^ 2

def rG (eps : ℝ) : ℕ := ⌈8 * ((2 + 8 * eps) + 2) / eps⌉₊ + 1

def leastPowerExponent {D : D7} (L : SizeData₂ D) (p : ℕ) : ℕ :=
  ⌈Real.logb p ((D.R.k : ℝ) ^ 2 * L.sc.x ^ (3 * Scale.A))⌉₊ + 1

def loG {D : D7} (L : SizeData₂ D) (C bo : ℝ) (p : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  let s := L.sc
  let pr : ℝ := if D.host.cls p = PrimeClass.inertClass then
      (if (p : ℝ) < s.p0 D.R.k then C * (rG s.eps : ℝ) * s.b' * Real.log s.x
       else if (p : ℝ) < s.X' then
         C * (s.b' / s.x + L.etaSz + L.Sc + (s.w'' D.R.n + D.R.t) * Real.log s.x / s.delta1) + bo
       else 0) else 0
  let orr : ℝ := if D.host.cls p = PrimeClass.inertClass ∧ (p : ℝ) < s.X' then
      C * Real.log s.x * (L.LaG * s.a / ((p : ℝ) * D.R.k) +
        (if (p : ℝ) < s.p0 D.R.k then s.a * (p : ℝ) ^ (-(leastPowerExponent L p : ℝ)) else s.a / (p : ℝ) ^ 2))
      else 0
  C * (L.Sc + D.R.k + L.etaSz) * Real.logb p (2 * s.a) + pr + orr

def LinkG_at {D : D7} {L : SizeData₂ D} (X : G.Inst D L) (K : G.Consts) (C bo : ℝ) : Prop :=
  (∀ u w, D.what₂.places.resChar w ≠ (D.mem u).A.T →
    DV.LocLe (D.plEq w) ⌊DV.ur D w (L.tau w)⌋₊ (L.NuLat u) (DV.MramL D u) ∧
    (L.lenMN u w : ℝ) ≤ (D.duS u : ℝ) * (L.tau w + L.io w) ∧
    (G.VV X K (G.aL L) (G.bL L) (D.what₂.places.resChar w) = ∅ → L.tau w = 0 ∧ L.io w = 0 ∧ L.lenMN u w = 0)) ∧
  (∀ p, ∀ t0 ∈ G.VV X K (G.aL L) (G.bL L) p,
    G.Sv X K (G.aL L) (G.bL L) p t0 ≤ 2 * (D.R.m : ℝ) * (G.Nv X p t0 : ℝ) * L.sc.a / p) ∧
  (∀ w, InertK D (D.what₂.places.resChar w) → (D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℝ) ≤ L.sc.X'' D.R.n →
    (∀ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, 0 ≤ L.gain j w ∧
      L.gain j w ≤ 4 * (D.host.TT.card : ℝ) * L.sc.a / (D.what₂.places.resChar w : ℝ)) ∧
    (∑ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, ∑ t ∈ L.TTw w,
        (L.vonMangoldtClassSum j t (D.what₂.places.resChar w) - loG L C bo (D.what₂.places.resChar w))) -
      (D.R.m : ℝ) * flG L C (D.what₂.places.resChar w) ≤
      ((∑ j ∈ SizeData₂.lifts D, L.gain j w) - L.tau w)) ∧
  G.ServedOrbitsContained X K (G.aL L) (G.bL L) ∧ G.MeanKappaBound X K

def LinkG {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps) : Prop :=
  ∃ (C x0 : ℝ) (δ : ℝ → ℝ), Filter.Tendsto δ Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
    ∀ x ≥ x0, ∀ i : Fm.Ix x, LinkG_at (Fm.Gx x i) Fm.GK C ((Fm.L x i).sc.b ^ δ x)
